aintiram - aivakaiyāṟṟal. niṟainta eṉṟa viṉaiyāl aintiram nūṟpeyaraṉṟu eṉpatum, āṟṟal niṟaivu eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. nūlāyiṉ nāṉmaṟai muṟṟiya eṉpatu pōla, aintiram muṟṟiya eṉṟa viṉai vantirukka vēṇṭum. `vētam niṟainta tamiḻnāṭu, eṉṟa pārati pāṭṭil, `niṟainta' eṉpatu mikunta eṉṟa poruḷil varuvatai eṇṇuka. niṟaivu - kuṟaivilāttaṉmai. aintiramāvatu - aintiṇai ilakkaṇam kūṟum aṟivāṟṟal. eḻuttilakkaṇam collilakkaṇam ellām kūṟiyiruppiṉum porulilakkaṇamē vāḻvuc ciṟappuṭaiyatu; ātaliṉ, acciṟappāṟṟal tolkāppiyariṭattu mikuntirukkak kaṇṭaṉar avaiyattār. ataṉāl `aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉṟa ciṟappaṭaikku uriyavar āyiṉar tolkāppiyar. tamiḻp periyār u.vē.cā palavakai ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum tēṭikkaṇṭu patippittiruppiṉum caṅkanūṟ patippāṟ ciṟappup peṟuvataiyum, aṭikaḷ vipulāṉāntar yāḻnūlāṟ ciṟappu; peṟuvataiyum, paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār cukkira nītimoḻi peyarppāṟ ciṟappup peṟuvataiyum īṇṭu niṉaivu koḷvōm, aintiram niṟainta oru vakaip pukaḻ īṇṭuc collappeṟṟatu. tolkāppiyaṉār vāḻkkai varalāṟukaḷai eḻutuvatu paṉampāraṉār pāyirak karuttaṉṟu, pāyiram nūl paṟṟiyatēyākum; eṉiṉum `pal pukaḻ' niṟutta paṭimaiyōṉ' eṉṟa iṟutiyaṭi tolkāppiyar vēṟu vakaiyālum naṉmatippuṭaiyavar eṉpataṉait tokuttuk kāṭṭum.
paṭimaiyōṉ - piṟarukku eṭuttukkāṭṭāṉa vāḻkkaiyōṉ. attaku vāḻkkai taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkeṉa vāḻum oḻukkattāl varuvatu. paṭimai - oḻukkamuṭaimai.
naṭaimuṭipu: poruḷum nāṭi muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa eṇṇip pōkkaṟu paṉuvalākat tokuttāṉ yāvaṉ eṉiṉ. pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyakattu ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṉukku ārāyntu muṟaikāṭṭi aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉat taṉ peyaraic ciṟappākat tōṉṟac ceytu malkunīr varaippiṉ palpukaḻai nilainiṟuttiya cāṉṟōṉ āvāṉ.
tiṟaṉurait toṭarcci
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari - vaṭakkē vēṅkaṭam teṟkē kumari eṉpatu poruḷāyiṉum uṇmaikkuṟippu eṉṉa? vēṅkaṭattiṉ vaṭakku varaiyum (aḵtāvatu vaṭapeṇṇai varaiyum) kumari malaiyil teṟkuvaraiyum uḷḷaṭaṅkak koṇṭatu tamiḻ nilapparappu eṉpatām. `ivai akappāṭ ṭellai' eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar teḷivupaṭuttiyiruppatu poruntum. `taṭanīrkkumari vaṭaperuṅkōṭṭiṉkāṟum' eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār.
pōkkaṟu paṉuval - nūliṉ peyar. tukaḷaṟupōtam eṉpatu pōla. tolkāppiyaṉ eṉat taṉpeyarai nūlukku vaittāṉ eṉṟa karuttup poruntātu. `aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ' eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi eṉa muṉvaruvataiyum `pal pukaḻniṟutta' eṉap piṉvaruvataiyum cērttuppārkka vēṇṭum. kuṟṟamaṟṟa paṉuval. kuṟṟam aṟukkum paṉuval eṉa iruporuḷum iyaiyum.
pulantokuttōṉ - parantu paṭṭa ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷai iyalkaḷāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉāṉ. “itu tokuttu yākkap paṭṭatu; eṉṉai? ulakattu naṭakkum akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ ilakkaṇamellām ivvaṟupatu cūttirattuḷḷē tokuttāṉātaliṉ” eṉṟu tokuttal eṉpataṟku viḷakkañ ceyvar iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷ uraiyāciriyar. itaṉāl eṅkō kiṭanta pala nūṟpākkaḷait tiraṭṭiṉār eṉṟa karuttu vaṉmaiyāka maṟukkattakum.
aintiram niṟainta - aintiṇai ilakkaṇam collum āṟṟal niṟainta eṉpatu poruḷ. ipporuḷ kūṟal iṭarppāṭuṭaiyatu. etirppukku uriyatu eṉiṉum uḷḷavaṟṟuḷ ottatu kāṇal eṉṟa uttippaṭi ūkikka vēṇṭum. oruvaraip pārāṭṭuṅkāl ceytataic cuṭṭip pārāṭṭa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟic ceytatai viṭṭuviṭutal poruntātu ceytataic cuṭṭiyapiṉ, poruntiya piṟavaṟṟaiyum iṇaik koḷḷalām. tiruvaḷḷuvar kaṟṟa nūlkaḷ evvaḷavō irukkum eṉiṉum pārāṭṭu tirukkuṟaṭ paṭaippukkē āṉantaraṅkam piḷḷaikkup pārāṭṭu tamiḻnāṭ kuṟippukkē. aḻakappar palpukaḻ cāṉṟavar eṉiṉum pārāṭṭu muḻuk koṭaikkē; koṭaiveḷippāṭē ēṉaip pukaḻkaḷaiyum veḷicceytaṉa. eṉavē `aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ' eṉpatu avar eḻutiya nūl tanta pukaḻaṭai. avar nūlē avarukku vaḻivaḻi ulakap pukaḻ niṟuttum cāṉṟu.
kaṭal cūḻ ulakil aintiram eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi viyākaraṇattai naṉkaṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉat taṉ peyarai veḷippaṭuttiṉār eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭāl.. inta vēṟṟumai nūlaṟivu avarukku ulakappukaḻukkup pōtiya kāraṇam ākumā? avar nūluḷ itupaṟṟiya oru kuṟippāvatu uṇṭā? appaṭip pukaḻpeṟuvataṟku aintirattukku māpāṭiyam eḻutiṉārā? aintiram eṉṟa colloppumai koṇṭu vaṭamoḻiyōṭu oṭṭiya iṭaikkālac cāyal itu. ivvāṟu mayaṅkiya colloppumaiyāl ilakkiya ilakkaṇat tuṟaiyilum camayattuṟaiyilum  piṟatuṟaiyilum tamiḻukku viḷainta kaḷaikaḷ palappala; aintiram eṉṟa vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇa nūl tolkāppiyar kālattu iruntirukkumēl avar ataṉai naṉku kaṟṟup pārttirukkalām. atu tavaṟillai; ennūlaiyum kaṟpatu muṟaiyē; eṉiṉum intat toṭarukku atu poruntuvataṉṟu; pāyirattukkuḷ varavēṇṭiyatumaṉṟu.
ippāyirattil varum eṇṇikkai toṭarpāṉa ōramaippaic cuṭṭik kāṭṭavirumpuvaṉ. oṉṟu  iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku aintu paṉmai eṉṟa eṇṇikkai nāṭṭam paṉam pāraṉārkku iruntamai teḷivākiṉṟatu. oṉṟu tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakam; iraṇṭu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum; mūṉṟu eḻuttum collum poruḷum; nāṉku nāṉmaṟai: aintu aintiram; paṉmai palpukaḻ niṟutta, ivvōṭṭattaik kāṇuṅkāl, nāṉmaṟaikkup piṉ varum aintiram eṉpatu evvāṟēṉum aintu eṉṉum eṇ kuṟippiṉatāka iruttal vēṇṭum. orucārār urai kūṟuvatupōl eḻuttu, col, poruḷ yāppu aṇi eṉṟu aintiṉaik koḷḷalām eṉiṉum eḻuttum collum poruḷum eṉa muppākupāṭē aṉṟirunta vaḻakkātalāṉum, meyppāṭu viṭupaṭṭup pōtalāṉum avvurai piṟkālaccāyalatu.
tirukkuṟaḷ uraikaḷaiyellām kaṟṟa perukkam oruvaṉukku iruntālum parimēlaḻakar uraiyai naṉku kaṟṟavaṉ eṉṟu vīṟupaṭappārāṭṭuvatu uṇṭaṉṟō? atupōl eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṟa muppulavaṟivu iruntālum tolkāppiyar tam aintiṇaiyilakkaṇa vaṉmaiyaip poruḷatikārattu virivupaṭa veḷippaṭuttiyuḷḷār. ātaliṉ `aintiram niṟainta' eṉṟa peyar vīṟu peṟṟāhr eṉka. iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇai maruṅkiṉ kāmakkūṭṭam' eṉṟa tolkāppiyattoṭar oru cāṉṟākum. aintiṇai eṉavē oruvāṟu puṟamum aṭaṅkum, eḻuttum collumē pulantokuttiruntāl tolkāppiyar olkāpperumai eytiyiruppārā? eḻuttum collum illātu. poruḷoṉṟukkē pulantokuttiruntāl apperumai peṟṟirukkamāṭṭārā? uṟaḻntu pārkka.
aintiṟam eṉa valliṉappāṭam iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉpar cilar. attaku pāṭapētam illai; eṉiṉum tiram eṉṟa iṭaiyiṉap pāṭattukkē mikutal. āṟṟal eṉṟa poruḷ koḷḷalām.
14-4-1986il ulakat tamiḻ caṅkat toṭakkaviḻāvil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟa aintiṟam eṉṟa nūl aṇmaik kālattut tōṉṟiyatu. tūya naṟṟamiḻil nūṟpā yāppiṉatu. palatuṟaik kalaikkuṟippuṭaiyatu. mayakkanaṭai mikkatu. paṉampāraṉār pāyiram kūṟum aintirattukkum ipputu nūlukkum evvakaiyāṉum toṭarpillai.
`mayaṅkāmarapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi' eṉpataṟku muṉṉai nūlkaḷil eḻuttilakkaṇam collilakkaṇattuḷ mayaṅkik kiṭantatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, muttamiḻ mayaṅkik kiṭantatu eṉac civañāṉa muṉivarum viḷakkuvar. tol kāppiyattukku muntiya nūl etuvum kiṭaikkātapōtu, ivvāṟu poruḷ koḷḷac cāṉṟu eṉṉa? eḻuttumuṟai - nūṉmuṟai eṉṟu porunta poruḷ ceyvar aracañ caṇmukaṉār. `eḻuttumuṟai' eṉṟa eṉ cintaṉaik kaṭṭuraiyil tolkāppiyar nūl eḻutiya muṟaikaḷai ārāyntu eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. ippāyirattil varum pōkkaṟu paṉuval, nāṉmaṟai, aintiṟam eṉṟa pakutikaḷ mēlum cintaṉaikku uriyavai.
pāyiramuṭivurai
ippāyira muṭivuraiyāka varalāṟṟu nōkkilum kāppu nōkkilum vaḷarcci nōkkilum tamiḻ, tamiḻiṉam, tamiḻakam paṟṟi eññāṉṟum iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṟivuraiyai eḻutuvēṉ. iḵtu uraikkuppuṟameṉa eṇṇuvatu tavaṟu. uraikkāppu, uraivaḷarcci, uraikkalvi ellām nilaipeṟa vēṇṭumeṉiṉ tamiḻ nilaipeṟṟāka vēṇṭum; tamiḻ nilaipeṟutal eṉpatu tamiḻar nilaipēṟākum; tamiḻar nilaipēṟu eṉpatu tamiḻakam nilai pēṟākum; tamiḻakam iṭattāṟkuṉṟiṉ, tamiḻar eṇṇikkai iyalpākak kuṉṟum; tamiḻar eṇ kuṉṟiṉ. kuṭiyaracil tamiḻiṉnilai āṟṟalaṟṟuk kuṉṟum. ivvāṟu nilamum makkaḷum moḻiyum kuṉṟiṉ ellākkalaiyum tamiḻil kuṉṟum. maṟaiyavum aḻiyavum ceyyum. ulakiṉai nilaipeṟuttalum iṟaivaṉiṉ oru kaṭamaiyākac cuṭṭap paṭumāyiṉ. tamiḻiṉai nilattālum āḷeṇṇālum nilaipeṟuttal tamiḻiṉattiṉ kaṭamai yaṉṟō?
tollūḻik kālattut tamiḻ nākarikam mokeñcotarai ārappākāṟum parantu kiṭantatu. paṉampāraṉār, tolkāppiyaṉār kālattut tamiḻulakam teṟkē paṉṉeṭuṅ kāvatam paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟoṭu kumarikkōṭāka nīṇṭiruntiruntatu. peruṅkaṭal kōḷukkuppiṉ kumarikkōṭu tāḻat teṉṉellai mākaṭalāyiṟṟu. kiḻakkē pūmpukār māmallapuram ellām kaṭalvāy amiḻntaṉa. teṉkumariyum paṉmuṟai alaivāyp paṭṭuc curuṅkalāyiṟṟu. iyaṟkaic cīṟṟattāl tamiḻpperunilam eytiya aḻivukaḷ nilanūliṉar tokuttuc cāṉṟupaṭak kāṭṭuvar. nam kaṇkāṇak kōṭik karaiyum karaiyalāyiṟṟu. perun tamiḻakam perukkuṟukkampaṭṭataṟkuc ciṉapperuṅkaṭal peruṅkāraṇam. itaṉaip poṟāta tuṟavi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ. “kumarikkōṭum koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa” eṉṟu vaitu pāṭiṉār; vaitu pāṭi nāṭṭuppaṟṟaiyūṭṭiṉar.
kaṭalkōḷ pōla moḻikkalappukkōḷum tamiḻp perunilattaik kuṟukkiyatu eṉṟa varalāṟṟuṇmai palarkkup paṭavillai; eṭuttuc coṉṉālum ēṟuvatillaiyē. cilappatikāramum patiṟṟuppattum iṉṉa piṟavum vaḻaṅkiya cēranāṭum āḻvārkaḷ pācuram icainta vēṅkaṭamalaiyum tamiḻiṟ pirintu pāratattiṉ piṟa mānilaṅkaḷ āyiṉa. ayalnāṭu kavaravillai eṉṟa ōr amaiti namakkut tōṉṟiṉum. tamiḻukku iḻappu eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. taṅkap poṉkaṭṭi maṭi tavaṟip pārata nilattupputaiyuṇṭālum, uṭaiyāṉukku iḻapput tāṉē. vēṅkaṭamum kumariyum teyvattaṉmaiyaṉa. kēṭila eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar mutalāyiṉōr pukaḻntālum tamiḻarkku eṉṉāyiṉa?
paṉṉeṭuṅkālam tamiḻakamāka irunta cēranāṭṭup pakutiyum vēṅkaṭappakutiyum ayalnilamāṉatu paṭaiyeṭup pālaṉṟu. paṇpāṭu māṟṟattālaṉṟu. āṭci vēṟupāṭ ṭālāṉṟu. orē oru kāraṇam ayaloli ayaleḻuttuk kalanta ayalmoḻik kalappākum. tamiḻakattiṉ ellaip pakutikaḷai viḻippōṭu tamiḻākak kāttuk koḷḷat tavaṟiyatālum, ayalmoḻikaḷ ellārtam cevivāykaḷil nirampap pukuntamaiyālum, ammoḻikaḷait tol kāppiyam mutalāṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟiyapaṭi tamiḻ vaṭivākkik koḷḷātu viṭṭamaiyālum, ayaloli kaḷōṭu ayalvarivaṭivaṅkaḷum eḻuttuvaḻakkil iṭam peṟṟamaiyālum tamiḻukku uḷḷa iṭam curuṅkiṟṟu; ayalukku iṭamperukiṟṟu; eṉavē moḻiyolik kalappu tamiḻukku iṭaviḻappu; piṟamoḻikaṭku iṭavirippu. ivvaralāṟṟu nilaiyai iṉiyēṉum tamiḻiṉattār eṇṇi uḷḷa tamiḻ nilattaiyāvatu emmoḻikkalappum evvolik kalappum evveḻuttuk kalappum pukaviṭātu kāttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭāmā? kaṇkāṇa iḻantum aṟivuṇarvu vēṇṭāmā?
kalappiṉṟit tamiḻait tamiḻiyaṟkaippaṭi kākkum tuṇivum aṟivum avviyaṟkaippaṭi kālattuṟkēṟṟa kalait tuṟaikaḷai yellām koṇṭu vantu vaḷarkkum uramum taramum, tamiḻait taṉṉiṟaivuṭaiyatākkum āḷviṉaiyum ācaiyum ovvoru tamiḻaccikkum tamiḻaṉukkum piṟappuk kaṭamaiyākum. ituvarai uṇarārum iṉiyuṇarka.
orupāl ivvāṟu palliḻappu nēriṉum intiya moḻikaḷuḷ tamiḻukkut taṉi mutaṉmaiyuṇṭu. ulakamoḻi, ulakaḷāviya moḻi eṉṟu ovvoru pārataṉum pāratat tamiḻaṉum cuṭṭikkāṭṭat takka orumoḻi tamiḻmoḻiyē. palaveḷi nāṭukaḷil tamiḻkkuṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  cila nāṭukaḷil tamiḻ āṭci moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iṭampeṟṟuḷḷatu. aintāvatu ulakattamiḻ mānāṭu naṭattiya ōr intiyamoḻi tamiḻē. intiya moḻikaḷuḷ mutaṟkaṇ ulaka mānāṭu kaṇṭatum tamiḻē, intiyāvil piṟa pala mānilaṅkaḷilum tamiḻar tamiḻp  paṟṟōṭu vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. enta ayalnāṭṭil iruntālum, enta ayalmānilattil iruntālum tamiḻarkku vīṭṭumoḻi tamiḻē. maṉaiyakattuḷ tamiḻkkuṭumpattār pēcuvatu tamiḻē. iṉṉanaṭaimuṟaiyāl kaṉṉittamiḻukkut taṉitta vāḻvuṇṭu. palatoṉ moḻikaḷaippōla aḻittoḻintu citaiyāmal. niṉṟu vaḷarntu perukum cīriḷamait tiṟam uṇṭu. tamiḻkkulam curuṅkāmal. tamiḻiṉam. curuṅkāmal, tamiḻcuruṅkāmal. kalappu iṉiyum perukāmal tamiḻttāyaik kākkum kuṟikkōḷ tamiḻarkku eṉṟum vēṇṭum.  iṉṟaiya tamiḻar ikkuṟikkōḷuṭaiyavar eṉpatu kaṇṭu ivvurai pārāṭṭukiṉṟatu; etirkālattamiḻaraiyum yāṇṭiruntālum ennilaiyil iruntālum tamiḻk kuṟikkōḷōṭu pāratattil taḻaittu vāḻuṅkaḷ eṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭi vāḻttukiṉṟāṉ ivvuraiyāḷaṉ.
tamiḻar kuṟikkōḷ tavaṟār kalappil
tamiḻē nilaipput tarum.

ciṟappuppāyiram māṇikkavurai muṟṟum.


tolkāppiyam – eḻuttatikāram
mutalāvatu nūṉmarapu - māṇikkavurai
iyal muṉṉurai
nūlukku marapāka vaḻaṅkiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷaik kūṟuvatu nūṉmarapākum. ivviyalil eḻuttilakkaṇattukku uriya eḻuttu, uyir, kuṟil, neṭil, mey, valliṉam, melliṉam. iṭaiyiṉam mutalāṉa pala kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kūṟappaṭum. curukkam, viḷakkam, eṭuttāṭci ivai kuṟiyīṭṭiṉ payaṉ. aṟiviyal kalaiyiyal eṉappaṭṭa ellāttuṟaikaṭkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ uḷa.
eḻuttatikārattiṉ mutaliyalāṉa nūṉmarapil eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷaic colvatu pōlac collatikārattiṉ mutaliyalāṉa kiḷaviyākkattil uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai, peyarviṉai iṭai uri eṉṟiṉṉa collilakkaṇak kuṟikaḷaiyum poruḷatikārattiṉ mutalāvatu akattiṇai yiyalil peruntiṇai, aintiṇai, kaikkiḷai, mutal karu uri eṉṟiṉaiya poruḷilakkaṇak kuṟikaḷaiyum colluvar āciriyar. pāyiram kūṟuvatupōla itu mayaṅkāmarapiṉ ōr eḻuttumuṟai.
nūl - ilakkaṇanūl; īṇṭu pōkkaṟu paṉuvalāṉa tolkāppiyattaik kuṟikkum. mutalāvatu iyalukku eḻuttumarapu eṉṟu peyar irukkalām. āṉāl nūliṉ toṭakkaviyal eṉṟu kāṭṭuvataṟku nūṉmarapu eṉṟa peyar vaikkappaṭṭatu. muṉṉuraiyai nūṉmukam eṉṟu iṉṟum nām vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
iyaṟkaruttu
tamiḻmoḻi akaramutal ṉakaram varai muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ (olikaḷ) koṇṭatu. kuṟṟiyalikaram. kuṟṟiyalukaram. āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟum eḻuttup pōlvaṉa. ivai cārpeḻuttām. puḷḷipeṟum. kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu oru māttirai icaikkum. neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu. iraṇṭu māttirai icaikkum ōreḻuttu mūṉṟu māttirai olikkum taṉmai tamiḻil illai. kūṭutalāṉa māttirai olikkavēṇṭiṉ. vēṇṭiya aḷapukkuriya eḻuttukkaḷaik kūṭṭi olikka vēṇṭum. imaikkum kālamum noṭikkum kālamum oru māttiraik kālavaḷavām.
akaramutal oḷakāram varai paṉṉiraṇṭum uyir eṉṟa kuṟiyum. kakaramutal ṉakaram varai patiṉeṭṭum mey eṉṟa kuṟiyum peṟum. meykkum mūṉṟu cārpeḻuttukkum māttirai arai. makaramey kāl māttirai olikkum iṭaṉuṇṭu.
kāl māttirai peṟum makarameykkup puḷḷi vaṭivu uḷḷāka irukkum. (ma) potuvāka meyyeḻuttukkaḷ taṉinilaiyil puḷḷivaṭivukaḷ peṟum. uyireḻuttukkaḷil ekarattukkum okarattukkum puḷḷivaṭivu uṇṭu. (eo) meyyum uyirum cērntu uyir meyyākuṅkāl varivaṭivu māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭum. k, c, t eṉavarum meyyeḻuttiṉ mēl irukkum puḷḷiyai maṭṭum eṭuttuviṭṭāl ka, ca, ta, eṉṟu akarattoṭu kūṭiya uyirmeyyākiviṭum. ēṉai uyir meykaḷ kā, ki, kī, ku, kū, ke, kē,  kai, ko, kō, kau eṉṟavāṟu pala vaṭivuttiripukaḷ eytum. uyirmey eṉpatu kuṟiyīṭāka iruntālum mutalil meyyoliyum piṉ uyiroliyum tōṉṟum.
mey valleḻuttu āṟu, melleḻuttu āṟu, iṭaiyeḻuttu āṟu eṉṟu muvviṉamām. ivai tammuḷ toṭaruvatu meymmayakkam (kalappu, iyaipu,) eṉappaṭum appā, aṇṇā, allā, eṉat taṉmeykkuppiṉ taṉmeyyē varutaluṇṭu. āṭci, āṇṭu āyvu eṉa orumeykkuppiṉ vēṟṟumey varutalumuṇṭu. ivai oroṟṟuṭaṉilai eṉappaṭum. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷil ra, ḻa, nīṅkiya patiṉāṟu meykaḷ tammuṉtām varum. ra, ḻa, iraṇṭiṉ muṉ cārpu vāḻvu eṉap piṟa eḻuttukkaḷē varum.
kuṟṟeḻuttu aintil a, i, u eṉṟa mūṉṟum cuṭṭuk kuṟiyum. neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻil ā, ē, ō eṉṟa mūṉṟum viṉākkuṟiyum peṟum.
uyireḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttukkaḷum mēlē colliya māttiraiyaḷavukaḷaik kaṭantu, icaittamiḻil icaittal uṇṭu eṉṟu yāḻmaṟai kūṟum.
karuttu viḷakkam
eḻuttu eṉpatu tamiḻil poruṭcuṭṭuṭaiya moḻiyoliyaik kuṟikkum; varivaṭivaiyum kuṟikkum. nūṉmarapu mutaṟkaṇ olikaḷaiyum olikkuriya aḷapukaḷaiyum kūṟiya piṉ, cilavaṟṟiṟku varivaṭivum kūṟum. ilakkaṇat teḷivukku vēṇṭappaṭum varivaṭivukaḷai maṭṭum tolkāppiyam nūṉmarapiṟ collum.
mayakkam - oru meyyeḻuttōṭu oru meyyeḻuttut toṭarum nilai, cērntiyaiyum taṉmai. nām iṉṟu kūṟum kalakkam, kuḻappam eṉṟa poruḷillai. meymmayakkam eṉpatu tamiḻukku uyiraṉaiya amaippukkūṟu. iṉṉa meykkuppiṉ iṉṉameytāṉ varalām eṉṟa moḻiyiyalpai itaṉāl uṇaralām. moḻi nilaipēṟṟukkum moḻik kāppukkum collākkattiṟkum colloḻuṅkukkum iv vilakkaṇam varampāṉatu. itu ceyaṟkaiyākap pukuttum puṟavitiyaṉṟu. makkaḷ vāyvaḻakkiliruntu tōṉṟum iyalpu neṟi. moḻikku moḻi immayakkam vēṟupaṭumātaliṉ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ amaippai naṉkuaṟintu vaḻātu pēcavum eḻutavum vēṇṭum. ellā ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum itaṉai yoṭṭi naṭattaliṉ nūṉmarapu meymmayakkam paṟṟi oṉpatu nūṟpākkaḷil virittuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
oli, eṇṇikkai, vakai, aḷapu, vaṭivu, mayakkam eṉṟa eḻuttiṉ aṟukūṟukaḷ nūṉmarapil moḻiyappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum innūṉmarapu taṉiyeḻuttukkut taṉitta nilaiyil, ataṉ taṉmaikaḷai nuvalum. varivaṭivupaṟṟiya ceyti nūṉmarapaḷavil niṉṟuviṭum. puṇarcciyilakkaṇam aṉaittum olinilaiyil vaittu vitikkappaṭuvaṉa eṉpataṉai maṟavātu niṉaittuk koḷka. ātaliṉ eḻuttu eṉpatu olinilaiyaik kuṟikkum eṉavum eḻuttatikāram eṉpatu olinilai yaṭippaṭaiyil oli, piṟappu, oliyiṭaippuṇarcci mutalāṉa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷaic cuṭṭum atikāram eṉavum aṟika.
muppatu taṉiyolikkum muppatu taṉivarivaṭivaṅkaḷ tamiḻil uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu ovvōr oliyum taṉakkeṉa oruvarivaṭivu uṭaiyatu. ivvuṭaimai cemmaiyāṉa tamiḻ varalāṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. iṅ'ṉam oṟṟumaiyiruttaliṉ eḻuttu eṉṟa oru kuṟiyīṭē iraṇṭiṉaiyum kuṟikkap payaṉpaṭṭatu; eṉiṉum ikkāla vaḷarcciyil mēlum teḷivu karuvi oliyeḻuttai oliyaṉ eṉavum variyeḻuttai vaṭivaṉ eṉavum putiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ amaittukkoḷvōm. oliyaṉai `eḻuttoli' eṉpar naṉnūlār.


tamiḻukku eḻuttu muppatu
(1)	eḻutteṉap paṭupa
	akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy
	muppaḵ teṉpa
	cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
innūṟpā eḻuttu eṉṟa mutaṟkuṟiyīṭum muppatu eṉṟa eṇ varampum kūṟum.
akalavurai
tamiḻmoḻikku aṭippaṭai mutaleḻuttu eṉṟu vaḻivaḻic collappaṭupavai muppatu. ivai akaram toṭaṅki ṉakaraviṟutiyākum. cārpeḻuttu eṉṟu aṭutta nūṟpāvil kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟum ivaṟṟoṭu cērā. mutaleḻuttākā: mutaleḻuttu varicaiyil eṇṇappaṭā eṉpatu karuttu. mūṉṟalaṅkaṭai - mūṉṟu allāta iṭattu a ā i ī u ū e ē ai o ō oḷa; k ṅ c ¨ñ ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟṉ -ivai ikkāla vaṭivaṉkaḷ.
tiṟaṉurai
eḻuttu eṉpatu īṇṭu oliyeḻuttaic cuṭṭum. ellā moḻikkum oli mūlam ātalāṉum, iḵtu eḻuttatikāram ātalāṉum mutalnūṟpā eḻuttu eṉat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ vāyiliruntu varum ōcaiyellām eḻutteṉap paṭuvatillai. poruṇmaiyāṟṟaluṭaiya oliyē eḻuttu eṉappaṭum. ittaṉic ciṟappiṉaic cuṭṭutaṟkē `eḻuttu eṉappaṭupa' eṉṟu naṭaiceytār āciriyar. tamiḻmoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷaik koṇṭu kūṭṭiyalacip pārppiṉ, avaiyellām muppatu oliyaṉil aṭaṅki niṟkum eṉpatu tamiḻ nūlōr kaṇṭa aṟiviyal muṭipu. ivvarampu tolkāppiyarkkup paṉṉeṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē ēṟpaṭṭu vaḻivaḻi vantatu eṉṟa pēruṇmaiyai `muppaḵtu eṉpa' eṉṟa toṭarāl aṟiyalām.
eḻuttu eṉpataṉ ilakkaṇam yātu? poruṇmaic cuṭṭu maṭṭum uṭaiyataṉṟu; ataṉōṭu taṉakkeṉṟa poruṭkuṟippāṟṟalum poruḷ vēṟupaṭukkum muraṇāṟṟalum uṭaiyatu. muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum ivvilakkaṇappaṭi oṉṟaṉaiyoṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttum āṟṟaluṭaiyavai; tam poruḷāṟṟalai nilai niṟuttupavai. avaṉ, avaḷ, avar eṉṟa mūṉṟu coṟkaḷum etaṉāl poruḷvēṟum pālvēṟum paṭukiṉṟaṉa? īṟṟiluḷḷa ṉakaram ḷakaram rakaram eṉṟa vēṟupaṭṭa oli varavē kāraṇam. vāl, vāḷ, vāḻ ivai īṟṟil varum lakaram ḷakaram ḻakaram ivaṟṟāl poruḷ vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ivai oliyaṉkaḷ aṭu, āṭu, iṭu, īṭu: uṭu, ūṭu, eṭu, ēṭu; oṭu, ōṭu ivaiyellām poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaṟku vēṟuvēṟu uyireḻuttukkaḷē aṭippaṭaiyām. iṅṅaṉam pallāyiram tamiḻccoṟkaḷaip poruḷpaṭa vaittu vēṟṟumaikkāraṇam pārppiṉ, avaiyellām muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ cērkkaiyākavum muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vēṟṟu nilait taṉiyāṟṟalākavum muṭiyak kāṇalām. ulakap poruṭkaḷaiyellām 110 mūlaṅkaḷāka vētiyiyal aṭakki muṭippatai oppunōkkuka.
vētiyiyal poruḷmūlattiṟkum tamiḻ oli mūlattiṟkum oru vēṟṟumaiyuṇṭu. putupporuḷ kāṇakkāṇa vētiyiyal mūlakkūṟukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai kūṭivarum. paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻukku muppatu oliyaṉ eṉpatu muṭintavarampu. ayaloliyaṉ vantu kūṭutaṟkut tamiḻil iṭaṉillai, tamiḻukku iyaṟkaiyillai eṉpatu immutal nūṟpā kāṭṭum muṭintanilai. ataṉālaṉṟō kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam mūṉṟum tamiḻolikaḷāka iruntālum mutaleḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭa villai. itaṉai mūṉṟalaṅkaṭai eḻutteṉappaṭuvaṉa muppaḵtu eṉṟa etirmaṟai naṭaiyāl valiyuṟuttiṉār āciriyar. cārpeḻuttām tamilolikkē itu varampāyiṉ, ayaloliyum ayaleḻuttum tamiḻ varampukkuḷ nuḻaitalō nuḻaittalō cālumā? tolkāppiyattukkup piṉ vanta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum itālkāppiyattaṭam patintaṉavē. muppatu eṉṟa eṇ varampum eḻuttu varampum tamiḻ naṉceykku eṉṟum māṟātavai. tolkāppiyar varainta immoḻi varampiṉait tiruvaḷḷuvarum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar. mutaṟkuṟaḷ `akaramutal' eṉat toṭaṅkuvatum iṟutikkuṟaḷ `kūṭi muyaṅkaṟpeṟiṉ' eṉa ṉakara īṟāka muṭivatum kāṇka.
eṟumpumutal yāṉai īṟāka eṉavum oṉṟu mutal nūṟu īṟāka eṉavum kumari mutal imayam varai eṉavum naṭaivaḻakku uṇṭu. atupōl akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy eṉṟār. palarkkum iyalpākat terinta ceyti eṉpatu innaṭaik karuttu. evvakai eḻuvāyum kūṟāmal `eṉpa' eṉṟataṉāl muppatu eṉṟa tokai vaḻakku kālañcuṭṭa muṭiyāt toṉmaittākum.
cārntu varal marapiṉmūṉṟu - taṉ piṟappukkum veḷippāṭṭiṟkum oru mutaleḻuttaic cārntu varum iyalpuṭaiyaṉa. itaṉāl eḻutteṉappaṭṭa muppatum piritoṉṟiṉai oṭṭinillātu tāmē piṟantu veḷippaṭum taṉittaṉmaiyāṉa eṉpaṉa teḷivām. tolkāppiyar `eḻuttu' eṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiyataṉaip piṉṉaiyōr mutaleḻuttu eṉṟu teḷivupaṭuttuvar. ayal nāṭṭavaṉai nōkki uḷnāṭṭavaṉ eṉavum ayalmoḻiyai nōkkit tamiḻmoḻiyait tāymoḻi eṉavum colvatai oppunōkkuka. `cārntu varal marapiṉ' eṉṟa aṭaiyē im mūṉṟum mutaleḻuttākā eṉpataṟkuriya ētuvaikkāṭṭi niṟkum.
oruviṉā: “eḻutteṉap paṭupa akaramutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa” eṉṟa aḷavil mutal nūṟpā amain tirukkalāmē; mūṉṟum allāta viṭattu eṉṟu vilakkic colliyataṉ payaṉ eṉṉa? itu paṟṟi aṭutta nūṟpā irukkumpōtu, ivvilakkuc collavēṇṭumā? itu vilakkiccollum kuṟippaṉṟu. muppattu mūṉṟu eṉṟu eṇṇāviṭiṉum okka uṭaṉ niṉaikkat takkavai eṉpatu kuṟippu. muppatu paḻaṅkaḷ ikkūṭaiyil uḷḷaṉa. mūṉṟu piñcukaḷ tavira eṉṟa naṭaipōṉṟatu mutal nūṟpā naṭai. “mūṉṟutalaiyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttu” eṉṟu tolkāppiyam puṇariyal iyaittumoḻitalai niṉaika. immūṉṟukkum taṉippuṇarccikaḷ uṇmaiyiṉ okkak kūṟavēṇṭiya kaṭappāṭu ilakkaṇikku uṇṭu. ivvāṟu oru nūṟpāviṟ kūṟāviṭṭāl. immūṉṟum cārpeṉat tōṉṟātu taṉimutaṉmai peṟṟu viṭumaṉṟō? itaṉait tavirkkavē `mūṉṟalaṅkaṭai' eṉa iṟutiyiṟ kūṟiṉār. `muppaḵtu eṉpa cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē' eṉpataṉāl muppatu eḻuttuppōla immūṉṟum tolmarapuṭaiyavai eṉṟa varalāṟu peṟappaṭum.
tamiḻmoḻikku eḻuttu muppatu eṉṟa oru ceytiyait taruvatu tolkāppiyattiṉ nōkkamaṉṟu ivvāṟu ivvarampaik kaṭaippiṭittu oḻukuka eṉpatu nūṟpāviṉ payaṉ.
yāmeyyāk kaṇṭavaṟṟuḷ illai eṉaittoṉṟum
vāymaiyiṉ nalla piṟa
eṉṟu tām teḷintuṇarntatai veḷippaṭuttik koḷvatā tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ eṇṇam? illai. illai. ittaku ciṟappuṭaiya vāymaiyoḻukkattaip poyyātu kaṭaip piṭittu vāḻka eṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭuvatu tirukkuṟaḷiṉ payaṉ. inneṟippiṭiyait tolkāppiyattukkum tirukkuṟaṭkum pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.

cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟu
(2)	avaitām
	kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
	āyta meṉṟa
	muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻutō raṉṉa	
itu muṉnūṟpāviṟ cuṭṭiya cārpu mūṉṟiṉaik kūṟum.
akalavurai
mēlainūṟpāvil cārntu varal marapuṭaiya mūṉṟu eṉṟārallavā? avai kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟu kuṟiyaṉa. ivai mūṉṟum puḷḷi peṟuvaṉa. mēlainūṟpāviṟ colliya eḻuttai yottaṉa.
vaḻakku kēṇmiyā, nāṭu, e.ku (eḵku)
tiṟaṉurai
kuṟiyīṭu nērkuṟi, viḷakkakkuṟi eṉa iruvakai. mukkāli, nāṟkāli, kāṟṟāṭi eṉṟa coṟkaḷ viḷakkakkuṟikaḷ; ivaṟṟiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷ nērkuṟikaḷ, taṉakkuttāṉē peyar nērkuṟi: taṉṉiyalpaic cuṭṭum peyar viḷakkakkuṟi ivvakaiyil eḻuttu (mutaleḻuttu) muppatum nērkuṟikaḷ. a ā k ṅ eṉpaṉa piṟappup peyar. kuṟṟiyalikaram mūṉṟum kāraṇappeyarkaḷ allatu viḷakkap peyarkaḷ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. ivaṟṟukku nērpeyarkaḷ ila, cārntu varuvaṉavātaliṉ itukarutiyē, `eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉṟu cārpu kūṟiṉār.
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟa peyarkaḷ ikara vukara eḻuttukkaḷiṉ cāyaliṉa eṉpatu teḷivu. ikaramum ukaramum mutaleḻuttām. kuṟṟeḻuttām avai oru māttirai yuṭaiyavai; kuṟṟikaram kuṟṟukaram eṉavum collappaṭum. ivaṟṟai vēṟupaṭuttavē araimāttiraiyuṭaiya cār peḻuttukkaḷ kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉappeyar eytiṉa. tēynta kācu, kiḻinta āṭai, iṭinta cuvar pōla eṉka.
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram pōla āytamum kuṟumai yuṭaiyatēṉum nēraṭiyāka oru mutaleḻuttiṉ cāyalaṉṟu. cila vuyirum cila meyyum kalanta cārpuppiṟappiṉatu. tēyvut taṉmaiyatu. āytam eṉpataṉai “āytal uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam” eṉṟa uriyiyalāṉum, “nalipuvaṇṇam āytam payilum”eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyalāṉum aṟiyalām. āyutaṅkaḷ kūrmaikkākat tīṭṭit tēykkap paṭutalai vaḻakkāṉum uṇarka.
immūṉṟum ōriṉam ātalāl māttiraiyaḷavu kūṟumpōtu `avviyal nilaiyum ēṉai mūṉṟē' eṉa nūṉmarapilum piṟappilum kūṟuṅkālai tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum eṉap piṟappiyalilum `ēṉaimūṉṟu' eṉṟa tokaikkuṟi āḷuvar. muppatiṉoṭu otta ēṉai mūṉṟu eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. immūṉṟum cārntuvarum oli nilaiyil maṭṭum ōriṉam alla; puḷḷipeṟum varivaṭivilum ōriṉappaṭum eṉṟu uṭaṉterivikkavē `muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉṟār. eṉavē ovvoṉṟum puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu teḷivu.
“oṟṟeḻuttiyaṟṟē kuṟṟiya likaram” eṉpatu ceyyuḷiyal itaṉāl kuṟṟiyalikaram oṟṟākiya puḷḷi peṟum eṉpatu pōtarum. “meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyil “kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa” eṉṟa puṇariyal nūṟpākkaḷāl kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. `āytappuḷḷi' eṉṟē moḻi marapilum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalilum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalilum kūṟuvatāl puḷḷipeṟum eṉpatu teḷivu. puḷḷi eytum taṉmaiyālum ivai ōriṉam eṉpataṉai veḷippaṭuttavē `muppāṟpuḷḷi' eṉṟār āciriyar. ivai puḷḷipeṟuṅkāl puḷḷi niṟkum nilaiyil cila vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiya lukaramum uriya ikara ukara vaṭiviṉmēl orupuḷḷi peṟum. miyā, nāṭu eṉa varum; mutaleḻuttu vaṭivaṉē ivaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaṉ; puḷḷi mēlē peṟuvatu cārpu kāṭṭuvatām.
āytam cilavuyirum cila meyyum kalanta cārpātaliṉ. enta uyirvaṭivaṉ mēlō enta meyvaṭivaṉ mēlō puḷḷi peṟutal poruntātu. avvāṟu peṟṟāl meykkuriya puḷḷiyākavō cilapōtu ekara okaraṅkaṭkuriya puḷḷi yākavō mayakkat tarum. taṉiyuyirum ākātu taṉimeyyum ākātu eṉṟa iraṇṭuṅ keṭṭāṉ nilaiyil naṭuvē oru puḷḷi iṭaippaṭṭu niṟkum. eḵku, aḵṟiṇai, veḵku, eṉavarum. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram tattam mutal vaṭivaṉaic cārntaṉa. āytattukku aṉṉa cārpiṉmaiyāl oru naṭuppuḷḷiyē ataṉ vaṭivaṉ āyiṟṟu. 0 eṉṟa iṉmaik kuṟipōla āytam (.) eṉṟa puḷḷiyē koṇṭatu eṉavum. oru puḷḷitāṉ uṭaiyatu eṉavum atu naṭuvil niṟpatu eṉavum.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittē
eṉṟa moḻimarapu nūṟpāvāl teḷiyavēṇṭum. āytappuḷḷi eṉpataṉāl puḷḷiyuruvē ataṉuru eṉpatum puḷḷi micaiya eṉṉāmal `micaittu' eṉṟa orumaip payaṉilaiyāl appuḷḷi oṉṟutāṉ eṉpatum muṉṉar micaittē eṉpataṉāl niṟkumiṭam iṭai allatu naṭuvu eṉpatum teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum.
ivviḷakkattāl innūṟpāviṉ nērurai piṉvarumāṟu: kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟuvakaiyāṉa puḷḷiyolikaḷum mēṟkūṟiya eḻuttōṭu oppaṉa.
kuṟṟeḻuttu
(3)	avaṟṟuḷ
	a i u
	e o veṉṉu mappā laintum
	ōraḷa picaikkuṅ kuṟṟeḻun teṉpa
iḵtu oru māttirai peṟum eḻuttukkuṟi kūṟum.
akalavurai
mutal nūṟpāvil eḻuttu muppatu eṉappaṭṭatallavā? avaṟṟuḷ a i u e o eṉṟa antappakuti aintum taṉittaṉi oru māttiraiyaḷavu olikkum. ataṉāl kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum eṉṟu colluvar nūlōr.
tiṟaṉurai
`avaṟṟuḷ' eṉpatu `akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāymuppaḵtu' eṉpataṉaic cuṭṭum. mēlai nūṟpāvaik kuṟikkātu,aṅku tokaic col illāmaiyiṉ, `ōraḷavu icaikkum kuṟṟeḻuttu' eṉpatil kuṟil eṉṟu colvataṟkuk kāraṇam amaintuḷḷatu. itu peyareccak kāraṇanaṭai. poy collum ciṉṉavaṉ, paṭitta mētai eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
aintum oru māttirai icaikkum eṉṟālum ovvoru kuṟṟeḻuttum oru māttirai peṟum eṉpatu naṭaiyiṉ karuttu. kuṭumpa aṭṭaikkārarkkellām poṅkalukku oru kilō cīṉi kūṭutalāka vaḻaṅkappaṭum eṉṟa naṭaiyai nām iṉṟum kūṟavillaiyā?
aḷapum aḷavum vēṟuvēṟu poruḷaṉa. aḷapu - māttirai, oliyiṉ kālakkaṇakku. aḷapeṭaiyeṉa varumēyaṉṟi aḷaveṭaiyeṉa vārātu. aḷavu - taram, kūṟu itu eṇṇaḷavu niṟaiyaḷavu eṉa varum. kuṟṟeḻuttu oru māttiraiyaḷavu. neṭṭeḻuttu iru māttiraiyaḷavu eṉakkūṟalām. koḷḷaḷavu, aḷavōṭu peṟuka eṉpaṉa iṉṟaiya vaḻakku.
icaittal - poruṇmaippaṭa oḻuṅkupaṭa olittal. itu moḻiyoliyaik kuṟikkum.
neṭṭeḻuttu
(4)	ā ī ū
	ē ai 
	ō oḷa veṉṉu  mappā lēḻum
	īraḷa picaikkumneṭṭeḻut teṉpa.
iḵtu iraṇṭu māttirai peṟum eḻuttukkuṟi kūṟum.
akalavurai
ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉṟa appakuti eḻum taṉittaṉi iraṇṭu māttirai olikkum. aṅṅaṉam irumāttirai kāraṇamāka neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum eṉṟu colluvar nūlōr.
tiṟaṉurai
mēlaic cēvaṟpaṭṭi kīḻaiccēvaṟpaṭṭi eṉṟāṟpōl kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpaṉa oṉṟaiyoṉṟu nōkkiṉa; eṉṟālum kuṟṟeḻuttu oru māttirai. mēlum nīṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttāka māṟavillai. a a iraṇṭu cērntu ā eṉṟa neṭilāvatillai i i  iraṇṭu cērntu ī eṉṟa neṭilāvatillai. a eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu; ā eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu. i eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu; ī eṉpatum taṉippiṟappu. ai oḷa mutalāṉa piṟavum aṉṉa. itaṉāṟṟāṉ kuṟṟeḻuttu nīṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttu ākum eṉṉāmal `īraḷapicaikkum neṭṭeḻuttu' eṉṟār. mutal nūṟpāvil varaiyaṟuttatupōl eḻutteṉappaṭum ovvoṉṟum poruṇmaiyil taṉittaṉmaiyum mutaṉmaiyum uṭaiyatu. ivvilakkaṇam eṉṟum niṉaivu koḷḷattakkatu.
aintum kuṟṟeḻutteṉṟālum aḷapoṟṟumaiyil ōriṉamāmaṉṟip poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyilum piṟappilum taṉittaṉmaip peṟṟiyaṉa. immoḻiyuṇmaiyai neṭṭeḻuttukkum meyyeḻuttukkum valliṉa melliṉa iṭayiṉattukkum koḷka. tāy tantai kaṇavaṉ maṉaivi makkaḷ uṭaṉpiṟantār eṉa uṟavoṟṟumaiyiruntālum vāyum vayiṟum vēṟu eṉṟa vaḻakkuṇṭaṉṟō.
oruviṉā: kuṟṟeḻuttu aintāka neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu varalāmā? uyir paṉṉiraṇṭāka meypatiṉeṭṭu varalāmā?
naṉṟāka varalām. kuṟiyiliruntu neṭil piṟappatillai; uyirukkum meykkum otta toṭarpillai ellām poruṇmaiyāṟṟalāl taṉittaṉip piṟappiṉavai aim poṟikaḷuḷ meyoṉṟu, vāy oṉṟu, mūkku iraṇṭu, kaṇ, iraṇṭu, ceviyiraṇṭu irukkalāmā eṉpatu pōṉṟa viṉā itu. itu piṟappiṉ iyaṟkai. neṭṭeḻuttu īṇṭu ōr oliyaṉaittāṉ kuṟikkum; vaṭivaṉaik kuṟikkavillai. ataṉāl ū oḷa eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟukkum iraṇṭu vaṭivaṉkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉavē eṉṟu mayaṅka vēṇṭā.
aḷapeṭai
(5)	mūvaḷa picaittal ōreḻut tiṉṟē
	nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭaiya
	kūṭṭi yeḻūuta leṉmaṉār pulavar
iḵtu eḻuttu varampiṉai uṟutippaṭuttuvatōṭu kūṟutalolikku vaḻi kūṟum.
akalavurai
tamiḻmoḻikkaṇ oru māttiraiyuṭaiya ōr oliyaṉ uṇṭu; iru māttiraiyuṭaiya ōr oliyaṉ uṇṭu. mūṉṟu māttiraiyuṭaiya ōr oliyaṉ illai. iraṇṭu māttiraikku mēlāka vēṇṭiṉāl anta aḷapukkuriya olikaḷaik kūṭṭi olittuk koḷka eṉṟu vaḻi kūṟuvar ilakkaṇappulavar.
uṭaiya - uṭaiya olikaḷai; uṭaiyaṉavaṟṟai; aṉcāriyai peṟāta kuṟippu viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyar ceyaṟpaṭuporuḷ, eḻuutal - oliyeḻuppuka, olikka, viyaṅkōḷviṉai.
vaḻakku
aṇṇāa, tōḻīi, āṭūu, vēel, kōoṉeṉavum pukāaar nampīii eṉavum varum. `naṟṟāḷ toḻāar (2) `ceṟāa ay vāḻiya' (1200) eṉpaṉa tirukkuṟaḷ.
tiṟaṉurai
(a) innūṟpā tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭai eḻuttuvarampai niṉaivūṭṭi uṟutippaṭuttum peruñciṟappiṉatu; oppiyal moḻiyāyvukku uṟutuṇaiyāvatu. eḻutteṉappaṭuvatu veṟṟoliyaṉṟu; veṟum vāyōcaiyaṉṟu; poruṇmai potintataṉṟē. ōraḷapicaikkum kuṟilukkum īraḷapicaikkum neṭilukkum poruṇmaiyuṇṭu eṉpatum kuṟililiruntu neṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu piṟakkavillai eṉpatum ovvōr eḻuttum taṉakkeṉṟa taṉmaiyum vēṟupaṭuttum āṟṟalum vāyntatu eṉpatum muṉṉart teriyak kaṇṭōm. ivviḷakkam piṉvarum patiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttukkum okkum.
kāṇka:	
ali		aḷi		aḻi;
	āli		āḷi		āḻi;
	kali		kaḷi		kaḻi;
	vali		vaḷi		vaḻi;
	naṭa		naṭi		naṭu;
	kaṭa		kaṭi		kaṭu;
	paṭṭu		pāṭṭu	piṭṭu		pūṭṭu		poṭṭu;
	nāṉ		nām		nāṇ		nāy		nār		nāl 	 nāḷ.
(ā) iṅṅaṉam pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ oliyaṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷāl vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. perukukiṉṟaṉa. vaḷantarukiṉṟaṉa. ōr oliyaṉ ōriṭattu māṟiṉālum pala oliyaṉkaḷ mutaṉilai iṭainilai iṟutinilaikaḷil māṟiṉālum poruḷ māṟṟattai aṟikiṉṟōm. immuṟai ulaka voliyaṉmuṟai. cila moḻikaḷil mūṉṟu māttirai yuṭaiya oliyaṉum uṇṭu. tamiḻil attaku amaippu illai. īṇṭu oliyaṉaip paṟṟiyap pēcum pōtellām kaṇ pārkkum vaṭivaṉkaḷai uṭaṉ eṇṇakkūṭātu cevikēṭkum oliyaḷavaiyē karutal vēṇṭum.
(i) aṭu - kuṟṟeḻuttut toṭaṅkiyatu;
āṭu - neṭṭeḻuttut toṭaṅkiyatu. ā eṉṟa neṭilukkumēl poruḷ tarum mūvuḷavuṭaiya uyirneṭil tamiḻil illai. māttirai kūṭutalāka olippatai innūṟpā taṭukkavillai aṅṅaṉam olikkalām eṉpataṉaiyum vitikkiṉṟatu. āṉāl akkūṭutalāṉa olikaṭkup poru ḷillai eṉavum poruḷillaiyeṉavē eḻuttākā eṉavum teḷivu ceykiṉṟatu. āaṭu āaaṭu eṉṟu mūṉṟu māttiraiyākavum nāṉku māttiraiyākavum kūṭutalāka nīṭṭiṉālum āṭu eṉṟa poruḷ vēṟupaṭutalumillai kūṭutalumillai eṉpatu moḻiyiyal.
(ī)  `ōreḻuttu iṉṟē' eṉpataṉ karuttu:
mūṉṟaḷavu olittal ōr oliyaṉ illai eṉpatāl iraṇṭu oliyaṉkaḷil uṇṭu eṉṟu karuttuk koḷḷal tavaṟu tamiḻil evvakaiyālum appaṭippaṭṭa oliyaṉ illai eṉpatuvē muṭinta muṭipu. iru oliyaṉkaḷil mūvaḷavu icaikkum eṉṟu koṇṭāl oliyaṉukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta ilakkaṇamāṉa poruṇmai kūṭutal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? āaṭu eṉṟa nīṭciyāl poruṇmai vēṟupaṭāmaiyiṉ muppatukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttē illai eṉpatu teḷivu. eṉavē aḷapeṭai oliyaṉ aṉṟu.
(u) `nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ avvaḷapuṭaiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal. eṉpataṉ karuttu: tēvaippaṭṭāl eṉpataṉāl itu moḻikku aṭippaṭaiyaṉṟu eṉpatum kūṭṭi olittuk koḷka eṉpataṉāl ceyaṟkai eṉpatum peṟalām toḻāar, pōoy, pulāal, tūuymai vāaymai eṉṟaviṭattu oru māttirai kūṭṭiṉāṟpōtum. viṭūum, peṟūum, varūum, tarūum eṉṟaviṭattu irumāttirai kūṭṭa vēṇṭum. ātaliṉ `avvaḷapuṭaiya' eṉṟu paṉmaippaṭa moḻintār. `eḻuutal' eṉanūṟpāvilē akakkāṭṭu amaintirukkum nayam kāṇka.
(ū) itu aḷapeṭai eṉṟa kuṟipeṟum ceyyuṭkum potu nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ eṉṟu potumpaṭac coṉṉamai yāl mūṉṟu mutalākap pala māttiraiyaḷavu olikkum. oruvaraik kūppiṭum viḷivaḻakkil nīṇṭu payiṉṟu varum eṉpataṉai viḷimarapiyaliṟ kāṇka.
(ē) aḷapeṭaiyaic colluvatilum eḻutuvatilum iṉṟu naṭaimuṟaippiḻai malintu varukiṉṟatu. nūṟpāviṉpaṭi kūṭutal oliyaik kūṭṭinīṭṭic colla vēṇṭumēyaṉṟip pirittut taṉittuc collavum kūṭātu. eḻutavum kūṭātu.
kaṟṟataṉā lāya payaṉeṉkol vālaṟivaṉ
naṟṟāḷ toḻāar eṉiṉ.
keṭuppatūum keṭṭārkkuc cārvāy maṟṟāṅkē
eṭuppatūum ellām maḻai
ḻā, tū ivaṟṟai mēlum oru māttiraiyaḷavu appaṭiyē nīṭṭic colluka; ar, um, eṉap pirittuc collaṟka; pirittu eḻutaṟka; aṭutta varikkuk koṇṭu cellaṟka. itu kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya oli neṟi.
māttirai
(6)	kaṇṇimai noṭiyeṉa avvē māttirai
	nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarttōr kaṇṭa vāṟē
itu māttirai eṉṟa kuṟiyiṭum ataṟku viḷakkamum kūṟum.
akalavurai
māttirai eṉpatu kuṟiyīṭu. itu oliyaḷavukku varum. māttirai yeṉṟāl eṉṉa? ovvoru muṟaiyum iyalpākak kaṇtiṟantu imaippataṟku ettuṇaik kālam irukkum. attuṇaiyaḷavuṭaiya oli oru māttiraikku oppām. atu pōlavē peruviralai naṭuviralōṭu cērttuk kaiviralai iyalpākac cuṇṭum pōtu entavaḷavu kālam vēṇṭum. antavaḷavu oru māttiraikkuc camam. māttiraiyaik kaṇṇālum ceviyālum nuṇmaiyāka uṇarnta muttamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aḷantu kaṇṭamuṟaikaḷ ivai. avvē appaṭippaṭṭa īraḷavām. kaṇṭavāṟu avvē eṉa muṭikka māttirai iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tokai.

tiṟaṉurai
aḷapu eṉpataṉ maṟṟoru tamiḻp peyar māttirai emmāttiram eṉpatu vaḻakku. mā eṉṟa col ciṟiya aḷavukku oru peyarākum eṉpataṉai mākāṇi orumā irumā eṉṟa vaḻakkiṟ kāṇka. `vaḻaṅkiyal mā' eṉat tolkāppiyamum. `māniṟaivillatum paṉṉāṭkākum' (184) eṉappuṟanāþṉūṟum moḻiyum.
kaṇṇimai iyaṟkai. toḻiṟkālam; noṭi ceyaṟkai; ōcaikkālam. iraṇṭumē kāla vaḷavaik kuṟikkum. imaippoḻutu noṭippoḻutu eṉpatu vaḻakku. eḻuttiṉ māttirai ōcaiyum kālamum iṇaintatātaliṉ, tolkāppiyam irucāṉṟu collum. iraṇṭum orē nērattuc ceytaṟ kuriyavai. īṇṭukkūṟum ilakkaṇam oru māttiraikku uriyatu. imaittalai imai eṉavum noṭittalai noṭi eṉavum mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyarākak kūṟiṉār. pukaittalaip pukai eṉṟum veṭittalai veṭi eṉavum kūṟumāppōla eḷitākap purintu koḷḷalām eṉpatu `avvē' eṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ kuṟippu. kaṇṇimaiyum noṭiyum yārkkum eḷimaiyallavā? `maṉattukkaṇ mācilaṉātal aṉaittaṟaṉ eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ naṭaikkuṟippu.
māttiraiyai nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr - kaṇṇāl imaiyaḷavaiyum ceviyāl noṭiyaḷavaiyum kaṇittōr, ikkaruttiṉaiyē.
kaṇṇiṉum ceviyiṉum tiṇṇiti ṉuṇarum
uṇarvuṭai māntark kallatu teriyiṉ
naṉṉayap poruḷkōḷ eṇṇaruṅ kuraittē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar meyppāṭṭiyaliṟ pulappaṭuttuvar.
aḷapu, māttirai eṉṟa kiḷavikaḷ oru poruḷaṉa vāyiṉum toṭarāṭciyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. aḷapeṭai eṉpōm. māttiraiyeṭai eṉṉōm; māttiraiyaḷavu eṉpōm aḷapaḷavu eṉṉōm aḷapiṟantu uyirttal eṉpōm. māttiraiyiṟantu uyirttal eṉṉōm. ivvēṟṟumaikkuk kāraṇam aḷapu eṉpatu māttiraiyilum viritta taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu.


uyirkkuṟi
(7) 	oḷakāra viṟuvāyp
	paṉṉī reḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa.
itu muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum uyireḻuttuk kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavurai
mutal nūṟpāvil eḻutteṉap paṭuvaṉa akaramutal eṉṟu colliyiruppataṉāl, innūṟpāvil oḷakāra iṟuti eṉṟār. eṉavē akara mutalāka oḷakāram varai paṉṉiraṇṭu oliyaṉum uyir eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu koḷḷum eṉṟu colluvar nūlōr.
vaḻakku
a ā i ī u ū e ē ai o ō oḷa eṉap paṉṉiraṇṭām.
tiṟaṉurai
mēlē aintum kuṟṟeḻuttām, ēḻum  neṭṭeḻuttām eṉṟu uṭkuṟiyīṭu cuṭṭiṉavar innūṟpāvil avviru pakutikkum uyir eṉṟa potukkuṟi kāṭṭuvar.
uyir eṉṟa peyarkkāraṇam yātu? olippāṉ. olippaṭuvāṉ, olippiṭam, olippumuṟai eṉṟa nāṉkuñ cērvatāl oliyaṉ piṟakkum. innāṉkum oliyaṉ piṟappukku vēṇṭum. “uṟṟavaṉtīrppāṉmaruntu uḻaic celvāṉ eṉṟu appāl nāṟkūṟṟē maruntu” eṉṟa kuṟaḷai oppunōkkuka uḷḷiruntu varuṅkāṟṟu varumvaḻiyil nā aṇṇam pal itaḻ mūkku mutalāṉa paṭuvuṟuppukkaḷait toṭutalum uṟutalu miṉṟit taṉṉaḷavil olikkumpōtu uyirpiṟakkum. meyyuṟupput tīṇṭaliṉṟi uyirppaḷavil piṟattaliṉ uyir eṉpatu kāraṇappeyar. uyir - oruvakaik kāṟṟu `kāṟṟaṭaitta paiyaṭā' eṉpa.
“paṉṉī ruyirum tannilai tiriyā
  miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icaikkum”
eṉṟa piṟappiyal nūṟpā uyirkaḷ meytīṇṭit tiriyātu miṭaṟṟiliruntu varum kāṟṟiyakattāṟ piṟakkum oliyaṉkaḷ eṉpataṉai veḷippaṭuttum.
meykkuṟi
(8)	ṉakāra viṟuvāyp
	patiṉeṇ ṇeḻuttum meyyeṉa moḻipa.
itu muppatu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ meyyeḻuttuk kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavurai
kakaramutal ṉakaram varai yuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭu oliyaṉkaḷum mey eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu peṟum eṉṟu moḻivar nūlōr.
vaḻakku
k ṅ c ñ ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ eṉap patiṉeṭṭām.
tiṟaṉurai
muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ ivaiyeṉat toṉṟutoṭṭu ellārum aṟiyappaṭṭa ulaka vaḻakkātaliṉ, `akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy' eṉavum `oḷakāraviṟuvāy' eṉavum `ṉakāraviṟuvāy' eṉavum vāḷā kūṟiṉār. oṉṟu mutal nūṟuvarai. oṉṟumutal pattuvarai eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkiṉai niṉaika.
muntunūl etaṉaiyum, muntunūlōr evaraiyum tolkāppiyam peyar cuṭṭavillai. eṉpa moḻipa, eṉmaṉār pulavar eṉmaṉār pulamaiyōrē, kaṭinilaiyiṉṟē āciriyaṟka eṉṟa naṭaipaṭat tolkāppiyattup paṉþṉūṟu iṭaṅkaḷil varum. ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭuvarum aṭippaṭṭa iruvakai vaḻakku marapukaḷ eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatē innaṭaiyiṉ karuttu. ataṉālaṉṟō eḻuvāy potuvākavuḷḷatu; mēlum pala nūṟpākkaḷ moḻipa, eṉpa eṉṟaḷavil eḻuvāyiṉṟiyum uḷa.
mutalnūṟpā kūṟum muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ uyir eṉavum mey eṉavum iru pakutippaṭum eṉṟu aṟivatōṭu, mūṉṟāvatu pakuti oṉṟu illai eṉavum teḷikiṉṟōm.
mey eṉpataṉ peyarkkāraṇam yātu? uḷḷiruntu varuṅkāṟṟu nā aṇṇam pal itaḻ mūkku mutalāṉa uṟuppukkaḷiṉ tacaippakutiyōṭu tacaippakuti toṭa oli piṟattaliṉ mey eṉappaṭum; ataṉāṟṟāṉ oṟṟu eṉṟa kuṟiyum meykku uṇṭu.
kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, uyireḻuttu, mey yeḻuttu, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉpavai iṉa muḻuvataiyum kuṟikkum tokutik kuṟiyīṭu kaḷākavum varum; a eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttu, ā eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttu, ya eṉṟa iṭaiyeḻuttu eṉṟāṟpōla avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaiccuṭṭum pakutik kuṟiyīṭukaḷākavum āḷappaṭum.
uyiriṉ nilai
(9)	meyyō ṭiyaiyiṉum uyiriyal tiriyā
itu meyyoṭu cērntu olikkumpōtu uyiriṉ nilai kūṟum.
akalavurai
mutaleḻuttu uyir eṉa mey eṉa iruvakaippaṭum eṉṟu mēlē kūṟiṉār. avai tammuṭ cērntu varum; meymmēl uyir ēṟivarum. taṭaippaṭṭup piṟakkum meyyoliyaṉoṭu cērntiruntālum uyir taṉṉiyalpiṉ paṭi uṟupput toṭātu kāṟṟaḷavil olikkum. ittaṉmai yiliruntum māṟupāṭillai. itaṉāl nām aṟivatu eṉṉa? uyirmey eṉṟa kūṭṭuṟavil uyirō meyyō taṉ piṟappiṉ muṟaiyaiyum taṉpeyark kāraṇattaiyum iḻappatillai.
uyiriyal - uyirāṉatu meyyoṭupaṭātu piṟakkum vaḷinilai.
tiṟaṉurai
collāṅkuṅkāl uyirum meyyumākiya muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ nilai eṉṉa? ivai taṉittaṉiyē niṟpatillai. uyirmeyyāka iṇaintē varum.
uyirmoḻikku mutalil maṭṭum taṉittu niṟkum avvaḷavē.
kāṇka: aṉpu, āṇ, icai.
mutalallāta iṭai iṟutiyellām meyyoṭu kūṭittāṉ niṟka vēṇṭum.
kāṇka: aṟam, aṭavi, aṟuvaṭai
moḻimutalāṉa coṟkaḷiluṅkūṭa meykkuppiṉṉē tāṉ uyirvarum.
kāṇka: kacaṭu, kācu, kuṟippu, kūṭṭuṟavu
coṟkaḷil ivvāṟu meykaḷōṭu kūṭi varuvatē uyirkaḷiṉ veḷippāṭu.  ātaliṉ `meyyōṭu iyaiyiṉum' eṉṟu uyirmēl vaittu nūṟpittār āciriyar.
meykkō ellāviṭattum uyirōṭu cērntutāṉ veḷippaṭum eṉṟa nilaiyillai. moḻimutalil maṭṭum veḷippāṭṭiṟkumeykaḷ uyirkaḷōṭu kūṭi uyir meykaḷāka niṟkum. kāṇka: paṭu, pāṭu, piṭi, pīṭu, itaṉai, “uyir meyyallaṉa moḻi mutalākā” eṉṟa nūṟpā teḷikkum. ēṉaip piṉṉiṭaṅkaḷil ellām mey uyirōṭu kalantum varum. taṉittum varum. kāṇka: tolkāppiyaṉ vaḷḷuvar, paccaiyappar ātaliṉ mey uyirōṭu iyaintu varutal peritillai. meyyōṭu uyir iyaintu varutalē peritu eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu innūṟpāvāl viḷaṅkum.
pulāluṇṇā oruvar pulāluṇ oruvaraik kātalittuk kūṭiṉālum, uṇṇāta tannilaiyiliruntu māṟār eṉṟa uvamaiyai innūṟpāviṟku ēṟṟit teḷika.
meykku māttirai
(10)	meyyi ṉaḷapē araiyeṉa moḻipa
itu meykku māttiraiyaḷavu kūṟum.
akalavurai
muppatu eḻuttuḷ oruvakaiyāṉa uyirukku mēlē māttirai kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṉoru vakaiyāṉa meykku māttirai araiyākum eṉṟu moḻivar nūlōr. ovvoru meyyum araimāttirai peṟum eṉpatu karuttu.
tiṟaṉurai
uyir vakaiyil māttirai kāraṇamākak kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa iru pakutiyuṇṭu. valliṉam melliṉam iṭaiyiṉam eṉṟu mey pirivu paṭṭālum māttirai kāraṇa māka vēṟṟumaiyiṉmai teḷiyalām. maṉitaṉ āṟaṟivuṭaiyaṉ eṉṟāl, ovvoruvaṉum āṟaṟivuṭaiyāṉ eṉṟu naṭaip poruḷpaṭumaṉṟō. atupōl meymmāttirai arai eṉpatu ovvoru meyyum araimāttirai eṉap poruḷpaṭum.
tūymai, iṉpam, tamiḻ eṉa uyirmeyyāka iruntālum, oṟṟāka iruntālum ennilaiyilum meykka arai māttirai uṇṭu.
cārpukku māttirai
(11) 	avviyal nilaiyum ēṉai mūṉṟē
itu cārpeḻuttukku māttirai kūṟum.
akalavurai
muppatu eḻuttukku uyir vakaiyākavum meyvakai yākavum māttirai kūṟiṉār. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa muppatu eḻuttuḷ aṭaṅkāta ēṉai mūṉṟum meyyeḻuttup pōla araimāttirai peṟṟu niṟkum.
vaḻakku:  kēṇmiyā. pāṭu. eḵku.
tiṟaṉurai
meyyum ēṉai mūṉṟum araiyaḷavu peṟum eṉṟu orē nūṟpāvākak kūṟiyirukkalāmē eṉiṉ, mey uyirōṭu otta mutaleḻuttātaliṉ taṉiyē muṉpākak kūṟiṉār. mey eḻuttām eṉavum ēṉai mūṉṟum `eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉavum colliya vēṟṟumaiyai yuṇarka.
āṉ īṉ eṉpaṉapōla ēṉ eṉpatuvum iṭam paṟṟiya aṭiccol. itu eñciyataik kuṟikkum. ēṉai, ēṉaiya, ēṉatu, ēṉavai, ēṉōr eṉṟellām varutal kāṇka.
makarakkuṟukkamum vaṭivum
(12)	araiyaḷapu kuṟukal makara muṭaittē,
	icaiyiṭaṉ arukun teriyuṅ kālai
	uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.
itu meyyeḻuttuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttiraik kuṟaivum ataṟkuriya varivaṭivum kūṟum.

akalavurai
meyyeḻuttukku māttirai araiyeṉṟu muṉpu kaṟṟōm. anta araimāttirai pātiyaḷavākak kuṟukutal - atāvatu kāl māttiraiyākak kuṟaital makarameykku maṭṭum uṇṭu. ārāyntu pārttāl ikkuṟukkam oli nerukkaṭiyil aritāka varum. arai māttiraiyāka irukkum makarameykkum kāl māttiraiyāka olikkum makarak kuṟukkattiṟkum varivaṭivil orē oru vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. iyalpāṉa makarameykku uriyavaṭiviṉmēl oru puḷḷi irukkum. kuṟukiya makara meykkō uriyavaṭiviṉ uḷḷē appuḷḷi irukkavēṇṭum. eṉavē puḷḷi mēliruntāl kuṟukāta iyalpu makaram; puḷḷi uḷḷēyiruntāl kuṟukiya makaram.
vaḻakku:	iyalpāṉa makaram, arai māttirai, mēṟpuḷḷi -m makarakkuṟukkam, kālmāttirai, uṭpuḷḷi - ma
	pōṉma - orumoḻiyil makarakkuṟukkam; ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil maṭṭum varum.
	varuma vaṇṭi - irumoḻippuṇarcciyil makarakkuṟukkam; ulaka vaḻakkilum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum varum.
tiṟaṉurai:	makaram kuṟukumiṭaṉ iraṇṭu:
	“ceyyu ḷiṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum”
	“ṉakārai muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum”
eṉa moḻi marapil irunūṟpākkaḷ uḷa.
itu pōṉma eṉṟa oru collaḷavil atuvum ceyyuḷiṟṟāṉ kāṇappaṭum.
“vakāra micaiyum makāraṅ kuṟukum”
eṉpatupuḷḷi mayaṅkiyalil varum ēṉaiyiṭam. itu vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potu. irumoḻippuṇarcciyil nilaimoḻi makaram varumoḻi vakarattiṉmuṉ māttirai kuṟukum.
(kāṇka: taruma vaḷḷal, paṟakkuma vaṇṭu, collum vāy)
`icaiyiṭaṉ arukum teriyuṅkālai' eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṟ colliyatait terivikkum mukattāṉ. moḻimarapilum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalilum uriya iṭaṅkaḷaic cuṭṭiṉār tolkāppiyar. ataṉālaṉṟō `mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi' eṉṟu pāyiram tolkāppiya nūṉeṟiyaip pārāṭṭukiṉṟatu. inneṟi tāṉē teḷivittal eṉappaṭum.
araiyaḷavu kuṟukal eṉpatu iruvakaiyil oru poruḷē koḷḷa iṭaṉuṇṭu. meykkukkūṟiya arai māttiraiyaṉiṉṟu kuṟukutal eṉṟum urai ceyyalām. ataṉōṭu araiyaḷavākak kuṟukutal eṉṟum urai ceyyalām. oru kallil irumāṅkāy vīḻttum naṭai itu. tamiḻilakkaṇakkattil oṉṟaṉ kuṟukkamāvatu cempākam kuṟaitalām.
`teriyuṅkālai' eṉpataṉai `uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvākummē' eṉpataṉōṭum iyaittu muṭikkalām. ārāyumiṭattu puḷḷai niṟkum iṭavēṟṟumai yāṉum makarakkuṟukkam teḷivākum eṉpatu karuttu.
tamiḻ vaṭivaṉkaḷuḷ puḷḷikku vēṟupāṭu terikkum ciṟappāṟṟal uṇṭu. āytattaic cuṭṭumpōtu naṭuvilum, makarak kuṟukkattiṟku uḷḷēyum, meym mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mēlēyum irukkum.
makarak kuṟukkattiṉ vaṭivaṉ piṟkālattu vaḻakku viḻuntatu.
meykkup potuvaṭivaṉ
(13)	meyyi ṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭum nilaiyal
itu meyyeḻuttukkup potu vaṭivu kūṟum.
akalavurai
meykaḷ patiṉeṭṭaṉṟō ovvoru meykkum taṉi vaṭivu uṇṭu. itu urivaṭivaṉ eṉappaṭum. ataṉmēl mey eṉṟa iṉaṅkāṭṭap puḷḷi eṉṟa oru potu vaṭivaṉum irukkum. iru vaṭivum cērntatutāṉ meyyiṉ uru.
iyaṟkai - iyalpu, taṉmai. nilaiyal - niṟka.
vaḻakku: k ṅ c ña ṭ ṇ t n p m y r l v ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ

tiṟaṉurai
meypuḷḷiyaṭaiyatu eṉṟa naṭaimāṭu kompuṭaiyatu eṉṟa naṭaiyai okkum. kompuṭaiyateṉavē talaimēl eṉpatu peṟappaṭumāṟu pōla, puḷḷiyuṭaiyateṉavē uri vaṭivattiṉ mēl eṉpatu peṟappaṭum veṟum puḷḷi meyyākātu. puḷḷiyoṭum nilaiyil eṉṟa ummaiyāl, puḷḷiyum meykku iṉṟiyamaiyā vaṭivam eṉpatu uṟutiyām. puḷḷi nīṅkiṉ meyyiṉ taṉmaiyum nīṅkiviṭum.
oruviṉā: meykaṭku urivaṭivaṉkaḷ irukkumpōtu mēlum oru puḷḷip potuvaṭivaṉ ēṉ? ivvaṭikaṉ iṉṟēl kaṅacaña mutalāyiṉa akaram ēṟiya uyirmeyyāki viṭum. iyaṟkaiyāṉa taṉimeykkum akaram ūrnta uyir meykkum vēṟṟumai terivippatu taṉimey puḷḷi peṟukai yākum. eṉavē puḷḷip pēṟṟiṉ payaṉ otta vaṭiviṉil; vēṟṟumait teḷivu ceyvatu. muṉcoṉṉa uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyum piṉṉē collappaṭum ekara okarappuḷḷiyum ippayaṉē koṇṭavai.
māttiraic curukkattukkup puḷḷiyiṭal eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu peritum poruntātu. avvāṟāyiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttai nōkkak kuṟṟeḻuttellām puḷḷipeṟutal vēṇṭum. aḷapeṭaiyai nōkka neṭṭeḻuttup puḷḷi peṟutal vēṇṭum. ekaramum okaramum puḷḷi peṟukai māttiraik kuṟukkattālaṉṟu. avai ōraḷapu icaikkum iyaṟkaik kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ īraḷapicaikkum ēkāra ōkārattōṭu vaṭivaṉ vēṟṟumai terikkavē ekaramum ōkaramum puḷḷi peṟalāyiṉa. āytam etaṉ kuṟukkam? araimāttiraiyuṭaiya mey etaṉ kuṟukkam? atu nilaiyāṉa puḷḷipeṟutaliṉ vaṭivaṉ vēṟṟumai teḷippatu puḷḷippayaṉ eṉpatu kaṇkūṭu. puḷḷiṭaiya ōr eḻuttu māttirai kuṟaivākavum irukkalām; ataṉait terikkap puḷḷi kuttappeṟṟatu eṉṟāl niṟaiyāṉa vitiyaṉṟu. oliyaṉkaḷiṉ urivaṭivaṉkaḷ ottaṉavāka irukkum nilaiyil, vēṟṟumaippaṭutta puḷḷikkoṭai vēṇṭappaṭum. itu tamiḻ vaṭiva neṟi.
meyppuḷḷi iṉṟum uṇṭu. eṉṟum vēṇṭum. itu mēṟpuḷḷiyākum.
	 puḷḷipeṟal
(14)	e	ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyu maṟṟē
itu kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekara okara vaṭivaṉ kūṟum
akalavurai
kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekara voliyaṉum okara voliyaṉum mēṟcolliyappu urivaṭivattiṉmēl puḷḷi peṟṟē niṟkum iyalpiṉa.
vaḻakku: e ō
tiṟaṉurai
`meyyiṉiyaṟkai' eṉṟatupōla `ekara vokarattiyaṟkai' eṉpataṉāl meypōla ivaiyum puḷḷiyoṭu niṟṟal toṉmarapu eṉpatu teḷivām. moḻivaralāṟṟil oliyaṉkaḷ mika muṉ eḻuntavai. vaṭivaṉkaḷ mikap piṉtōṉṟiyavai. ataṉmēlum oppunōkkiṉ tamiḻmoḻi pallūḻit toṉmai, cemmai, iyaṟkai, nilaipēṟu uṭaiyatām. ātaliṉ piṟamoḻikaḷaic cārttit tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ oliyaṉ vaṭivaṉkaḷaik kaṇippatu aṟivuṭaimai yākātu.
aṇmaikkāla āyvukaḷ tamiḻiṉ mututoṉmaiyai uṇarntu ulakattup palapiṟa moḻikaḷait tūkkip pārkkum naṉṉaṭaiyaip pulappaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl paṇṭutoṭṭē ulakap paṉmoḻikaṭkut tamiḻtāyākavō ceviliyākavō vaḻaṅkiya ūṭṭam veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu. innaṟceyti virippiṟ perukiyōṭumātaliṉ, āṅkāṅku vēṇṭumaḷavu vaḻikkalpōṟ kāṭṭappaṭum.
oruviṉā: a i u eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ puḷḷipeṟa villaiyē : e o iraṇṭu maṭṭum puḷḷi peṟuvatu ēṉ? a i u eṉṟa kuṟṟeḻuttukkum ā ī ū eṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttukkum tolkāppiyar kālattum vaṭivaṉkaḷ teḷivākat tammaḷavil vēṟṟumaippaṭṭiruntaṉa.
iccikkalil aritāṉa oṉṟiṉai āḻamāka nām niṉaiya vēṇṭum. neṭṭeḻuttukku e o vaṭivaṉkaḷ iruntapōtē kuṟṟeḻuttukkum puḷḷiyoṭu kūṭiya e o vaṭivaṉkaḷ iruntaṉa. kuḻappam vantu mayaṅki vēṟṟumaip paṭuttuvāṉ ippuḷḷikaḷ piṉpu vaikkappaṭṭaṉavalla eṉpataṉai naṉku purintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. muppatu mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varivaṭiva vēṟṟumaiyaik kūrntu pārppiṉ māṟṟam ciṟiya kōṭākavō ciṟiya vaḷaivākavōtāṉ irukkum. eṉiṉum ivvēṟṟumaik kūṟukaḷ uṭaṉkālattuk kaṇṭavai. makarak kuṟukkappuḷḷiyum meyppuḷḷiyum ekara vokarap puḷḷiyum kuḻappam vantu vēṟṟumai kāṭṭa vēṇṭip piṉcērntavai yalla eṉpatu mutaṉmaiyāṉa karuttu.
kōṭu, vaḷaivu pōlap puḷḷiyum tamiḻ vaṭivaṉkaḷil ōr kūṟu avvāṟāyiṉ puḷḷiyai maṭṭum vitantu nūṟpākkaḷ ceyvataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa? nalla viṉā. muppatu oliyaṉkaḷum paṇṭum ovvōreḻuttālāṉavai. aḵtāvatu ōreḻuttai eṭutteḻutavēṇṭum eṉpatillai. eḻutteṉappaṭupa muppaḵtu eṉpa eṉṟa naṭaiyil, varivaṭivām eḻuttu muppatuvē eṉṟa kuṟippup peṟappaṭum. pattu nūṟu āyiram kuṟitta tamiḻeṇkaḷ (ya, ḷa, ta)ōreḻuttāka iruppatai uṇarka. immuppatu eḻuttukkaḷuḷ oru cāraṉa puḷḷiyum peṟuvaṉavāka iruntaṉa. eḻuttāṇiyai eṭutteḻutāmal puḷḷi vaikka muṭiyātaṉṟō? iṉṟaiya nilaiyum atutāṉē. mēlum eḻutum vēkattil puḷḷi viṭappaṭutalum uṇṭaṉṟō? iṉṟum innilaiyuṇṭu. eṭutteḻutum amaippai niṉaintu viṭṭeḻutak kūṭāta uṟuppu puḷḷiyeṉpataic cuṭṭik kāṭṭavum puḷḷi kuṟitta nūṟpākkaḷai yāttār tol kāppiyar eṉa aṟika. meyyiṉ iyaṟkai ekara vokarat tiyaṟkai eṉṟa collāṭciyālum ivvuṇmai pōtarum.
ekara okarattiyaṟkai eṉpataṉāl ippuḷḷikaḷ uyirk kuṟilukkē. (kāṇka: eṭu, oṭu) meyyiṉ iyaṟkai eṉpatu uyirmeyyaik kuṟikkātatupōla itaṉaiyum koḷvōm. ēri puḷḷi peṟiṉ eri eṉṟa neruppaik kuṟikkum; ōti puḷḷi peṟiṉ oti eṉṟa marattaik kuṟikkum eṉṟa ceyyuḷ mēṟkōḷḷilum uyirkkuṟilē puḷḷipeṟalai oppiṭuka.
ekara okaram puḷḷiyiṉṟi eḻutappaṭuvatum ēkāram kīḻppaṭukkaikkōṭu peṟuvatu; ōkāram kīḻkkātu peṟuvatum aṇmaiyai vaḻakku.
uyirmey vaṭivaṉkaḷ
(15) 	puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum
	uruvuru vāki akaramō ṭuyirttalum
	ēṉai yuyirō ṭuruvutirin tuyirttalum
	āyī riyala vuyirtta lāṟē.
itu patiṉeṭṭu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiroṭum cērum uyirmeykaṭku vaṭivaṉ muṟaikaḷaik kūṟum.
akalavurai
puḷḷiyoṭu niṟkum meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ (kṅcñ) muṉṉē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. appuḷḷikaḷ illāta meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ (kaṅacaña) avvaṭivē vaṭivāka akaram ēṟiya uyirmeyyāka olikkappaṭum. iḵtu orumuṟai. puḷḷiyillāta vaṭivaṉkaḷ ā mutalāṉa patiṉoru uyirkaḷōṭu cērumpōtu tam urivaṭivu palavāṟu tirintu olikkum. iḵtu orumuṟai. patiṉeṇ meyyum paṉṉiruyiroṭum cērntu uyirmeyyākuṅkāl puḷḷi nīṅkiya meyvaṭivattiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil ivviru muṟaiyil uyirmeykaḷiṉ tiri vaṭivaṉkaḷ tōṉṟum.
urupu - vaṭivu. uyirttal - olittal. āṟu - neṟi.
vaḻakku: irumuṟaikaḷāvaṉa:
1. akaravuyirmeykkup puḷḷiyillāta meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ:
	ka ṅa ca  ña ṭa ṇa ta na pa ma ya ra la va ḻa ḷa ṟa ṉa
2. ākāra mutalāṉa ēṉai uyirmeykaṭkup puḷḷi yillāta meyvaṭivaṉkaḷ palvēṟu tiripupaṭutal:
	kā ki kī ku kū ke kē kai ko kō kau
ivai ikkāla vaṭivaṉkaḷ
uyirmeyyil varum tirikuṟikaḷ
ā	-	pakkattuk kāl vāṅkutal: h - kā - ṅā cā iṉṉa piṟa (15)
	-	kīḻvilaṅku iṭamāka vaḷaital : i    	 - ṇā ṟā ṉā (3)
i	-	kokki mēlē peṟutal : 	 - ki ṅi ci iṉṉa piṟa (18)
ī	-	ciṟitu nīṇṭa mēl vaḷaivuccuḻi peṟuvaṉa 		-  kī ṅī cī iṉṉa piṟa 	(18)
u	-	kīḻvilaṅku iṭamāka vaḷaivu peṟuvaṉa - 		 - ku, ṭu, mu, ru, ḻu	, ḷu (6)
	-	kīḻvilaṅku cuḻittu nērkōṭu peṟuvaṉa: 	 - nu, ṇu,tu, nu, lu, ṟu, ṉu 	(7)
	- 	ciṟukōṭu kīḻē iḻuppu: 1 - cu pu yu vu (5)
ū	-	talaitūkkiya paṭukkaikkōṭu - u - kū (1)
	-	kīḻiḻuppum iṭamākac cuḻiyum :       	-  ṅū, cū, ṭū, pū, mū, ṟā, rū, vū, ḻū, 		ḷū (10)
	-	kīḻvilaṅkuc cuḻippum kāl vāṅkalum :	 nū - ñā ṇū, tū, nū, lū, 		ṉū, ṟū, þṉū (7/)
e	-	iṭappakkam oru cuḻikkompu : b - ke, ṅe, ce iṉṉa piṟa (18)
ē	-	iṭappakkam iru cuḻikkātu: n - kē, ṅē, cē iṉṉa piṟa(18)
ai	-	iṭappakkam iṇaippukkompu : i - kai, ṅai, cai, iṉṉa piṟa(14)
	-	iṭappakkam cērnta yāṉaikkai: -?, ?ṇa, ?la, ?ḷa, ?ṉa(4)
o	-	iṭappakkak kompum valappakkak kālum : b-h - ko, ṅo, co, iṉṉa		 ṟa (15)
	-	iṭappakkak kompum kīḻ vilaṅku iṭamāka vaḷaivum: b	 - b‚, bƒ, b„ (3)
ō	-	iṭappakkam irucuḻikkātum valappakkam kālum : n - kō, ṅō, cō 	iṉṉa piṟa (15)
		iṭappakkam iru cuḻikkātum kīḻvilaṅku iṭamāka vaḷaivum: n		    - n‚, nƒ, n„. (3)
oḷa	-	iṭappakkam oru cuḻikkompum valappakkam kompu cērnta 	pātikkālum : b - ḷa- kau ṅau cau iṉṉa piṟa (18)
tiṟaṉurai
mēṟkāṭṭiya uyirmeyk kuṟikaḷ cila nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷākap peruvaḻakkil uḷḷavai. acciyalmuṟai naṉku kāl koṇṭa piṉ ellāmoḻikkum pōlat tamiḻmoḻikkum eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ cemmaiyeytiṉa kaiyeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟkūṭa vēṟupāṭukaḷ kaṟaintu varukiṉṟaṉa. vaṭṭāra vaḻakkup pōlavum kuḻūukkuṟi pōlavum vaṭiva vaḻakkilum cila vēṟupāṭukaḷ tavirkka muṭiyātavai. eṉiṉum accuruvaṅkaḷ pallāṇṭukaḷākac cevvuruvam āyiṉa. aṇmaiyil tamiḻaka aracāṇaiyiṉpaṭi uyirmeyyil ākārattilum aikārattilum okarattilum ōkārattilum patiṉmūṉṟu tiruttaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṇai yeḻuttukkaḷ paḷḷiyilum aracu viṉaiyaḷavilum parappappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittiruttaṅkaḷil peruṅkūṟukaḷ muṉpē iruntu piṉpu teḷivum eḷimaiyum karuti māṟiyavai eṉṟu varalāṟu aṟital nallatu. aracāṇaiyiṭṭa periyār eḻuttukkaḷāvaṉa: ṇā ṟā ṉā; ṇai lai ḷai ṉai; ṇo ṟo ṉo; ṉō ṟō ṉō.
eḻuttuvaṭivaṅkaḷ kālakkōṭpaṭutalum karuvikkōṭ paṭutalum kaikkōṭpaṭutalum uṇmaiyāṉum, oliyaṉ poruḷoṭu nēr toṭarpu iṉmaiyāṉum iṉṉapiṟa kāraṇattāṉum tolkāppiyam uyirvaṭivaṉkaḷaiyum mey vaṭivaṉkaḷaiyum viḷampavillai. vēṟṟumait terivukku vēṇṭiyavaḷavē naṉi cila nūṟpākkaḷil atuvum puḷḷi paṟṟiya eḻutturu viṉaik kūṟiyuḷḷatu. iṭaikkālattu eḻunta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum tolkāppiyattaip piṉpaṟṟic cila uriyuruvaṅkaḷaiyē toṭṭuccelkiṉṟaṉa. accuppativuc cemmai vanta iññāṉṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷ orunilai vārppurup peṟṟaṉavātaliṉ pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil aṟuvakai yilakkaṇam kaṇṭa taṇṭapāṇi cuvāmikaḷ uyirkkum meykkum uyirmeykkum ellām taṉittaṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷai virivupaṭap puṉaintu nūṟpāviṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. itu niṟka.
`puḷḷiyillā ella meyyum' eṉṟa innūṟpāvil uyirmey vaṭivaṉkaḷai iruvakaiyuḷ aṭakkik kāṭṭuvar tolkāppiyar. uyirmey akarattiṟku maṭṭum oruvakai; ēṉaiya patiṉoṉṟukkumākap piṟitoruvakai. mey puḷḷi nīṅki viṭṭāṟ pōtum, ka ṅa mutalāṉa uyirmeyyākiviṭum. iṅku puḷḷi nīkkam eṉṟa aḷavil eḷiya vilakku muṟaiyākum. ēṉaiyuyirōṭu urupu tirintu uyirttal eṉṟu curuṅka moḻintamaiyāl, ā mutaliyaṉa cērumpōtu varum tiripukaḷ orupāṟ paṭātaṉa eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. uyireḻuttukkuriya paṉṉiraṇṭu taṉivaṭivaṉkaḷum uyirmeykaḷākumpōtu ciṟitum cērakkāṇōm. meykkuriya vaṭivaṅkaḷō eṉiṉ naṭuvurut tiripiṉṟiyē uyir meyyākum. nilaip pōkkaik kāṇalām. itaṉālaṉṟō `ellāmeyyum' eṉat tiripil meyvaṭiviṉ mēl vaittu uyirmeykaṭku vaṭivam cuṭṭukiṉṟār tolkāppiyar. ka kā ki kī ku kū eṉṟāṅku iṉṟuvarai meyvaṭivamē naṭunāyakamāka niṉṟu nilavum orumai varavai uṇarka.
uyirmey nilai
(16)	meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟa nilaiyē.
ituuyirmeyyil uyiriṭam kūṟum.
akalavurai
mēlainūṟpāvil uyirummeyyum kalantu uyir meyyākuṅkāl varum vaṭivat tiripukaḷ maṭṭum iruvakaiyākac cuṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. uyirmey eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭil uyir mutalil uḷḷatu. “paṉṉīreḻuttum uyireṉa moḻipa” eṉṟataṉ piṉpē “patiṉeṇṇeḻuttum meyyeṉa moḻipa” eṉac collappaṭṭatu. ātaliṉ uyirmeyk kalappil uyirē muṉṉolikkum eṉṟu karutappaṭuvatu iyalpu itaṉait terivikkavē avvāṟillai: meyyolimuṉ tōṉṟum. ataṉ piṉṉarē uyiroli tōṉṟum eṉṟu tamiḻoli muṟai kūṟuvar āciriyar. aiyaṅkaḷaital innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ.
vaḻakku: ka kā ki kī ku kū iṉṉapiṟa.
tiṟaṉurai
uyirmey - uyiroṭu kūṭiya mey, tayirvaṭai eṉpatu pōla. vēṟṟumaittokai nilai cilaviṭaṅkaḷil uyirum meyyum eṉa ummait tokaiyākavum varum. iravupakal eṉpatu pōla. uyirmeyk kalappil etumuṉ etupiṉ eṉṟu teḷivippataṟku oru kāraṇam uṇṭu. mēlainūṟpāvil `ēṉaiyuyirōṭu uruvu tirintu niṟkum' eṉṟār. aṅṅaṉam niṟkum tiripu vaṭivaṅkaḷ cila muṉ ciṟkum. (kāṇka: ke kē kai); cila piṉṉēvarum (kāṇka: kā ki kī ku kū ) iṉṉuñ¨cila muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟkum kāṇka: ko kō kau) iṅṅaṉam uyirmeyyil tirikuṟi muṉṉum piṉṉum varutaliṉ, tolkāppiyak kālattum innilai iruntirukku mātaliṉ, kuṟi niṟkum varicaiyaṉṟu olitōṉṟum nilai eṉṟu teḷivu ceyvar tolkāppiyar.
oru viṉā: uyirmey vaṭivil meyyoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉum iruttaliṉ niṉṟa muṟaippaṭi `meyyuyir' eṉṟē kuṟiyīṭu ceytirukkalāmē? vaṭivukkāka avvāṟum irukkalām; eṉiṉum uyirmey eṉpatu mutaleḻuttu varicaiyātaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam moḻintār. `oṟṟu muṉṉāy varum uyirmeyyē' eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār itaṉaiyē piṉpaṟṟuvar. āṇ peṇ, iravu pakal, tāy tantai eṉṟa vaḻakkumuṟaikaḷaiyum niṉaika.


meym mūviṉam
(17)	 valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa.
(18) 	melleḻut teṉpa 'ñaṅaṇa namaṉa.
(19)	iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa
ivai meyyeḻuttiṉ mūviṉaṅkaḷaiyum kūṟum.
akalavurai
kuṟil neṭileṉṟu paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum iruvakaippaṭṭataṉṟē. atupōl valliṉam melliṉam iṭaiyiṉam eṉappatiṉeṭṭu meyyeḻuttum āṟu āṟu āṟāka mūvakaippaṭum eṉṟu colluvār nūlōr.
valleḻuttu meykaḷ - k c ṭ t p ṟ
melleḻuttu meykaḷ - ñ ṅ ¨ ṇ n m ṉ
iṭaiyeḻuttu meyykaḷ - y r l v ḻ ḷ
tiṟaṉurai
vallāṟu, vaṉṟoṭar, valloṟṟu, valittal, valippu, vallicai, vaṉmai eṉavum; meṉṟoṭar, melloṟṟu, melittal, melippu, mellicai, meṉmai eṉavum; iṭait toṭar, iṭaiyoṟṟu, iṭaimai eṉavum immūviṉamum āṭcipeṟum.
muṉṉiraṇṭum valittal, melittal eṉa viṉaiyāka varum. iṭaiccollukku viṉaiyāntaṉmai yillai. iṅku mūviṉa meykaḷaiyum ka ṅa ya eṉṟāṅku akaram paṭak kūṟiṉālum ivai oṟṟukkaḷēyām. “meyyiṉiyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum” eṉṟa tolkāppiya vitippaṭi, akaravoliyaṉākak koḷḷāmal akara vōcaiyākak koḷḷavēṇṭum. `puḷḷiyillā ellā meyyum' eṉṟu muṉṉē akaravuyir meyyākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉavē. avaiyalla ivai eṉṟu teḷika.
vāyvaḻik kāṟṟu mikka aṭaippālum uṟupputtacait taṭaiyālum piṟappatu valliṉam. vaṉmai pōlavē uṟupput taṭaippaṭṭu mūkkiṉvaḻik kāṟṟōṭṭattāl piṟappatu melliṉam. kāṟṟum uṟuppum mikka taṭaiyiṉṟi vaṉmai yuṟāmalum meṉmaip paṭāmalum piṟappatu iṭaiyiṉam. itaṟku ōraḷavu uyirttaṉmaiyum uṇṭu. iṭaiyeṉpatu orupakkam cārāta nekiḻ vicaiyaik kuṟikkum. `akalātu aṇukātu tīkkāyvārpōla eṉpatai oruvāṟu oppu nōkkuka. ceyyuḷiyalil.
vallicai vaṇṇam valleḻuttu mikumē
mellicai vaṇṇam melleḻuttu mikumē
iyaipu vaṇṇam iṭaiyeḻuttu mikumē
eṉṟa vaṇṇa nūṟpākkaḷil iyaipu eṉṟa col iṭai eṉpataṟku nikarāka āḷappaṭum. ivvāṭciyai nōkkiṉ vallolip piṟappup kūṟum. mellolip piṟappuk kūṟum iyainta nilai iṭaiyeḻuttām eṉṟu uṇaralām. peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum mey taṭumāṟi varum collai iṭaiccol eṉṟu ilakkaṇam kūṟumaṉṟō? oppiṭṭuk kāṇka. mēlum iṭaiyeḻuttukkaḷ ceyyuḷil āciṭai yetukait toṭaiyāka vantu iyaitalaiyum eṇṇuvōm.
valleḻuttum melleḻuttum vaḷivēṟṟumai yiruppiṉum uṟupputtaṭaiyāl iṉamāka oṟṟumaippaṭṭaṉa eṉiṉum poruṇmai nilaiyil ovvoṉṟum mutaleḻut tām eṉṟa oliyaṉiyalai maṟavātu koḷḷavēṇṭum. ōraḷavu īraḷavu eṉṟa māttirai vēṟṟumaiyāl uyirirupāṟpaṭṭatu. meykaḷukkuḷ māttirai vēṟṟumaiyillai. vaḷiyiṉ ticaiyāṉum uṟupput tīṇṭum muṟaiyāṉum mey mūviṉamāyiṟṟu.
poruṇmai matippil muppatu oliyaṉkaḷum taṉip peṟṟiyaṉa eṉpatu muṭinta teḷivu. oṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟu aṭaṅkātātaliṉ ovvoṉṟaiyum taṉiyākac cuṭṭuvatutāṉ matippiyal. eṉiṉum vaḻakkeḷimaiyum kaṟpōr niṉai vāṟṟalum tokainalamum āṭcit teḷivum puṇarccip potumaiyum nōkki uyirāka meyyākak kuṟilāka neṭilāka vaṉmaiyāka meṉmaiyāka iṭaimaiyāka ellām iṉaṅkaḷāyiṉa.
maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum
nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē
eṉṟa tolkāppiya iṟuti nūṟpā iṉaneṟi kūṟal niṉaiyattakum.
meym mayakkaṅkaḷ
(20)	ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉilai teriyuṅ kālai
	ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉark
	ka ca pa veṉṉum mūveḻut turiya.
itumutal eṭṭu nūṟpākkaḷ meymmayakkam kuṟitta varampukaḷ kūṟuvaṉa. iḵtu ōroṟṟuṭaṉilai eṉappaṭum.
akalavurai
muṉṉar - piṉpu, aṭuttu, uriya - uriyaṉa.
mayakkam - kalattal. oṉṟōṭoṉṟum aṭuttu niṟṟal eṉpatu poruḷ.
meymmayakkam - iṉṉameyyeḻuttukkuppiṉ iṉṉa meyyeḻuttu varutal : meyyum meyyum iṇaital.
patiṉeṭṭu meyyum tamiḻ oliyaṉkaḷāyiṉum aṭuttaṭuttu niṟkum uṟavil varaiyaṟaiyuṇṭu aṭuttu niṟkattakum meyyai naṭpumey eṉavum niṟkak takā meyyaip pakaimey eṉavum koḷvōm. itaṉai mūṉṟu vakaippaṭuttalām.
1. 	taṉmeykkuppiṉ taṉatē 	-	itu taṉatē 				varum uppu (k c t p - 4)		mayakkam 
2.	orumeykkuppiṉ piṟitē varum	-	itu piritē mayakkam			vāḻka (rḻ-2)	
3.	taṉmeykkuppiṉ taṉatum kaṉṉi	-	itu taṉatoṭum			varum allatu piṟitum varum kaṉṟu 		mayakkam piṟitoṭum			(ṭ ṟ ṅñṇ n m ṉ y l v ḷ - 12)		mayakkam	
meyyōṭu meykalakkum immayakkattil mutalmey puḷḷiyuṭaiya taṉimeyyākavē irukkum; aṭuttu varumey uyirmeyyāka irukkum eṉiṉum uyirmeyyil mutaṟkaṇ olippatu meyyēyaṉṟō ātaliṉ meymmayakkamākak koḷḷappaṭum.
urai: mēṟkūṟiya patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷum makkaḷiṉ pēccuvaḻakkil oru meyyōṭu iṉṉorumey cērum iṇainilaiyai viḷaṅkak kāṇpōmāṉāl  ṭ ṟ l ḷ eṉṟa puḷḷiyeḻuttu ovvoṉṟukkup piṉṉum ka ca pa eṉṟa mūṉṟu meykaḷum varuvataṟku uriyavai. itu piṟitoṭum mayakkam.
vaḻakku:	ka	ca	pa
ṭ	veṭkam	kāṭci	naṭpu
ṟ	toḻiṟkaḷam	toḻiṟcālai	toḻiṟpayiṟci
l	elkai	palcuṉai	velpakai
ḷ	koḷkalam	koḷcāṭi	koḷpali
tiṟaṉurai
mayakkam colliṭai vaittuk kāṇappaṭumāyiṉum, taṉiyeḻuttu nilaiyilēyē iṉṉataṟkuppiṉ eṉṟu cuṭṭa muṭiyumātaliṉ, eḻuttuk kuṟitta nūṉmarapiyalil mayakkaṅ kūṟiṉār. oru moḻiyil eḻuttiṇaital eṉṟa mayakkattaip palavakaippaṭuttalāmeṉiṉum payaṉum varampum uṭaiya mayakkamē kūṟappaṭum. uyirmey ōreḻuttaṉṟu; ceykai naṭaimuṟaiyil uyirākavō meyyākavō taṉippaṭuttappaṭum ātaliṉ mayakkap pēccillai. meyyum uyirum iṇaivatu oruvakai eṉalāmāyiṉum, “uṭaṉmēl uyirvan toṉṟuva tiyalvē” eṉṟu naṉṉūl veḷippaṭuttiya paṭi, ituvum mayakkam eṉappaṭātu.
uyirmeyyum uyirmeyyum mayakkamāmō eṉiṉ uyirumeyyum eṉṟa iraṇṭē eḻuttiṉ aṭi vakaiyākak koḷḷappaṭumpōtu uyirmeym mayakkattiṟku iṭaṉillai.
uyiroṭu uyirum uyiroṭu meyyum mayaṅkutal uṇṭēyeṉiṉum, ivaiyellām etaṉaiyaṭutta etuvum varalāmātaliṉ, varampiṉmaikkum payaṉiṉmaikkum ilakkaṇappēṟillai. mūṭaiyariciyai eṇṇip payaṉeṉṉa? kuṭattil nīrttuḷi mayakkattaik kūṟip payaṉeṉṉa? ilakkaṇattiṉ payaṉ teḷivum kaṭaippiṭippumām. varampuṭaiyatē ilakkaṇa vaḷaivuḷ iṭampeṟum. eḻuttu mayakkattil meyyoṭu mey oṉṟiniṟkum mayakkamē tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ amaippaik kāṭṭuvatu. varampuṭaiyatu piṟaḻātu kaṭaippiṭikka vēṇṭiyatu. puṇarcciyilakkaṇat tiṟkum taḷamāvatu. ātaliṉ virivupaṭa eṭṭu nūṟpākkaḷ meymmayakkattiṟkē yāttār tolkāppiyar. iyaṟṟamiḻ kūṟum ivvilakkaṇattai icait tamiḻil varum iṇai kiḷai naṭpu pakai narampukaḷōṭu oppiṭuka.
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅku - makkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkil uḷḷatu eṉṟapaṭi. eṉavē ceyyuḷ vaḻakkukkum ituvē aṭippaṭaiyām meymmayaṅku uṭaṉilai eṉpataṉ viḷakkam: vāḻka eṉṟa collil ḻakaram puḷḷiyāka irukkum. `ka' uyir meyyāka irukkum. itu ōroṟṟuṭaṉilai eṉappaṭum. varumey uyirōṭu kūṭiniṟkum eṉpataic cuṭṭavē uṭaṉilai eṉṟār. ituvē poruḷ eṉpataṉai ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi eṉavum ka ca pa veṉṉum mūveḻuttu eṉavum kūṟuvatāl teḷivām. mēlum  ka ca pa eṉa akaravuyir mēlaṉavākak kūṟiṉālum kā cā yā ki ci pi eṉṟāṅku ellāvuyir meykkum koḷḷavēṇṭum. ivviḷakkam piṉvarum nūṟpākkaṭkum ēṟkum. iḵtu iṉantaḻuvutal eṉṉum neṟi.
oru viṉā: meymmayakkam orumoḻiyil varumā? moḻit toṭaril varumā?
payiṟci
kāṭci
velka		orumoḻiyil vantaṉa
koḷka	
kaṭaṟkarai	moḻittoṭaril vantaṉa
nāṭkāṭṭi
veṭṭum puli
pūṅkā		
orumoḻiyāyiṉum paṉmoḻiyāyiṉum īṇṭu olittoṭarcciyē ilakkaṇamākum. `iṉṉavolikkuppiṉ iṉṉavoli' eṉṟa aṇukkanilaiyē meymmayakkamām. piṉvarum puṇariyalkaḷ kiḷavit toṭarāyiṉum avaṟṟukkum immayakkamē ilakkaṇam eṉṟaṟika nūṉmarapu kūṟum eḻuttilakkaṇam puṇarcciyil māṟuvatillai.
ituvumatu
(21) 	avaṟṟuḷ,
	laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum
itu muṉcoṉṉavaṟṟuḷ irupuḷḷikaṭku mēlum mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavurai
mēṟcoṉṉa nāṉku puḷḷikaḷuḷ l ḷ eṉṟa irupuḷḷikaṭkuppiṉ yakara vakara eḻuttukaḷum varum. eṉavē ka ca pa ya va eṉṟa aintum mayaṅkum eṉpatu karuttu.
vaḻakku:
	   ya		     va
l - 	nalyāṉai	nalvaravu
ḷ - 	āḷyār	āḷvār
ituvumatu
(22) 	ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉumpuḷḷi muṉṉart
	tatta micaikaḷ ottaṉa nilaiyē
itu melliṉattukku iṉamāṉa valliṉattōṭu mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavurai
eḻuttu varicaiyil ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ eṉṟa melloṟṟukkaḷukku mēlē irukkum ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṟa valleḻuttukkaḷ iṉamuṟaippaṭi piṉṉē vantu niralē mayaṅkum.
ṅka: ¨ñca; ṇṭa; nta; mpa; ṉṟa eṉṟavāṟu iṇaintuvarum. micaikaḷ - mēlē niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ.
vaḻakku:	 ṅka	- 	vaṅkāḷam
¨	ñca	-	tañcai
	ṇṭa	-	ilaṇṭaṉ
	nta	-	intiyā
	mpa	-	pūmpukār
	ṉṟa	-	 kuṉṟakkuṭi
tiṟaṉurai
muppatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ varicai iṉṟu akarac cuvaṭiyiṟ kāṇpatu pōlavē tolkāppiyak kālattukku muṉpum iruntu vantatu. akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy, oḷakāraviṟuvāy, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu, valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭaiyeḻuttu mutaliya nūṟpākkaḷum, piṟappiyal uyir mayaṅkiyal puḷḷimayaṅkiyalil varum eḻuttu varicaikaḷum, `tattam micaikaḷ' eṉṟa innūṟpāvum `appatiṉoṉṟē puḷḷiyiṟuti’ eṉṟa moḻimarapu nūṟpāvum `ñakāra mutalā ṉakāra  vīṟṟup puḷḷiyiṟuti iyaipu' eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal nūṟpāvum tamiḻeḻuttu māṟāta varalāṟṟorumaikkuc cāṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
ituvumatu
(23) 	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
	kacañapa mayavav vēḻum uriya
itu muṉ coṉṉavaṟṟuḷ iru puḷḷikaṭku mēlum mayakkam kūṟum
akalavurai
mēṟcoṉṉa āṟu puḷḷikaḷuḷ ṇṉ eṉṟa iru puḷḷikaṭkup piṉ ka ca ña pa ma ya va eṉṟa ēḻu eḻuttukkaḷum varum. eṉavē ivaṟṟoṭu ṇakarattukku ṭakaramum ṉakarattukku ṟakaramum muṟkūṟiyapaṭi cērum.

(24)	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē
itu nāṉku puḷḷikaṭku oru meymmuṉ mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavurai:
ñ n m v eṉṟa nāṉku puḷḷikaṭkup piṉṉē
yakaram varuvatu poruḷuṭaiyatē.
mey - poruḷ peṟṟaṉṟu - peṟṟatu
vaḻakku
uṟiñ yātu, porun yātu, tirum yātu, tev yātu
uriñ - uriñcutal, porun - poruntutal, tirum - tirumputal, tev - pakai.
tiṟaṉurai
immeymmayakkam puṇarmoḻil varukiṉṟatu. muṉviḷakkiyapaṭi meymmayakkaṅkaḷ oru moḻiyilum varalām. atu kaṇakkaṉṟu. iṉṉa eḻuttukkupippiṉ iṉṉa eḻuttu varum eṉṟa attuṇaiyē īṇṭu ilakkaṇam; eḻuttut toṭarccikkē ilakkaṇam eṉpatai maṟavaṟka.
tolkāppiyar kālattilē immayakkam aritāka iruntamaiyiṉ, vaḻakkil aritāka uṇṭu eṉṟu terivippataṟ kāka `meypeṟṟaṉṟu' eṉa naṭaiceytār. yakaram eṉṟu potuppaṭak kūṟiṉālum' āvō ṭallatu yakaram mutalātu' eṉpataṉāl `yā' eṉṟa ōruyirmeyyē varum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
pala meym mayakkaṅkaḷ oru collilum puṇarmoḻiyilum varum. cila mayakkaṅkaḷ puṇarmoḻiyil maṭṭum varum. innūṟpā kūṟum mayakkam iraṇṭām vakaiyaic cārntatu.
uṟiñ, porun, tirum eṉṟa mūṉṟum uṟiñcu, poruntu, tirumpu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaramākap piṟkālattut tirintaṉa.
immeym mayakkam piṟkālattu vaḻakkāṟillai.
`aiyaṉ ven viṭāta koṟṟat tāvam vantaṭainta taṉṟē' eṉṟa vālivataippaṭalac ceyyuḷil nakara vakara mayakkam putiya varavākum.
ituvumatu
(25)	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvun tōṉṟum.
itu muṉcoṉṉavaṟṟuḷ orupuḷḷikku mēlum mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavurai:
mēṟkūṟiya nāṉku puḷḷikaḷuḷ m eṉṟa oru puḷḷikkup piṉṉē vakaramum varum. eṉavē ya va, eṉa iraṇṭu varum eṉpatu karuttu.

tiṟaṉurai
`yavavun tōṉṟum' `ēḻumuriya' `vavvun tōṉṟum' eṉṟa ummaikaḷ ivaṟṟukkumēl illai eṉṟa muṟṟumai kāṭṭuvaṉa laḷaḵkāṉ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ, yaḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ eṉṟa āytavolikaḷ icainayam tarupavai.

itu mūṉṟu puḷḷi muṉ patteḻuttukkaḷiṉ mayakkam kūṟum.
akalavurai
moḻimarapu eṉṟa aṭuttaviyalil ka ca ta pa ña na ma va ya eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāka varum eṉṟu colluvar. y r ḻ eṉṉum puḷḷikkup piṉṉē avvoṉpatu eḻuttukkaḷum ¨ṅakaramum vantu mayaṅkum.


vaḻakku
	      ka	       ca	    ta	      pa	   ña

tiṟaṉurai
moḻimarapil varum etiratu nōkki `mutalākeḻuttu’ eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭu īṇṭu āḷappaṭṭatu. mutaleḻuttukkum mutalākeḻuttukkum vēṟṟumai yuṇarka. ṅakarattaip pirittuk kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atu nēriya mutalākeḻuttillai eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. mutalākeḻuttu eṉa moḻimēl vaittuc cuṭṭappaṭṭatupōl tōṉṟiṉum atu curukkaṅ karutiyatēyām. olit toṭarcciyeṉṉum meymmayakkamē īṇṭup poruḷām.
mutalākeḻuttu ṅakarattōṭu cērntu varicaiyāka iraṇṭum varum eṉpatu karuttaṉṟu. yaraḻakkaḷiṉ piṉpu ṅakaramum taṉi ōr eḻuttāka vēyṅaṉam, vērṅaṉam vīḻṅaṉam eṉa mayaṅkum eṉṟu koḷka. ṅaṉam - taṉmaip poruḷum iṭap poruḷum tarum cārpuccol, tāṉē niṟkum mutaṉmaiyillai yātaliṉ, moḻi mutaleḻuttōṭu eṇṇappaṭavillai pōlum.
meymmayakkattil muṉṉatu puḷḷiyāka irukkum. piṉṉatu uyirmeyyākavum irukkum eṉṟa muṟaippaṭi ṅakarattai `ṅaṉam' eṉa uyirmeyyākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
`āṅṅaṉam virippiṉ' āṅṅaṉam aṟipa' `āṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ' eṉat tolkāppiyattil iccol cuṭṭaṭuttu āḷappaṭum.
ṅakarattai tūyamoḻi mutaleḻuttākak koḷḷāmaikku oru kāraṇam āṅṅaṉam eṉpatu āṅkaṉattiṉ melliṉat tiripākak karutiyirukkalām. āṅkaṉam eṉṟa vaḻakkum uṇṭu. āṅku, āṅkaṇ eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kakaram varutalaik kāṇalām. malaiyāḷa moḻiyil niṅṅaḷ, māṅṅā, māṅṅā, tēṅṅā eṉa vallolikaḷ mūkkolikaḷāka māṟiya perumpōkkiṉai oppiṭuka. tirintanilaiyil varum eḻuttiṇai moḻi mutalākat tolkāppiyam matikkavillai. `avvai yoṭṭi ṅavvum mutalākummē' eṉṟa naṉṉūlār cuṭṭiya muṭavanaṭai ēṟkattakum.
eṅṅaṉ iruntatev ṟevvaṇṇam collukēṉ
aṅṅaṉ iruntateṉ ṟuntīpaṟa
eṉṟa uyyavanta tēvanāyaṉār tiruvuntiyāril, ṅaṉam eṉpatu ṅaṉ eṉṟa vaṭivil niṟpataik kāṇalām. ituvē aṭivaṭivāyiṉ neñcam eṉpatiṟpōl am eṉpatu cāriyaiyākum.

itukāṟum meymmayakka nilaiyil oru puḷḷimuṉ piṟameykaḷ varum piṟavaravu meymmayakkamē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. innūṟpāvil k ṅ c ñ¨ ṭ ṇ t n p m y l v ḷ ṟ ṉ eṉṟa patiṉāṟu eḻuttukkaḷum taṉmuṉ tāṉ varum eṉavum r ḻ eṉṟa iru eḻuttukkaḷum taṉmuṉ piṟitē varum eṉavum varampu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

meyyōṭu meymmayaṅku nilaiyaip potup paṭaiyākac cuṭṭiṉāl, ra ḻa eṉṟa iraṇṭu nīṅka ēṉaip patiṉāṟu puḷḷikaḷum taṉmuṉ tāṉ varum. naṭaiyāṟṟalāl, ra ḻa iraṇṭaṉmuṉ piṟavē varum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. meynnilai - meymmayakka nilai.

tiṟaṉurai
itu meym mayakkam paṟṟiya iṟuti nūṟpā. iṉṉameykkup piṉ iṉṉamey varutal eṉpatu tamiḻiṉ iyaṟkaiyātaliṉ ivvamaippiṉai naṉku viḷaṅkik koḷḷavēṇṭum. mikat toṉmaiyāṉa tamiḻ iṉṟuvarai nilaipeṟṟiruppataṟkum varuṅkālamellām nilaipeṟṟa vaḻakkup peṟutaṟkum ittakaiya amaippiṉaik kaṭaip piṭippatu makkaḷ kaṭamai. tamiḻmoḻikku nilaipēṟum kālavorumaiyum kaṉṉimaiyum tarum meymmayakkaviyal kuṟittu ivviṭattu vēṇṭumaṭṭum terintu koḷvōm.
patiṉeṭṭumeykaḷai mayakka varavil taṉatē mayakkam, piṟitē mayakkam, taṉṉoṭum piṟitoṭum mayakkam eṉa mūṉṟākap pakukkalām.

	tāṉ	piṟa

orumoḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ tāymoḻi yāḷarkkum kuḻantaikaṭkum moḻic cūḻnilaiyuṭaiyārkkum kēḷviyuṭaiyārkkum mūccōṭṭam pōlavum kuruti yōṭṭam pōlavum tāmē patiyum kēṭṭuvaruvatu moḻi: moḻiyaṟivu peṟac cevi vaḻiyē eḷiya iyaṟkaiyākum. cevivaḻi uṇarappaṭutaliṉ icaikkuk kēḷvi eṉṟa peyaruṇṭu.
ittaṉmaiyaiyum itupōṉṟa moḻit taṉmai kaḷaiyum tāymoḻi makkaḷ tam moḻiyaip pēcat toṭaṅkiyanāḷ mutalē uṇarntiruppar eṉavum putiya karuttukkaḷai marapumuṟaiyiṟ paṭaittukkoḷvataṟkum ilakkiya vuṭaimaikaḷaip pēṇikkoḷvataṟkum piṟatākku talāl moḻikkuk kēṭu vārātu kāttukkoḷvataṟkum vitimīṟalkaḷaik kaḷaintukoḷvataṟkum ilakkaṇa varaṉ vēṇṭum eṉavum ilakkaṇamillāmoḻi aracillā nāṭu okkum. eṉavum kēraḷa pāṇiṉīya āciriyar araca varumaṉ cāṟṟiya poṉmoḻi tolkāppiyattukkuc cevvaṉ poruntum. pēcakkaṟṟuk koṭuppatu. ilakkaṇamaṉṟu: kākka ākka vaḷarkka vaḻivaḻiviḷaṅkat taṭaṅkāṭṭuvatē ilakkaṇam eṉṟu teḷika. ilakkaṇam puṟamiruntu tiṇikkappaṭum ceyaṟkaiyaṉṟu. makkaḷiṉ vāymoḻik kūṟukaḷaikkaṇṭu karaiyuṭaiyātu kaṭṭi uyartti vaḷarppatē ilakkaṇa nōkkam.
tamiḻukku ivvaraṇkaḷuḷ oṉṟu meymmayakkam eṉappaṭum. ataṉai oṉpatu nūṟpākkaḷil viritturaippar tolkāppiyar. piṉvarum puṇarccikaṭkum immayakka naṭpu pēraṭippaṭaiyākum. itaṉaip piṟaḻātu kaṭaippiṭippatē moḻikkāppu. parantu ōṅkiya paṉmoḻit tākkam cāṭuṟum ikkālattu, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ iyaṟkaiyaik karuttūṉṟik kākkavēṇṭum. karaiyuṭaintāl maturāntaka ēripōl āṟutaraiyākit taṭamāki mēṭākik kāṭākātā? kalappup perukivarutaliṉ moḻi viḻippu vēṇṭum kālam itu ātaliṉ ulakattup palamakkaṭ peyarkaḷaiyum paṉṉāṭṭup peyarkaḷaiyum palaporuṭ peyarkaḷaiyum, ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷaiyum iṉṉōraṉṉa ellāvaṟṟaiyum, uṇavai vāy varampukkuḷ aṭakkik koḷvatu pōlat tamiḻ meymmayakka varampukkuḷ aṭaittuc cevviyal kāṇka. tamiḻccoṟkaḷaik kūṭap piḻaimayakkamāka eḻutumpōkku eḻuttāḷariṭai vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu. kavalaippaṭuvār cilarē. cīttalaic cāttaṉārkaḷ perukavēṇṭum. paṭupiḻai kaḷaiyum tamiḻ vaḷarcciyeṉa vātiṭuvārkku iraṅkiyoḻika.
āṅkilamoḻi intiyap peyarkaḷait taṉakkēṟpa māṟṟik koṇṭataiyum nām ēṟṟukkoṇṭataiyum naṉku cintiyuṅkaḷ. perumpiḻaiyāka eppaṭiyellām tamiḻaip pāṭupaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ taravarumpavillai evaṟṟai evvāṟu eḻutavēṇṭum eṉṟa nērkāṭṭukkaḷ cilavaṟṟai varaivaṉ.
meccikkō, teṉmārkku, ālantu, kāntilantu, cerumaṉi, pirañcu, āttirēli, vāciṅkaṉ, āppirikkā, pākittāṉ, icurēl, mācukkō, iraṅkūṉ, tilli, nākapuri, pāṭṭiṉā, kācumīram eṉṟāṅku ūrppeyarkaḷaiyum:
kurōmikō, cerunaṉkō, kōrppaccēv, ciṭṭiṉi, teṉṉicaṉ, cāṉcaṉ, parukku, alaccāntar, peṉṉāṭcā, arittāṭṭi, akamatu. aputul, akkupar, patumāvati, vīṭaṇaṉ, cukkirīvaṉ, aruccuṉaṉ, vīṭumaṉ, cantirakuttaṉ, cūrapaṉmaṉ, mariyāḷ, ēcu, yōvāṉ. cūcaiyappar, tōmaiyar eṉṟāṅku makkaṭ peyarkaḷaiyum;
pippiravari, ēppiral, ākattu, ceṭṭampar, aṭṭōpar, patti, mutti, catti, māṉiyam, puṇṇiyam, kaṇṇiyam, cittiram, cattiram, pattiram, cāttiram, tōttiram, marumam, varuṇaṉai, varumaṉ, tarukkam, akkiṉi, mukkiyam, pākkiyam, cāṅkiyam, karuppiṇi, karuppattaṭai, varukkam, aruccaṉai, coruṇam, āṉmā, āttumā, tarumam, taṉmam, cattam. niruvāṇam, vākkiyam, āciramam, vāttiyam eṉṟāṅku pala coṟkaḷaiyum tamiḻiyaṟkaippaṭi eḻutuvatu arumaiyō? ippala coṟkaḷil tamiḻccoṟkaḷum uṇṭu. ayaṟ coṟkaḷaik kaiyāḷa nēriṭiṉ appōtum tamiḻ meymmayakkattaip pōṟṟa vēṇṭumaḷavukkē mēlaik kāṭṭukkaḷ. itumoḻik kāppu neṟi eṉappaṭum. veḷḷi, poṉ, kalaikkōṭṭu muṉivaṉ, pukai niṟak kaṇṇaṉ, vēḷvi pakaiñaṉ, vaccirat teyiṟṟaṉ. māperumpakkaṉ eṉṟu kampar ceytatupōla ollumaḷavu tamiḻākki viṭuvatuvē naṉmarapākum.
moḻimutaleḻuttilum moḻiyīṟṟeḻuttilum ilakkiyaṅkaṇṭu ciṟupāṅku tolkāppiyattilum vēṟupaṭum pavaṇanti mutalāṉōr iṭainilai meymmayakkattil vēṟupaṭavillai. itaṉāl tamiḻukku ivvilakkaṇattiṉ uyirppāṅku uṟutiyām.

ceyya tamiḻiṉ tiṟam.
cuṭṭukkuṟi
(28) 	ai uam mūṉṟuñ¨ cuṭṭu.
itu cuṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavurai
ōraḷapu icaikkum kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aintām eṉṟu muṉpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ akaram ikaram ukaram eṉṟa mūṉṟum uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttu nilaiyil niṉṟē cuṭṭuk kuṟippiṉaiyuṇarttutaliṉ cuṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟiyum peṟum. akaram cēymaic cuṭṭu, ikaram aṇmaiccuṭṭu, ukaram iṭaiccuṭṭu.
vaḻakku atteru, itteru, utteru, avvīṭu, ivvīṭu, uvvīṭu, `ammūṉṟum eṉpatuvum akakkāṭṭu. iccuṭṭu peyarkkumuṉ varum.
tiṟaṉurai
a i u mūṉṟum cuṭṭu eṉṟa kuṟi peṟum. ivai taṉiyeḻuttākavē irukkum. ivaṟṟukku uyir mayaṅkiyalil taṉippuṇarcci viti kūṟuvar āciriyar. cuṭṭē iṟutiyākavum amaitaliṉ, `cuṭṭiṉiṟuti' eṉpar. atu itu utu eṉpaṉa vaṟṟaic `cuṭṭu mutalukaram'  eṉavum avai ivai uvai eṉpaṉavaṟṟaic `cuṭṭu mutalākiyavai yeṉ ṉiṟuti' eṉavum av iv uv eṉpaṉavaṟṟaic `cuṭṭumutal vakaram' eṉavum iccuṭṭiṉum vēṟupaṭa āḷal kāṇka. atu mutaliyavaṟṟuk kellām urupiyaliṟ cāriyai kūṟum āciriyar cuṭṭukku aṅṅaṉam kūṟāmaiyum karutuka.
naṭumai kāṭṭum ukarac cuṭṭu. iṉṟu perumpāṉmai vaḻakkil illai; eṉiṉum iṭainilaiyāṉa naṭuviṭam uṇmaiyiṉ ataṟku ōrcol vēṇṭumaṉṟō? poru ḷiruppac col vīḻtal, vīḻaviṭutal aṟiyāmaiyākum. iṉṟō ippakutiyaik kuṟippataṟku meyppāṭu kāṭṭit taṭumāṟukiṉṟōm. ātaliṉ ukaraccuṭṭuk kiḷavikaḷai yellām peruvaḻakkāṟṟuppaṭuttal nallatu. kalviyālum eḻuttālum uyirppikka vēṇṭiyaṉa ivai.
“oru kūṭṭattār iṅkum maṟṟoru kūṭṭattār uṅkum iṉṉoru kūṭṭattār aṅkum niṉṟu kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum” eṉpatu tiru.vi.ka.naṭai.
a avaṉum iivaṉum uuvaṉum kūṭiyakkāl
eevaṉai vellā rikal
innaṭai vāypāṭu āṅkilattiṉ irupattu nālāvatu eḻuttum irupattaintāvatu eḻuttum pōlap payaṉ paṭuvatai oppunōkkuka.
viṉākkuṟi
(29)	āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉā.
itu viṉā eṉṟa kuṟi kūṟum.
akalavurai
īraḷavu icaikkum neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻeṉa muṉpu kūṟappaṭṭatu. avaṟṟuḷ ā ē ō eṉṟa anta uyir mūṉṟum neṭṭeḻuttu nilaiyil niṉṟē viṉāpporuḷait tarutaliṉ viṉā eṉṟa kuṟiyum peṟum.
viṉā a eṉa aḷapeṭaip pāṭam oṉṟu koḷḷal yāppup poruttamākum eṉṟa vēṅkaṭarāculu kuṟippu okkum.
vaḻakku: avaṉā coṉṉāṉ? nīyē coṉṉāy? yāṉō coṉṉēṉ? ivai peyarvaḻi vanta viṉākkaḷ.
avaṉ varuvāṉā?
nī celvāyē?
yāṉ colvēṉā?
ivai viṉaivaḻi vanta viṉākkaḷ.
immūṉṟum peyarkkum viṉaikkumpiṉ varuvaṉa.
muccuṭṭil iṭavēṟṟumaiyiruppatupōla immūṉṟu viṉāvilum cila vēṟupaṭu poruṇmai tolkāppiyar kālattu iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
tiṟaṉurai
cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum tarum ivai nūṉmarapil iṭampeṟalāmā? eṉpatu viṉā. nūṟmarapu muppattu mūṉṟu eḻuttuḷ varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku ōriṉam nōkkik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kūṟum iyalātalāl cuṭṭum viṉāvum ivviyaliṟ cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. ivai cuṭṭukkuṟippum viṉākkuṟippum uṭaiyavātaliṉ moḻiyāntaṉmaiyil aiyamillai eṉiṉum īṇṭu nōkkam kuṟiyīṭē. ataṉāṟṟāṉ cuṭṭu eṉavum viṉā eṉavum kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyē payaṉilaiyāka amaittār āciriyar. kuṟiyīṭu kūṟuvatu piṉṉar eṭuttāḷum curukkattiṟkaṉṟē. aintiṉaik kuṟil eṉavum ēḻiṉai neṭil eṉavum paṉṉiraṇṭiṉai uyir eṉavum patiṉeṭṭiṉai mey eṉavum ivvāṟē vaṉmai meṉmai iṭaimai eṉavum orupaṭittāṉa eḻuttukkaḷukku oru kuṟiyīṭu paṭuttiyataik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ammarapuppaṭi a i u mūṉṟu uyirkkuṟilum ōriyalpuṭaiya  cuṭṭuk kuṟiyām. ā ē ō mūṉṟum ōriyalpuṭaiya viṉāk kuṟiyām. itaṉāl nām aṟiyak kiṭappatu eṉṉa? oruvaḻip paṭṭa eḻuttut tokutikku kuṟiyīṭu oṉṟu vaṭippatē nūṉmarapiṉ karuttu eṉpatām. ātaliṉ muḻuccoṉṉilaiyil varum atu, av, avai, avaṉ, yā, yātu, yāvaṉ, evaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟukku nūṉmarapil iṭamillai.
col nōkkil cuṭṭum viṉāvum iṭaiccoṟkaḷ eṉka. ēkāramum ōkāramum piṟaporuṭ kuṟippukkum varutaliṉ iṭaiyiyalil nūṟpākkaḷ yāttār. ā viṉā eṉṟa oru poruṭkuṟippēyuṭaimaiyiṉ innūṟpāvē pōtiya tāyiṟṟu. cuṭṭukkum iḵtokkum. itu muṉcolliyatu pōtum eṉṟa neṟi.
kuṟilkaḷ cuṭṭātalum neṭilkaḷ viṉāvātalum iyaṟkai nayamuṭaiyaṉa.
e eṉṟa kuṟilum oruvakai viṉāvāy varutal uṇṭu. em peyar, emmuṟai, em mey eṉa varumiṭaṅkaḷ uḷa. eṉiṉum perumpālum ekaram akarac cuṭṭukku muṉṉōṭiyāy varum cuṭṭu naṭaiyaik kāṇkiṟōm.
“epporu ḷāyiṉum alla tilleṉiṉ
  apporu ḷallāp piṟituporuḷ kūṟal”

“evvayiṟ peyarum veḷippaṭat tōṉṟi
  avviyal nilaiyal cevvi teṉpa”
“evvaḻi variṉum viṉaiyē viṉaikkuṟippu
  avviru mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa”
mēlum ivviṭaṅkaḷil ekarac cērkkai ellām eṉṟa poruṇmaik kuṟiyākat tōṉṟuvatai uyttuṇarka.
epporuḷ yāyhrvāyk kēṭpiṉum apporuḷ
meypporuḷ kāṇpa taṟivu.
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷum ivvoṭṭu naṭaiyākum.
iṉattiṟ cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum eṉṟa tolkāppiya eṇṇappaṭi, ekarattaiyum viṉāvākak koḷḷalām. 
kāṇka: eppaṭi, emmoḻi, ekkuṟaḷ.
iyalum icaiyum
(30)	aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum
	uḷaveṉa moḻipa icaiyoṭu civaṇiya
	narampiṉ maṟaiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu icait tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭaiyum aḷapu mikutiyum kūṟum. 
akalavurai
nūṉmarapiṉ toṭakkattil uyireḻuttukkaḷukkum meyyeḻuttuk kaḷukkum māttirai kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. ivai pēccu vaḻakkukkum iyalvaḻippaṭṭa ceyyuḷ vaḻakkukkum uriyavai. icaittamiḻukkum ivveḻuttukkaḷē aṭippaṭai; eṉiṉum icai paṇṇātaliṉ āḷatti ceyyuṅkāl iyalukkuc colliya eḻuttiṉ māttiraikaḷ mikku olikkum eṉpatu karuttu.
urai: uyirukkuk kūṟiya māttiraikaḷ kūṭutalāka olittalum meyyoṟṟukkaḷ ōcai nīṭṭittu olittalum ākiya iraṇṭum uṇṭu eṉṟu collappaṭupavai vāyppāṭṭoṭu poruntiya yāḻicai nūliṭattaṉa eṉpar iyaṟpulavar.
aḷapu - māttirai; iyainīṭal - alaku kūṭṭal
moḻipa - moḻiyappaṭupavai, viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyar.
icai - miṭaṟṟicai. maṟaiya - kalaiyiṭattaṉa, aḵṟiṇaip
palaviṉpāṟ kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟu. moḻipa - eḻuvāy;
maṟaiya - payaṉilai.
innūṟpā naṭaiyai
eṇṇeṇpa ēṉai yeḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum
kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻu muyirkku
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ naṭaiyōṭu oppiṭṭut teḷika.
tiṟaṉurai
moḻiyiṉṟi iyalillai; atupōl moḻiyum iyalumiṉṟi icaiyillai. iyalukkum icaikkum taḷamāvatu moḻi eṉiṉum iyalukku uriya eḻuttoli tāṉō icaikkalaikkum eṉpatu viṉā. ivviṉāvukku viḷakkam taruvatu innūṟpā. icaiyil varum collum poruḷuṭaiya taṉṟē; poruṇmaiyaḷavil iyaloliyaṉkaḷiṉ taṉmai uṇṭu. “ceyyuḷiṭattu eḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttirai yaḷavuraikkum ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻukkum icaittamiḻukkum potuvām” eṉpar yāḻ nūl eṉappaṭum tamiḻ vāḻnūl kaṇṭa aṭikaḷ vipulāṉantar.
iyaloliyaṉai uṭkoṇṭatu icai. paṇkaḷukkum tiṟattukkum porunta ārōcai amarōcai paṇṇuṅ kālum āḷatti ceyyuṅkālum mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭum olikaḷ palavāṟu nīṇṭicaikkum, uyiroliyē icaivīccukku iḻumai yūṭṭumātaliṉ, `aḷapiṟantuyarttal' eṉa avvoliyai mutaṟkaṇ moḻintār. oṟṟu māttirai nīḷuvatillai; ōcaiyē nīḷumātaliṉ, icainīṭal eṉṟār. uraiyāciriyarkaḷuḷ icaimaṟai teḷinta aṭiyārkku nallār icaiyil varum uyirkaḷaiyum meykaḷaiyum kuṟittup piṉvarumāṟu uraiviḷakkam eḻutukiṉṟār:


“kuṉṟāk kuṟilaintuṅ kōṭā neṭilaintum
  niṉṟārnta mannakaran tavvoṭu - naṉṟāka
  nīḷattā lēḻum nitāṉattāl niṉṟiyaṅka
  āḷatti yāmeṉ ṟaṟi”
eṉpatu āḷattikku iṉṉum paṭuvatōrilakkaṇam uṇarttu kiṉṟatu. kuṉṟāk kuṟilaintum kōṭā neṭilaintum eṉṟatu kuṟṟeḻuttālum neṭṭeḻuttālum āḷatti ceyyappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu. kuṟṟeḻuttaintāvaṉa a i u e o; neṭṭeḻuttaintāvaṉa  ā ī ū ē ō eṉa ivai, niṉṟārnta veṉṟatu - meyyeḻuttākiya patiṉeṭṭeḻuttuḷḷum mavvum navvum tavvumeṉa mūṉṟeḻuttumallā maṟṟai yeḻuttukkaḷ āḷattikku varappeṟā eṉṟavāṟu “eṉavum mutaliṟ pāṭumiṭattu makarattiṉ oṟṟālē nātattai uccarikkum marapu uṇṭu eṉavum kūṟuvar.”
itaṉāl nām uṇarakkiṭappatu eṉṉa? tamiḻiṉ uyirolikaḷē niṟaṅkaḷiṉ aḷattiyāy icaippaṇṇiṟku uriya curaṅkaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉpatum carikamapatani eṉṟa peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷ piṉṉar curaṅkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum icai varalāṟṟu muṟaiyil teḷivām.
ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉṟum
ivvēḻeḻuttum ēḻicaic kuralē
eṉṟa cēntaṉ tivārakamum cāṉṟātal kāṇka. itaṟku akāracātakam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻip peyar.
`icaiyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟai' eṉpataṉ viḷakkam: tamiḻicaiyil yāḻkkaruviyē mutaṉmaipeṟum. cilappatikāram peruṅkatai cintāmaṇi mutaliya kāppiyaṅkaḷil kāppiya talaimāntarkaḷiṉ yāḻicaiyaṟivu pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ellā icai nuṇukkattiṟkum koḷḷiṭamāvatu yāḻē ātaliṉ `narampiṉ maṟai' eṉṟār tolkāppiyar. poruḷatikārattiṟ karupporuḷ kūṟumiṭattum `yāḻiṉ pakuti' eṉṟa karuviyē icai cārpāka niṟṟal kāṇalām.
narampiṉ maṟai eṉpatē icaiyaik kuṟikkap pōtu māyirukka, ` icaiyoṭu civaṇiya' eṉṟataṉ karuttu yātu? īṇṭu icai eṉpatumiṭaṟṟicai eṉṉum vāyppāṭṭiṉaik kuṟikkum. ivvāyicaikkuttāṉē eḻuttaṭippaṭaiyuṇṭu. aḷapu kaṭantu olittalum meyyōcai nīḷalum uṇṭu. kuḻal yāḻ taṇṇumai muḻavu mutaliyaṉavellām kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ eṉappaṭum. ivai miṭaṟṟicaikkup piṉvaḻi niṟpaṉa eṉpataṉaic cilappatikāram teḷivākkum. ātaliṉ `icaiyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟai' eṉpatu miṭaṟṟicai yoṭu otticaikkum yāḻkkalaiyaik kuṟikkum. tolkāppiyam iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlāyiṉum ēṟṟa peṟṟi icaittamiḻk kuṟippum koṇṭatu eṉpataṉai, `vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē' eṉṟa marapiyal nūṟpāvāṟ kāṇkiṟōm.
paṇṭaik kālattut tamiḻicai vallunar tāmē pāṭṭum pāṭi uṭaṉ yāḻum mīṭṭiṉar.
`kōvalaṉ kaiyāḻ nīṭṭa vavaṉum
kāviriyai nōkkiṉavum kaṭaṟkāṉal varippāṇiyum
mātavitaṉ maṉamakiḻa vācittal toṭaṅkumaṉ'
eṉṟa cilappatikāravaṭikaḷum,
`iruṅkaṭaṟ pavaḷac cevvāy tiṟantivaṉ pāṭiṉāḷō
  narampoṭu vīṇai nāviṉ naviṉṟatō'
eṉṟa cīvaka cintāmaṇiyaṭikaḷum, 
“maṉperum pāṇa ṉārum māmaṟai pāṭa vallār
  muṉpiruṟ tiyāḻiṟ kūṭal mutalvaraip pāṭukiṉṟār.”
eṉṟa tiruttoṇṭar purāṇavaṭikaḷum miṭaṟum yāḻum iṇainta tamiḻicai marapai eṇpikkum. `icaiyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟaiya' eṉṟa tolkāppiyattukku ikkāppiyaṅkaḷ uraiyutavi ceykiṉṟaṉa.
`icaiyoṭu civaṇiya narampiṉ maṟaiya' eṉpatu pāyirattil varum. `centamiḻ iyaṟkai civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntunūl kaṇṭu' eṉpataṟku oru cāṉṟu. eṉavē `muntunūl' eṉpatu iyalnūlōṭu ēṉaikkalaikaḷaiyum kuṟikkum.
tamiḻil eḻuttu vaṭivukkum eṇ vaṭivukkum icai vaṭivukkum neruṅkiya toṭarpu toṉṟu toṭṭu uṇṭu.
kakaraṅ koṭṭē ekaram acaiyē
ēkaram tūkkē aḷavē āytam
eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār mēṟkōḷ. “ivaimāttiraip peyarkaḷ “koṭṭu arai māttirai, ataṟku vaṭivu ka: acai oru māttirai, ataṟku vaṭivu e: tūkku iraṇṭu māttirai, ataṟku vaṭivuu. aḷavu mūṉṟu māttirai ataṟku vaṭivu  ḵ eṉakkoḷka” eṉpatu nallāriṉ viḷakkam. `eṭṭiṉoṭu iraṇṭum aṟiyēṉaiyē' eṉṟa tiruvācamum, `a u aṟiyā aṟivil iṭaimakaṉē' eṉṟa yāppu mēṟkōḷum. eṭṭēkāl laṭcaṇamē' eṉṟa taṉippāṭalum eḻuttoṭu eṇṇukku vaṭivut toṭarpu cuṭṭal kāṇka. tamiḻukkut taṉimatippu niṟuvum eḻuttu vaṭivu pōlat taṉimatippuc cuṭṭum eṇ vaṭivaiyum patipput tuṟaiyilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum nalvaḻakkiṟ pēṇik kāttal naṟṟamiḻar kaṭaṉ.
`uḷḷatu citaippōr uḷareṉap paṭāar' eṉpatu cāṉṟōr cuṭṭu, ātaliṉ ettuṟaiyilum nīṇṭa varalāṟum  taṉittaṉmaiyum kāṭṭum tamiḻuṭaimaikaḷaik kāttalum putiya kālattiṟku ēṟpa vaḻakkiṟ payaṉ koḷḷalum tamiḻvaḻit tōṉṟalkaḷiṉ poṟuppu.


nūṉpamarapu - iyal muṭivurai
ciṟappup pāyiram
(a) `ippāyirattiṉai “vāḻttiyal vakaiyē nāṟpākkum urittē” eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyaliṉpaṭi, nūlvāḻttākavum nūlāciriyaṉ vāḻttākavum koḷḷa iṭaṉuṇṭu.
