`vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari' eṉṟa pā nilaimaṇṭila āciriyappā eṉakkarutalām. maṇṭilayāppu nēraṭiyāka varum eṉavum āciriyam eṉṉum centūkkākum eṉavum pērāciriyar uraivaraitaliṉ, ivvāṟu karuta iṭaṉuṇṭu; eṉiṉum coṟcīraṭiyāl ippāyiram toṭaṅkuvatu muraṇākavuḷḷatu. nilaimaṇṭilam ellā vaṭiyum nāṟcīr koḷvataṉṟō? vēṟuvakai āciriyappāvuṅkūṭa mutalaṭi nāṟcīr peṟuvataṉṟō? ātaliṉ pāyirattai aṭivarai yaṟaiyillā nūṟpāvākak koḷḷutalum tolkāppiyam nūṟpāvaṇṇattātalāṉ, pāyiramum avvaṇṇamē eṉṟu koḷḷutalum arilaṟṟatu.
(ā) nāṉmaṟai evai? tolkāppiyattukku muntiyanūlkaḷum uṭaṉnūlkaḷum uṭaṉpintiya nūlkaḷum namakkuk kiṭaikkāta nilaiyil, oru talaiyāka nāṉmaṟai paṟṟit tuṇitaṟkillai eṉa yāṉum uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟēṉ; eṉiṉum tolkāppiyam muḻutum kiṭaittalāṉum, ammarapiṉ vanta avaiyap paṉuvalkaḷ aḷavākak kiṭaittalāṉum, takkār cilar nērpaṭa āyvu ceytiruttalāṉum perumpāṅku cila karuttukkaḷaik kaṇikkalām. vētaviyācar vakutta irukku ecur cāmam atarvaṇam innāṉmaṟaiyākā eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kālamutal varum karuttākum. avar kūṟum taittiriyam mutaliyaṉa avar kālattum illāta nūlkaḷātaliṉ yātum collutaṟkillai. mēlum peyaraḷavāṉē avai tamiḻ maṟaiyalla eṉpatu teḷivu.
maṟai eṉṟa nūṟcol tolkāppiyattuc cilaviṭaṅkaḷil varukiṉṟatu. icaimaṟaiyai `narampiṉmaṟai' eṉpar. maṟaiīṇṭu kalai eṉṟa poruḷaik kuṟikkum. antaṇar maṟaittu, maṟaiyōr āṟu. maṟaimoḻi mantiram eṉṟivai maṟaippeyar peṟṟaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇalām. vētam niṟainta tamiḻnāṭu eṉpatu pāratiyam `aṟaṅkarai nāviṉ nāṉmaṟai' eṉpataṉāl aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu paṟṟiya nāṉmaṟai eṉṟu karutalām. `aṟa mutalākiya muḻumutaṟ poruṭkum' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. irukku ecur cāmam atarvaṇam eṉṟa āriya maṟaiyaic collakkūṭātu eṉṟa karuttil eḻutappaṭuvatu ivvuraiyaṉṟu; aṅṅaṉam oru talaiyāka moḻitaṟku valuccāṉṟillai. tolkāppiyat toṉmaiyai nōkkuṅkāl pintiya viyācar tokuppu cuṭṭattakkataṉṟu. atarvaṇam vaciya mantiraṅkaḷ uṭaimaiyiṉ nāṉkāka eṇṇattakka maṟaikkuriya takutiyuṭaiyatumaṉṟu. `nāalvēta neṟi tiriyiṉum' (puṟam.2) eṉṟa perumatippukku uriyatumaṉṟu. tiriyī eṉṟa collil atarvaṇattukku iṭaṉillai.
tolkāppiyattai mutaṟkaṇ tamiḻkkaṇṇōṭu kāṇpatu tāṉ cenneṟi. aṅṅaṉam pārkkum nilaiyil iyalpāka vēṟṟuc cāyal variṉ meypporuḷ kāṇpatu cārpu neṟi. eṉavē nāṉmaṟai eṉpataṟku aṟamporuḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉṟa nāṟporuḷaik kūṟum oru maṟai eṉṟu ippōtu uraiceytirukkiṉṟēṉ. `toyyāvulakattu nukarcciyum' (puṟam.214) `aruḷuṭaiyārkku avvukappēṟum' (kuṟaḷ.247) tamiḻkkoḷkaiyātal teḷika. puṟanāṉūṟu mutalāṉa avaiyap paṉuvalkaḷil varum nālvētam, nāṉmaṟai eṉṟa coṟkaḷukku, maṟaikūṟum poruḷ nāṉku eṉṟē koḷḷal takum. maṟai nūlkaḷ nāṉku eṉṟu koḷvatu cālātu. aimperumkuḻu eṇpērāyam eṉa varum toṭarkaḷai oppunōkkuka. kuḻu aintu āyam eṭṭeṉap poruḷpaṭumā?
(i) aintiram eṉpataṉ poruḷ eṉṉa? vaṭamoḻiyil intiraṉaṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aintiram eṉṟa viyākaraṇanūl eṉpatu palar karuttu. attaku orunūl iruntiruppiṉum kālattāl tolkāppiyattukku muntiyatō eṉpatu pēraiyam. paṉṉūṟāṇṭukku muṉṉē maṟaintatu eṉpatu oru varalāṟu. aintira viyākaraṇam collilakkaṇam kūṟum. moḻiyilakkaṇam eṉpatu palar collum muṭivu. eḻuttukkum collukkum ataṉmēl vāḻkkaip poru ḷukkum ilakkaṇam kaṇṭa tolkāppiyar orutuṟaippaṭṭa aintiram kaṟṟar eṉpatu eṉṉa perumai yākum? narampiṉ maṟaiya, antaṇar maṟaittu, yāppaṟi pulavar eṉṟu cuṭṭiyatupōla, aintirak kuṟippēṉum tolkāppiyattuḷ uṇṭā?
pallaikkaḻakaṅkaṇṭa aṇṇāmalai vaḷḷal eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, oḷicciṟatal nīḷalai kaṇṭa irāmaṉ eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, amerikkāvaik kaṇṭa kolampacu eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi, cārpiyam teritta aiṉciṭiṉ eṉat taṉ peyar tōṟṟi eṉṟu collumāṟupōla, `aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉat taṉpeyar tōṟṟi' eṉṟa naṭai ōṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē ivvīṟu piṟaroruvarkkillāta ciṟappāka irukkavēṇṭum. tolkāppiyarkku aintirakkalvi aṉṉa ciṟappukku uriyatō? aṉṟu.
(ī) akapporuḷāṉa aintiṇaiyilakkaṇam tiṟappaṭa vakuttavar tolkāppiyar. avaiyac cāṉṟōrkkum tiruvaḷḷuvarkkum iḷaṅkōvukku iṉṉa perumpulavarkkum tiṇai vaḻikāṭṭiyavar tolkāppiyar. poruḷatikāramē tamiḻukkum tolkāppiyattiṟkum ciṟappu. ātaliṉ, aintiṇai allatu aintiṇai yoḻukkam kūṟavalla tiṟam niṟaintavar eṉpatē ippōtu eṉakkut tōṉṟum uraiyākum.
īṇṭu oruviṉā. ivvuraikku aintiṟam eṉa valliṉamāka vāvatu col irukkavēṇṭumallavā? viṉā nēriya viṉāvē. ittoṭarukkup pāṭapētamum illai. eṉiṉum ṟakaramum rakaramum poruḷ vēṟiṉṟi māṟi niṟkum cila tamiḻc coṟkaḷ uḷa. muri, muṟi, puram. puṟam, pirivu, piṟivu, perumpiṟitu, purattal, puṟantaral, nirappu,  niṟai, citar, citaṟu; karuppu. kaṟuppu eṉa varutalai oppunōkku. uruttiraṉpōlat tiraṇ eṉṟa collukkē āṟṟaṟporuḷ koḷḷalām. tiraṇai, tiraṭci eṉpaṉavum kāṇka. `ayintiram niṟaintavaṉ āṇai yēvalāl' eṉṟa kampariṉ toṭaril `aintiram niṟainta' eṉṟa pāyirappakuti paṭṭāṅkup patintuḷatu. ayintiram niṟaintavaṉ aṉumaṉ āvaṉ. ivaṉ aintavinta pērāṟṟalaṉ; māṇippaṭivam koṇṭavaṉ. `māṇiyām paṭivamaṉṟu' eṉa irāmaṉālum `neṟiniṉṟu poṟikaḷ aintum veṉṟavaṉ' `āṟṟal ītē aimpulattiṉ ceṟiyum ītē' eṉac cītaiyālum pulaṉaṭakkap pukaḻpeṟṟavaṉ.ātalāl `ayintiram niṟaintavaṉ' eṉṟa kampar pāṭalukku aimpulavaṭakkattāl niṟainta āṟṟaluṭaiya aṉumaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvatu mikaiyāmō?
(u ) tolkāppiyam kuṟitta palar tam palvēṟu koḷkaikaḷai aḷavai muṟaiyiṟ cāṉṟupaṭa ārāyntu eṉ muṭiviṉaip pulappaṭuttaṟku ivvurai iṭaṉaṉṟu eṉpataṉai uṇarkiṉṟēṉ. ivaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya eṉ karuttuk kaḷait tamiḻkkātal, cintaṉaikkaḷaṅkaḷ, tolkāppiyap putumai, tolkāppiyattiṟaṉ, oppiyalnōkku ākiya eṉ urai naṭainūlkaḷil mēṟkōḷ potuḷak kāṇalām; ātaliṉ eṉ tolkāppiyak  koḷkaikaḷai ivvuraiyaip payilvārkku vēṇṭiyavaḷavu karuttāṟṟuppaṭuttu muṟaiyil naṭukaṟkaḷ pōlac cila colli vaippēṉ.
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉpatu nūlāciriyariṉ iyaṟpeyar. pōkkaṟupaṉuval eṉpatu nūṟpeyarākalām eṉiṉum vaḻivaḻi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa tolkāppiyam eṉṟa nūṟpeyar eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭē. tamiḻiṉ mutu tolvaralāṟu nōkkiṉ, tamiḻukku mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya nūlaṉṟu tolkāppiyam. iṉṟu nallūḻāl namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa paṉuvaṟ celvattuḷ kālattāl toṉmaivāynta mutalnūl tolkāppiyam ākum.
tolkāppiyattukku muntiya nūlkaḷum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum uṭaṉaṭuttut tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷum aimpūtac cīṟṟattālum, iṉanalap potunōkku iṉmai yālum, ayaṉmoḻi nākarikavēṭkai aṟivai mayakkiyamai yālum aḻintoḻintaṉa. oru perunūlakam niṟaikkum palkalaic cuvaṭikaḷ paṭivāyppiṉṟi aiyakō eṅkō eppaṭiyō maṭintaṉa. celvākkuṭaiya perumakkaḷ  vallaṭimaiyai nalvāḻveṉa makiḻntu kūlimaṉam koṇṭu tāḻntu iḻintamaiyāṉum, kalvi kalai camayam caṭaṅkellām vēṟṟumoḻikaḷāy uruveṭuttamaiyāṉum cātimēl kīḻ kīḻiṉkīḻ eṉṟa piṟappupvaḻip pūcalkaḷāṇum ettuṇaiyō vaṭivil, kēṭukaḷ āḻaṅkoṇṭaṉa.
tamiḻukkup paḻaiya peruvāyppukkaḷaiyellām tūrttup putiya vāyppukkaḷai evvakaiyilum neruṅka viṭāmaiyālum, tamiḻ vāḻka eṉṟa ceyaloṭṭā āravārap pakaṭṭu muḻakkattālum, uṇmait tamiḻc cāṉṟōraip piṉpaṟṟā maṭamaiyāṉum evvaḷavō paṉþṉūṟāyiramāṇṭut tamiḻc celvaṅkaḷ eñciyaṉavum aḻintu varukiṉṟaṉa. ettuṇai māperiya tamiḻiṉattukku ituvā varalāṟu? etirkāla varalāṟu eṉṉō? nampikkai koḷvōm. innilaiyil tolkāppiyam oṉṟē ñālap paḻaiya tamiḻiṉattiṉ tolluyirum puttuyirumāka maṉṉi viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu inta oṉṟaiyāvatu cikkeṉappaṟṟi vaḷarntu uyti peṟuvōmāka.
(ū) tolkāppiyattiṉ kālam ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muntiyatu eṉpārtam kālakkoḷkai eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭu. iṉṟu nam pēṟākavuḷḷa avaiyanūlkaḷ tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram pōṉṟavai tolkāppiyattai nōkkak kālappintiyaṉa eṉṟālum intiya moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟōṭum ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟōṭum oppiṭuṅkāl nam nūṟcelvaṅkaḷ toṉmai cāṉṟaṉavē. tolkāppiyak kālakkalvi muṟaikaḷ ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai uyirōṭṭam peṟṟiruntaṉavātaliṉ, tolkāppiyavurai kāṇpataṟku ittoṉṉūlkaḷ peruviḻukkāṭu naṟṟuṇaiyāvaṉavē. muṟkāla marapuṇarcciyum marapuppaṟṟum cila nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai toṭarntu vantamaiyiṉ iṭaikkālattum tolkāppiyak kalvimarapu tollāṇai nallāciriyarkaḷpāl taḻaittintatu. ataṉālaṉṟō ki.pi.oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuppiṉ tolkāppiyam ilakkaṇavulakilum uraiyulakilum taṇiyāp puttuyir peṟṟatu. ilakkaṇa viḷakkam ilakkaṇakkottu varaiyil tolkāppiya vūṟṟukkaṇkaḷaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. tolkāppiyam ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu mīṇṭum tamiḻakattiṉ tāy nūlākavum ulakappaṇmoḻiyaraṅkil oppuyar vaṟṟa potu moḻiyiyal nūlākavum aṟivoḷi vīcukiṉṟatu.
(e) tolkāppiyar kālattu vaṭamoḻivaravum uṇṭu; ayal nākarika varavum uṇṭu. ivai toṭarvarukaiyākum. oppumai cuṭṭum pāṅkil ivai mikac ciṟuvaraviṉa. aṉṟu āciriyac cūḻnilaiyum āriyat tākkamum avvaḷavāka illai. ātaliṉ āriyak karuttut tōṉṟa nūṟpākkaṭku uraiyeḻutal poruttamaṉṟu. tamiḻ neñcaṅkoṇṭu tamiḻ vaḻiyākavē tolkāppiyattukku uraiyum viḷakkamum kāṇal eḻuttu neṟiyākum.
tolkāppiyattu iṭaiccerukalāṉa cila nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa eṉṟa karuttu eṉakku uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. ciṟiyavaḷavu pāṭapētaṅkaḷ uṇṭu.
tolkāppiyam yātta tolkāppiyar iruvar āvar eṉṟa maruṭkoḷkai. āyvukkaḷattukkē varattakkataṉṟu. itu il poruḷ eṉka.
tamiḻukkum tamiḻiṉattukkum vaḷamaiyāṉa uyarnta putiya etirkāla vāḻvu tolkāppiyattaip pōṟṟum aracālum tolkāppiyap paṟṟuṭaiya makkaḷālum tolkāppiyavaṟi vuṭaiya iḷaiñarkaḷālum tolkāppiya uṇarvuṭaiya eḻuttāḷarkaḷālum uṇṭākum eṉpatu eṉ nampikkai. ituvē ivvurai eḻutuvataṉ nōkkam.
nūṟpā 3
kuṟṟiya likaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
āytam eṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttō raṉṉa
eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟku iruvēṟu uraiyuṇṭu.


orucārār urai
`kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatām. āytam muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉa maṟupeyar eṉpatum aḵtu aṭuppukkūṭṭuppōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭivu peṟum eṉpatum ivvuraiyiṉ mukkiya karuttu. `āytam eṉṟa muppāṟpuḷḷiyum' eṉpatu oru toṭarāka amaiyum. ivvuraikkuk kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉṟa īriṭaṅkaḷil ummait tokaiyākak karutavēṇṭum. iḷampūraṇar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, veḷḷaivāraṇar ittakuvuraiyiṉar.
maṟucārār urai
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam eṉṟa mūṉṟuvakaip puḷḷikaḷum `eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉpatām. īṇṭumuppāṟ puḷḷi eṉpatu āytattai maṭṭum kuṟiyātu mūṉṟaiyum kuṟikkum. `eṉṟa’ eṉpatu mūṉṟiṉaiyum cērkkum. āytam muppāṟpuḷḷi yaṉṟu; kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaramum pōla atuvum oṉṟu pērāciriyar. civañāṉa muṉivar cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār. vēṅkaṭarācalu, mīṉāṭci cuntaraṉār, turaiyaraṅkaṉār ittakuvuraiyiṉar.
karuta vēṇṭiya viṉā eṉṉa? muppāṟ puḷḷi āytattaik kuṟikkumā? mūṉṟaiyum kuṟikkumā? mutalurai cila iṭarppāṭuṭaiyatu. kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam mūṉṟum eṉṟa naṭaiyiruntāṟpōtum. āytam eṉṟa oṉṟukkumaṭṭum āytam eṉṟa muppāṟpuḷḷiyum eṉṟu virittuccolvatu mikaiyākum. `eṉṟa' eṉpatu āytam oṉṟukkē cēruvatāyiṉ ikaramum ukaramum eṉa ummai nūṟpāvil `virintāṟṟāṉ poruḷ teḷivākum. `mutal karuvuripporuḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟē' eṉṟa akattiṇaiyiyal nūṟpavilum, `koṭinilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum' eṉṟa puṟattiṇaiyiyal nūṟpā vilum varum `eṉṟa' eṉṉum peyareccam mūṉṟiṉaiyum akappaṭuttalai oppunōkkuka.
āytam puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟa karuttil vēṟupāṭillai. ataṉaic cuṭṭa vēṇṭiṉ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṟ colliyatupōla `āytappuḷḷi; eṉṟāṟ pōtumē. mutaṟcārār karuttuppaṭi āytam mūṉṟu puḷḷiyuṭaiyateṉak koṇṭālum `muppuḷḷi' eṉṟu varuvatutāṉ poruttamēyaṉṟi muppāṟ puḷḷi eṉṟu pāliṭai varutal poruttamaṉṟu. pāl eṉpatu pakutiyaik kuṟikkum. `appāl aintum ōraḷa picaikkum' eṉavum. `appāl ēḻum īraḷapicaikkum' eṉavum aṭuttu varum nūṟpāvukkaḷil pāl - pakuti eṉap poruḷpaṭutalai uṇaralām. eṉavē `muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉpatu mūṉṟeḻuttukkum uriya puḷḷikaḷaiyē kuṟikkum. immūṉṟum vaṭivālum cārpeḻuttām eṉpataṟku ituvumōr cāṉṟu. āytamē muppāṟpuḷḷi peṟumeṉṟāl mūṉṟu puḷḷikkuḷ tammuṉ vēṟupāṭu irukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō? aṭuppuk kūṭṭuppōl eṉṟaliṉ vēṟupāṭiṉmai veḷippaṭai. muppuḷḷi eṉṉātu muppāṟpuḷḷi eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalāṉum, `eṉṟa' eṉpatu ummai tokka orunilaiyil mūṉṟaiyumē kuṟikkumātalāṉum iraṇṭāvatu uraiyē poruntum.
nūṟpā - 8
uyirmey eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷai uvamai yākupeyarākak koṇṭu meypporuḷ kūṟuvar palar. uyir taṉittiyaṅkum taṉmaiyatu. taṉittiyaṅkamāṭṭā meyyai iyakkuvatu eṉavum meykkut taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaliṉṟu. uyir cērntutāṉ iyakkam peṟum eṉavum paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ meymaiyiyalai ilakkaṇamum kāṭṭuvatu eṉavum viḷakkañ ceypa. tamiḻar kaṇṭa meymaiyiyaliṉpaṭi, uyir cārntataṉ taṉmaittu, iṟaiyaiyō kaṭṭiṉaiyō paṟṟi niṟkumiyal piṉatu. iṟaikku uṭampāvatu, meyyuṅkūṭa uyirkku viḷakkāvatu eṉṟa uṇmaikaḷai aṟikiṉṟōm. uyir ētāvatu oṉṟiṉaip paṟṟātu nillātu eṉṟa meyymmaiyaip
paṟṟuka paṟṟāṟṟāṉ paṟṟiṉai appaṟṟaip
paṟṟuka paṟṟu viṭaṟku
eṉṟa tuṟavukkuṟaḷ pulappaṭuttum. `avaiyē tāṉē yāyiru viṉaiyiṉ' eṉṟa civañāṉa pōta maṟaiyālum, `eḷḷum eṇṇeyum pōlniṉṟa entaiyē' eṉṟa tiruvācaka muṟaiyālum, `uṭampār aḻiyiṉ uyirār aḻivar' eṉṟa tirumantiraniṟai moḻiyāṉum; uyiriṉ cārpiyal viḷaṅkum. ātaliṉ immeyp poruḷuṇarvai ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷāṉa uyirmēlum meymmēlum ēṟṟik kāṇpatu karuttēṟṟam eṉṟa kuṟṟattiṉpāṟpaṭum ēṉai ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaṭkum ivvāṟu meymaiyiyal kāṭṭuvatu ovvātu. itaṟkumēl iṅku ikkaruttārāycci uraippuṟamāyviṭum.
uyirāyiṉum meyyāyiṉum muppatum mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭupavai; piṟappu vēṟṟumaiyum vēṟṟup poruṇmaiyum taṉitta oliyaṉ nilaiyum koṇṭavai; tam poruṇmaikku meykaḷ uyirkaḷaic cārntu niṟkum cārpeḻuttukkaḷ alla; uyiroliyaṉkaḷai ottavai meyyoliyaṉkaḷ. mutal nūṟpāviyal `akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy' eṉṟu muṭikkum toṭarnilaiyāl camattaṉmai teḷivām. ātaliṉ vāyuḷ uṟupput taṭaiyiṉṟi varum oliyaṉ uyir eṉavum uṟupput tīṇṭip piṟakkum oliyaṉ mey eṉavum oliyiyal muṟaiyiṟ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuvatē moḻineṟi.
nūṟpā -9 
piṟarkaruttu: innūṟpā uyirmeykku aḷavu kūṟuvatu; meyyōṭu kūṭiṉālum kūṭiya uyir taṉ māttiraiyil tiriyātu; ka eṉṟa uyirmeykkuṟil oṉṟarai māttirai yākātu; kā eṉṟa uyirmeyneṭil iraṇṭarai māttirai yākātu eṉavum; araināḻi yuppunīrpōla uyirmeyyil mey taṉ māttiraiyai iḻakkum eṉavum karuttuk kūṟuvar. uyiriyal - uyirkkuriya māttirai eṉapporuḷ ceyvar. muṉṉum piṉṉum varum atikāram māttiraippaṭṭatātaliṉ ivvāṟu poruḷkoḷḷat tūṇṭiyatu.
uyirmeyyuḷ meykku māttirai illai eṉpatuvē āciriyar karuttāyiṉ, meyyiyal tiriyum eṉṟu nūṟpittiruppar. `uyiriyal tiriyā' eṉṟa naṭaiyāl tiriyumō aṉṟō eṉṟa aiyappāṭu uyirai oṭṭiyatākum. meyttiripu paṟṟiya kuṟippukkē iṭiṉillai. aiyappāṭu mey paṟṟiya tāyiṉ, uyirōṭiyaiyiṉ meyyaḷavu iḻakkum eṉa nūṟpā piṟantirukkum.  īṇṭu atikāramum; muṟṟum aḷaviṉ mēlataṉṟu. uyireṉa moḻipa, meyyeṉa moḻipa eṉpataṉāl kāraṇappeyart toṭarcci peṟappaṭum. paṭavē, taṭaippaṭum meyyoliyōṭu cērntālum ivviyaipiṉāl uyir taṉ piṟappu vaḻiyiṉiṉṟu tirivatillai; atāvatu taṭaippaṭā nilaiyiṉiṉṟum māṟuvatillai eṉpatē teḷivu.
nūṟpā 10
oruviṉā meykku māttirai arai eṉpatil karuttuvēṟupāṭu illai itu taṉimeykkā? uyirmeykkum uṇṭā? uyirmey iṇaippil mey taṉ arai māttiraiyai iḻantuviṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatum `ka' oru māttirai yām  `kā' irumāttiraiyām eṉpatum ellāhrtaṅ karuttu. ikkoḷkai poruntumā? meykku māttirai yuṇṭeṉa veḷippaṭaiyāka nūṟpittirukkum tolkāppiyam uyirmeyyākuṅkāl meykku māttiraiyiḻappuc cuṭṭak kāṇōmē. ātaliṉ oṉṟarai iraṇṭarai uyirmeykaṭku uṇṭeṉac coṉṉāl varum piḻaiyeṉṉa?

moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉum terintuvē ṟicaippiṉum
  eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu moḻimarapu valiyuṟuttuvatālum,
“māttirai yaḷavum eṭuttiyal vakaiyum
  mēṟkiḷarn taṉ veṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟu ceyyuḷiyal eṭuttu moḻivatālum uyarmeyk kaṇṇum māttirai meykkuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu karuttu
“uyiril leḻuttum eṇṇap paṭāa
  uyirttiṟa miyakkam iṉmai yāṉa”
eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal nūṟpāvil uyirmey vilakkappaṭavillai eṉpataṉaiyum niṉaika. uyirmeykku māttiraiyillai yeṉiṉ oliyaṉām peṟṟiyirātu. `nīr taṉittu aḷantuḻiyum nāḻiyāy araināḻi uppiṟ kalantuḻiyum kūṭi oṉṟarai nāḻiyāy mikātavāṟu pōlvatōr peṟṟi yeṉṟu koḷvatallatu kāraṇam kūṟalākāmai yuṇarka; eṉavum āciriyaṉ āṇai yeṉpārum uḷar eṉavum nacciṉārk kiṉiyar colliyamaivar eṉ karuttu mēlum cintaṉaik kuriyatu.
nūṟpā 12
piṟarkaruttu: “uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē” eṉṟa nūṟpā pakarattōṭu makarattiṟku varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai kūṟuvatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉākkiṉiyarum uraippar. ivvurai muṟṟup poruttamiṉṟu; eṉiṉum iruperum urai yāḷarkaḷum ivvāṟu uraittiruppataṟku avar taṅkālattu varivaṭivac cūḻnilai oru kāraṇamāka irukkalām.
iyalpu makarattukkum makarak kuṟukkattukkum varivaṭivu vēṟṟumai kūṟuvatu eṉavum makarakkuṟukkam puṟattuppeṟum oru puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyum oru puḷḷipeṟum eṉavum itukāṟum palar urai ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar.
kuṟukā makarattoṭu kuṟukiya makarattiṉ vaṭivu vēṟṟumai moḻiyum eṉṟa karuttu uṭaṉpaṭattakkatē. āṉāl iru puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟa karuttu poruntiya uraiyaṉṟu. vēṟupāṭu cuṭṭuvatu innūṟpāviṉ nōkkam. ataṟku iṉṉoru puḷḷi peṟum eṉṉṟu oru viti coṉṉāṟ pōtumē. anta iraṇṭāvatu puḷḷi uḷḷē irukkum eṉṟu mēlum oru viti vēṇṭumā?
iraṇṭu puḷḷi varum eṉpatu tolkāppiya nōkkāyiṉ uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyum uruvākum eṉṟa ummai vēṇṭum. ummaiyiṉmaiyiṉ nām karutavēṇṭiyatu eṉṉa? makarak kuṟukkam mey eṉpataṟkuriya puḷḷi peṟum eṉavum kuṟukkam eṉpataṉait terikka avvorupuḷḷi tāṉum uḷḷē irukkum eṉavum koḷḷavēṇṭum. (kāṇka. pōṉma) itu oru kaṇita muṟai. ātaliṉ iru puḷḷipeṟum eṉṟa uraikaḷ cālā.
nūṟpā: 15 eḻuttuvaṭivaṅkaḷum karuttukkaḷum
tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivu kuṟittut tolkāppiyam cilavaṟṟaip pāṅkupeṟa vitikkum. ippakutikkaṇ varivaṭivupaṟṟik kālattukkēṟṟa cintaṉaikaḷaic cintikka vaippatu iv vuraiyiṉ kaṭamaiyākum. yāṇṭum tamiḻkkāppum tamiḻ vaḷarcciyumē ivvuraiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷ.
tamiḻoliyaṉ paṟṟiyum vaṭivaṉpaṟṟiyum aṇmaik kālattup palatuṟaiyiṉar palvēṟu karuttukkaḷai eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam eṉṟa peyarāl vēkamāka veḷippaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. itu kuṟittuk kaṭṭuraikaḷum nūlkaḷum pala vantuḷa. ulakattu aṟiviyalāl iṉṟu ellām viraivum māṟṟamum peṟuvatupōla moḻikaḷum peṟṟāka vēṇṭum. eṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ vēkavōṭṭattukku ēṟpa moḻikaḷ takutipeṟa vēṇṭum. ātaliṉ moḻic cīrtiruttam eṉpatu kālattāy peṟṟa cintaṉaik kuḻaviyākum.
palar karuttukkaḷ
uyiroliyaṉkaḷuḷ aikāram oḷakāram; iraṇṭum vēṇṭiyatillai; meyyoliyaṉkaḷuḷ ṅakaram ñakaram nakaram ṟakaram ḻakaram vēṇṭiyatillai; uyirmeyyilum aikāra oḷakāraṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyatillai; uyirmey eṉṟa iṉamē vēṇṭiyatillai eṉpārum uḷar.
vaṭamoḻiyolikaḷuḷ eṭṭāmeyyum muppatā meyyum muppattōrāmeyyum muppattiraṇṭāmeyyum muppattumūṉṟāmeyyum muppattaintāmeyyum āṅkila moḻiyuḷ āṟām oliyum pala olippuṭaiyolikaḷum vēṇṭum eṉpārum uḷar.
varivaṭivam paṟṟi veḷiyiṭum karutturaikaḷ palapala. eḻuttu muppatu eṉṟālum āytattaiyum uyir meyyeḻuttukkaḷaiyum eṇṇuṅkāl olikaḷ 247 ām. ivaṟṟil varum 131 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ vēṇṭiyatillai. 28 allatu 30 kuṟiyīṭukaḷ pōtum. ai oḷa eṉpaṉavaṟṟai ay, av eṉa eḻutalām. aintu kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum kāl vāṅkiṉāl neṭṭeḻuttākiviṭum. taṉineṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyatillai. putiya uyirkkuṟikaḷai ākkik koṇṭāl a eṉṟa oru vaṭivē uyiriṉattukku amaiyum. āytam vēṇṭiyatillai.
ke kē kai eṉṟiṉaiya uyirmeykaḷil uyirkkuṟikaḷ muṉṉē varukiṉṟaṉa; ko kō kau eṉṟiṉaiya uyirmeykaḷil uyirkkuṟikaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum varukiṉṟaṉa. ivai poruttamila eṉavum uyiroḷi piṉ nikaḻvatāl uyirkkuṟilkaḷ ellām meyvaṭivukkup piṉpu varuvatutāṉ muṟai eṉavum uyirmey vaṭivukaḷ tēvaiyillai; āṅkilam pōl meyvaṭivaṉaiyum aṭuttaṭuttu eḻutiṉāṟpōtum eṉavum kūṟuvārum uḷar.
ivaṟṟiṉum palapaṭi kaṭantu intiyavorumaip pāṭṭiṟku eḻuttellām oruvaṭivāka atuvum tēvanākari yāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpārum ulakamuḻunōkkil urōmaṉ eḻuttukkaḷait taḻuvik koḷḷalām eṉpārumākat tamiḻeḻuttuc cīrtiruttam kuṟittuk karutturai vaḻaṅkuvar mikappalar.
uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷaip perukkik kāṭṭi eḻutteṇṇikkai cumai eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tavaṟu; palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷil orumaippāṭu uṇṭu; āṇaiyiṉṟiyum iyakkamiṉṟiyum kaṭṭāyamiṉṟiyum neṭuṅkālamāka iyalpāṉa māṟṟaṅkaḷ peṟṟu nilaippatam eytiyavai tamiḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷ. kaiyeḻuttil tēvaikkēṟṟa vaṭṭāra vaḻakkukaḷum uṇṭu. evvakai vaḷarccikkum muppatu oliyaṉkaḷum iṉṟuḷḷa varivaṭivukaḷum pōtum. uḷḷatai muṟaivaittuk kaṟpikka vēṇṭum. uḷḷatē nallatu, vallatu eṉpārum uḷar.
tamiḻettuc cīrtiruttam kūṟuvōr kālattukkēṟpat tamiḻ peruvaḷarcciyuṟa vēṇṭum. kuḻantaikaṭku eḻuttuc cuvai kuṟaintu tamiḻ eḷimaiyāka vēṇṭum. kalvi perukik kallāmai oḻiya vēṇṭum. taṭṭaccu variyaccu mutalāṉa putupputup poṟiyilukkēṟpa eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ curuṅkavēṇṭum. ulaka muḻutum tamiḻ parava vēṇṭum eṉṟa naṉṉōkkam koṇṭavarkaḷē. yārtām innalluḷḷam tamiḻukku illātavarkaḷ? eṉavē nalluḷḷam īṇṭu aiyap poruḷillai. eḻuttuk karuttukkōḷkaḷai yellām taṉipporuḷākat tarukkippataṟku itu muḻu viṭamaṉṟu uḷḷatu kāppataṟkum varuṅkālap peruvaḷarccikkum eṉṟu muḷatamiḻukkum paṇput tamiḻiṉattukkum ulakat tamiḻp parappukkum nallaṉa eṉṟu yāṉ urāyntu kaṇṭa cila muṭipukaḷai eḻuti vaippēṉ. itu maṟṟoṉṟu virittalaṉṟu; uṟṟoṉṟu kūṟutalām.
irunūṟāṇṭukaṭku mēlākat tamiḻmoḻikku aṭittaḷattil viḷainta vērkkēṭukaḷ palappala. ayalaracu māṟṟakkaḷāl tamiḻmoḻi vaḻakkilum iḷaṅkalviyilum aḻivum iḻivum uṟṟatu. āṅkila āṭciyil makkaḷuṇarvu āṅkila moḻimēl ōṅki vaḷarntatu. virittup payaṉeṉ? tamiḻmakkaṭkut tamiḻiḻeḻutteṉṉa, tamiḻē cumaiyākat tōṉṟiviṭṭatu. illaṟap peṇṇukkuk karu cumaiyeṉṟāl yātu ceyyamuṭiyum? pallāyiram āṇṭākat tamiḻ māntar eṇṇiyaṟiyāta eḻuttuc cumai eṉṟa pōliyuṇarvu innūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuntataṟkuk kāraṇam tamiḻmoḻi kuṟṟuyirāṉatu maṭṭumaṉṟu; āṅkila mutalāṉa piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ aḻuttamāṉa mayalāṉa ātikkamum ām. oru tamiḻkkuḻantai tamiḻ kaṟkum nērattaiyum āṅkilam kaṟkum nērattaiyum tamiḻuṇarvaiyum āṅkilavuṇarvaiyum kaṇittuppārttāl, tamiḻil evvaḷavu eḻuttut tiruttamum eṉṉa tiruttamum uḻuttuppōm eṉpatu veḷḷiṭaimalai. ikkoṭum pōkku ennilait tamiḻariṭattum taṭittuviṭṭatu. eṉavē eḻuttuc cumaiyeṉpatu poyyāṉa oru tōṟṟam. ikkaruttu tamiḻmoḻikku maṭṭumaṉṟu; intiya nāṭṭiṉ palamoḻikkum ēṟkum. āṅkilattiṉ 26 eḻuttukku nālvakai vaṭivam illaiyā?
tamiḻmoḻi varalāṟṟil eḻutturuvaṅkaḷ māṟivantuḷa eṉpataṉai nāṉum aṟivēṉ. orumoḻiyil coṟkaḷ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ naṭaikaḷ māṟṟamuṟuvatu pōla vaṭivaṅkaḷum māṟṟam peṟuvatu iyalpē. oru nalluṭampu nāḷtōṟum kaṇṇukkut tōṉṟāmal vaḷarum iyalpu vaḷarcciyē māṟṟam eṉappaṭum. atupōl moḻiyiṉ pala kūṟukaḷum vaḷarcciyeṉṉum māṟutalkaḷ peṟuvatu poruḷiṉ iyaṟkai. avvāṟutāṉ eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ ciṟitākavum nuṇṇitākavum, vēṟṟumaiyuṇarvillāmalum māṟi vantuḷa. ataṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum piṟa paṉuvalkaḷum alaimāṟuvatupōl māṟṟam tōṉṟāmalē uḷmāṟṟam peṟṟaṉa.
itukāṟum tamiḻvaṭivaṉkaḷ māṟi varavillaiyā eṉṟu viṉavum eḻuttut tiruttikaḷ māṟṟam eṉpatu tōṉṟāmal iyalpiṉum iyalpāka māṟi vantirukkum pāṅkiṉaiyum. ataṉāl ellā ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ūṟupaṭā vāḻvaiyum karutip pārkkavillai. kaṭṭāya māṟṟam ivar tam vitiyākum. vaṭivaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟaiyoḻittup paḻaiyavum putiyavumāṉa 30 kuṟikaḷuḷ tamiḻmoḻiyai aṭakkiviṭalām eṉṟāl, aṭakkiviṭalām. āṉāl itukāṟum vanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷum nallaṭakkam eytāvā? ipputiya tiruttat tāl eḻutappaṭṭa nūlē tamiḻ eṉṟa mayakkap pōkkukaḷ ēṟpaṭāvā? muṟaiyāṉa tamiḻkkalviyum tamiḻk kalvikkuriya cūḻnilaiyum iṉṟupōlavē iḻivāka iruntāl ipputuveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ vāḻvutāṉ eṅkē? mēlum tamiḻ varalāṟē cilaveḻuttukku muntiyanūl, cila veḻuttukku pintiya nūl eṉṟu iruperum piḷavupaṭṭu, muṟkālattu neruppālum nīrālum palatokuti aḻintamaipōla, eḻuttālum iṉṟuḷḷaṉavum  aḻintoḻiyāvā? itaṉai niṉaikkum pōtu neñcam kulaikiṉṟatu. uḷḷa nūlkaḷai yellām āṇaiyeḻuttil koṇṭuvantu viṭuvōm eṉṟa nayappukku yār uṟuti? ituvarai coṉṉatupōtum pōtum. ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷum kalveṭṭukkaḷum patippumukam peṟā nilaiyai iṉṟum kāṇkiṉṟōmē?
paḻaiya eḻuttumarapil uḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ ellām iṉi varuṅkāla makkaḷ vaḷarccikku vēṇṭiyatillai; avai paḻaṅkoḷkaikaḷ eṉṟu orēyaṭiyākat tūkkiyeṟintu perumai koḷḷum āṇavamē eḻuttut tiruttattāl viḷaiyum viṉai eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ. anta eṇṇam uṭaiya valiya oru cārarum illāmal illai iṉṟuḷḷa eḻuttu vaṭivilēyē immaṉappōkkai kāṇumpōtu, vēṟu evvāṟu eṇṇam tōṉṟum? `uḷḷatu citaippōr uḷareṉap paṭāar’ eṉṟa caṅkamoḻi nam vaḻikāṭṭiyākuka. nāṭu viṭutalai peṟṟup pallāṇṭu ceṉṟapiṉ, iṉṟu maḻalaiyar paḷḷiyellām tamiḻ vitaikuḻikaḷāka illāmal putaikuḻikaḷāka irukkum avalattaip pārkkumpōtu, tamiḻil tavaṟi oru coṟcoṉṉālum tamiḻk kuḻantai kaḷiṭam taṇṭakkācu vāṅkum paḷḷikaḷ tamiḻmānilattil koṭikaṭṭi vaḷarumpōtu, eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam pēcuvār mutaṟkaṇpēca vēṇṭiyatu tamiḻaṉṟō. tamiḻ vaippaṉṟō, tamiḻ kalvimoḻiyātalaṉṟō? ivvuṇmai maṉam patiyumāka.
kālattiṟ kāṇappeṟum poṟikaṭku ēṟpat tamiḻvaṭivaṅkaḷai amaittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. atutāṉ vaḷarcci. iṉṟēl tamiḻ piṉṉaṭaiyum eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu aṟivuṭaimaiyaṉṟu. toḻilnuṭpaṅkaḷ mikaviraivil paḻaimaiyāki varukiṉṟaṉa. poṟikaḷ viraintu māṟṟam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. pattāṇṭaḷavil poṟi māṟṟaṅkaḷ talaikīḻāka vantuviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. miṉṉaṇuviyalum kaṇippāṉum ceyyum māṟutalkaḷ purāṇattaiyum viñcukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē makkaḷiṉattukku uriya potuvuṭaimaiyum taṉṉikarillāk karuviyumāṉa moḻiyaik kālattuviḻum karuvikaḷiṭam oppaṭaittaṟkillai. ceypoṟikaḷ māṟuntōṟum eḻutturuvaṅkaḷaik māṟṟikkoṇṭu pōvatu. aṟivākumā? aṟiviyalākumā?
moḻi nōkkic ceypoṟikaḷ varavēṇṭum. kāṇappaṭa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, ceypoṟikku moḻiyaṭimaip paṭakkūṭātu. ippōkku ceypoṟivīḻccipōla, ceyyeḻuttum vīḻcciyāki, palkālappiḷavu ēṟpaṭṭu aṟivut toṭarcciyai aṟuttuviṭum. toḻilnuṭpam iṉṉum vaḷaruṅkāl iṉṟaiya tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷ appaṭiyē eḷimai ceyyum putuppoṟikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyum eṉpatu eṉ nampikkai. evvāṟāyiṉum moḻi  eṉṟa nilaik karuviyaik kālantōṟum caṭutiyil māṟum nilaiyillāc ceypoṟikaṭku vaṇakkakkūṭātu. vaṇakkiṉ, uṉṉālē nāṉ keṭṭēṉ eṉṉālē nī keṭṭāy eṉṟa kataiyākkiviṭum. muḻutum nikaḻkālak kaṇkoṇṭu moḻit tiruttam colvatu oruvakaik kuruṭu eṉa viṭuka.
muṟkālattu moḻivāyil kēḷvic celvamāka iruntatu. cila nūṟṟāṇṭāka eḻuttuvāyil pēriṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. accuppoṟi palavakaiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa piṉ eḻutappaṭikkat terivatē kalviyeṉṟa nilai patintu viṭṭatu. moḻikku oliyaṉ uḷḷuṟuppākavum vaṭivaṉ puṟavuṟuppākavum mutaṉmaiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa ātaliṉ eḻutturuvaṅkaḷaip putupputup poṟikaṭkēṟpat tirittal eṉpatu ituvarai tōṉṟiya nūlkaḷaip paḻam poruṭ kāṭciyākkuvatōṭu, ōraḷavu kaṟṟāraiyum kallātārākki viṭum. tiruntāta cila nāṭōṭi moḻikaṭku eḻuttukkaḷai iṟṟai moḻiyiyalār vaṭittuk koṭuppatu pōla ñālat tāyāṉa tamiḻaiyum karutiviṭṭaṉarē eḻuttuttiruttikaḷ. ēḻaiyeḷiyavarkaṭkum iḷaṅ kuḻantaikkum iraykuvārpōla eḻuttut tiṇippikaḷ ceyyum muṉaippu aḻi kolliyāki viṭum. cevveḻuttil irupattaintu variyuḷḷavai tiṇippeḻuttil muppattiraṇṭu varikaḷākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa peruṅkuṟaiyālum ōraḷavu kaṟṟataṟkuṟit tamiḻarkaḷum ivveḻuttāl kallārāki viṭuvar eṉṟa nallaccattālum āṇaiyeḻuttait tamiḻitaḻkaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyiṟ piṉpaṟṟa villai.
āṅkilattai muṉmātiriyāka vaittu eḻuttut tiruttikaḷ tamiḻvaṭivaṅkaḷaip putukka niṉaikkiṉṟaṉar. uyirmey eṉṟa iṉamē vēṇṭā eṉṟa taṭippukku ivvātikkam oru piṉṉaṇi. eḻuttuttiruttattāl āṅkilam pōl tamiḻ vaḷarntu viṭum eṉpatu iṉṉōr eṇṇam pōlum, eṇṇam paḻutillai; ceymuṟaiyē paḻutu.
ōṅkivaḷar āṅkilam moḻiyamaippil oliyāṉum vaṭivāṉum olippāṉum muṉṉoṭṭup piṉṉoṭṭālum viṉaiyoṭṭāṉum kālavuṟuppukkaḷāṉum orumai paṉmaiyāṉum palvēṟu kuṟaiyuṭaiyatu; oḻuṅkaṟṟa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpatu; moḻiyiyalār oppiya karuttu. `oliyaṉukkotta vaṭivaṉ eṇṇikkaiyillai eṉpatum veḷippaṭṭa ceyti, pireñcu moḻiyaḷavu illāviṭiṉum āṅkilattil colliṉ eḻuttu vēṟu, ataṉai olikkum nilai vēṟu; aḵtāvatu eḻutiyatai olikkamuṭiyātu; olippataṟkuttaṉip payiṟci vēṇṭum. āṅkilavakarātikaḷ ippayiṟcikku mutaṉmai koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.
intiya moḻikaḷilum ciṟappākat tamiḻmoḻiyilum eḻutaṟkum olittaṟkum vēṟupāṭillai kiṭantāṅku eḻuttaik kūṭṭuvatē oliyākiviṭum. kaiyakala variccuvaṭi yaṭṭaiyait terintukoḷḷa oru kiḻamai pōm. terintu koṇṭāl tolkāppiyattaiyē eḻuttukkūṭṭip paṭikkalāmē. itaṟkumēl ōr eḷimaiyuṇṭā? airōppiya iṉattaic cārnta vaṭamoḻiyum piṟa intiya āriya moḻikaḷum tamiḻ marapiṉavē.
itaṉai iṅkuk kūṟuvataṉ karutteṉṉa? kuṟaipāṭu niṟaintatu eṉac cuṭṭap peṟum ulaka āṅkilam kuṟaipāṭukaḷai nīkki ulaka moḻiyāyiṟṟā? āṅkilattil cila tiruttattukku āṅkoṉṟu īṅkoṉṟumākac ceyta muyaṟciyum celavum eṉṉāyiṉa? āṅkilattār tam uḷḷa vaṭivaṅkaṭku accup poṟi vakai kaṇṭaṉarēyaṉṟi ivvakaikkāka vaṭivaṅkaḷaic cetukkavillai. vaḻivaḻivanta cevvaṭivaṅkaḷaik kālattu vīḻum poṟikaṭkeṉa māṟṟavillai. uṭalukku uṭaiyā, uṭaikku uṭalā? kaṇṇukku āṭiyā? āṭikkuk kaṇṇā? kālukku naṭaiyaṉā, naṭaiyaṉukku kālā? etaṟku etu, ataṟku eppaṭi eṉṟa vāypāṭu teḷivāka vēṇṭum.
oppiṭṭu oppiṭṭup pārttāl vaḷarntōṅkiya moḻikaḷ kūṭap pala kuṟaipāṭukaḷ uṭaiyaṉavākavē tōṉṟum. e o eṉṟa kuṟṟuyir iraṇṭum illāta moḻikaḷum palavuḷa. pala oliyaṉkaḷukkuc cilavaṭivaṉkaḷai vaittuk koṇṭu carikkaṭṭi vaḷarum moḻikaḷum palavuḷa. āṅkilattil paṉṉūṟāyiram col paṭittālum ovvoṉṟaiyum eppaṭi olippatu, eppaṭi eḻutuvatu eṉṟu taṉittaṉiyāṉa ciṟappuppayiṟci kaṭaicivarai vēṇṭum. epporuḷukkup periya eḻuttuppōṭuvatu eṉṟa niṉaivu vēṇṭum. āṅkilattil 104 eḻuttukkaḷ toṭakkattilēyē varaiya vēṇṭum. urōma eṇkaḷiṉ amaippu (āūāōṭuaiē - 1944) piṉmuṉ nōkkiyatallavā? intuṇaik kuṟai pāṭuḷḷum āṟṟal vāynta oḻuṅkiyal moḻitōṟum uṇṭu eṉpatai uṇarvōmāka. ammoḻi vaḷarvataṟku anta uḷḷoḻuṅku marapu iṭaṅkoṭuttuk koṇṭiruk kiṉṟatu eṉpataṉaiyum uṇarvōmāka.
moḻikaḷiṭai oliyaṉ eṇṇikkaiyum variyaṉ eṇṇikkaiyum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. coṟcērkkaiyum toṭaramaippum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. evvaḷavu vēṟum māṟum eṉṟu tōṉṟiṉālum kuḻantaikaḷum kallārum cevivaḻip payiṉṟu tārāḷamākap pēcukiṉṟaṉar. uḷḷē oru marapu vāypāṭu kiṭakkiṉṟatu eṉpatu teḷivu. ātaliṉ nam aḷavukōlum aḷakkumuṟaiyum iṉṉum collappōṉāl marapāḻum aṟiyāmaiyumē kuṟaiyuṭaiyaṉa. vaḷarnta cemmoḻi kaḷai avaṟṟiṉ marapaḻuttam kaṇṭu attaṭaṅkaḷil putiya pāttipiṭittu vaḷarkka vēṇṭum. māṟṟamum tiripum iyalpukku viṭṭuviṭa vēṇṭum.

āṅkilamoḻi ulaka moḻiyākap parantōṅki yirukkum vāyppu neṟikaḷait tamiḻpparappukku nām kaṭaippiṭikkak kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. evvakai moḻiyum amaippiṉ ciṟappālum poṟikkaruviyiṉ tuṇaiyālum ulaka moḻiyāki viṭavillai. āṭcimoḻi yākavum kalvimoḻiyākavum araṅku moḻiyākavum ceytimoḻiyākavum nūlmoḻiyākavum ellāt tuṟaiyilum āṅkila vāyilkaḷ virintaṉa. āṅkilavāṭci perukiya piṟaticai nāṭukaḷilum āṅkilamē mēṉmoḻi yāyiṟṟu. ciṟiya nāṭṭukkuriyatāka irunta āṅkilam ñālamoḻiyāṉataṟkup perunāṭāṉa aṭimaippāratam peruṅ kāraṇamākum. ciṟanta tol palamoḻikaḷaik koṇṭa intiya makkaḷ tattam vaḷañcāṉṟa tāymoḻiyai ellā vaḻiyālum nalkuravuppaṭutti, āṅkilattaiyē nāṭi vaḷarttatum āṅkilam ulaka moḻiyāṉataṟkup peruvāyp pāyiṟṟu. itu irunūṟāṇṭukkatai. ikkataiyai virittup payaṉillai.
āṅkilēyariṭamiruntu nām nāṭṭurimai mīṭṭōm; eṉiṉum nam moḻikaḷ nammiṭaruntu urimai peṟa muṭiya villai. viṭutalai peṟṟu muppatteṭṭāṇṭukaḷ ākiyum nam patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷil oṉṟukūṭa āṅkilattukkup pakkamāka vaḷaravillai. nam nāṭṭuppaṟṟu aiyakō ittakaittu. aṅṅaṉam āṅkilattai vaḷarttuviṭṭa intiyarkaḷuḷ orupakutiyāṉa tamiḻarkaḷ āṅkilam vaḷarnta ceyal neṟikaḷaip pārāmal, tamiḻmoḻiyai āṅkila amaippukkuc cetukki otukkip pitukki koṇṭuvara muṉaikiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻukkēṟṟa arumpoṟikaḷ kāṇa aṟiviyal muṉaippuk koḷḷāmal, elikkūṇṭiṟkuḷ puliyai aṭakkamuyalvatupōla, etaṟkō kaṇṭa accuppatippup poṟikaḷuḷ tamiḻiṉ iyaṟkaiyai oṭukkat tuṭikkiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkumēl itutāṉ vaḷarcci eṉṟu aṭāta vāḻttuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. teḷintu colliṉ ippōk kellām kaiviṭa vēṇṭiyavai eṉpatu eṉ karuttu.
cintuveḷi nākarikattukku muṟpaṭṭē eḻuttuvaṭivu peṟṟut tiṇmai eytiya nam tamiḻukkuc conta eḻuttē yillai; ituvum oru moḻiyā eṉṟu piṟkālattu vacaiyuṇṭākumpaṭi ippōkkuk koṇṭupōyviṭumō eṉṟu kavalkiṉṟēṉ. tamiḻ taṉimoḻi. emmoḻiyiliruntum piṟavāta taṉimoḻi, pala moḻikaḷait tōṟṟiya tāymoḻi, cevili moḻi eṉṟa uṇmaip perumai aṇmaik kālattuttāṉ putaiporuḷ pōla veḷippaṭṭu varum innaṉṉilaiyil matippiḻakkum oru tavaṟu nām ceyyalāmā? taṉmāṉam ceyya iṭaṅkoṭukkumā?
āṭat teriyātavaḷ muṟṟam kōṇal eṉpatu pōlavum, pāṭatteriyātavaḷ avaiyōr eṇṇikkai kūṭutal eṉpatu pōlavum, ōṭatteriyātavaṉ uḷḷūr eṉpatu pōlavum cilariṉ tiruttamuyaṟcikaḷ namakkut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. vallavaṉukkup pullum āyutam eṉpataṉai āṅkila moḻiyuṇartti viṭṭatu. ātaliṉ tamiḻ eḻuttu varivaṭivu paṟṟip piṉvarum teḷivukaḷaiyum uṟutikaḷaiyum ceyaṟpāṭukaḷaiyum nām koḷvōmāka.
(1) uḷḷa eḻuttu vaṭivaṅkaḷai nilaipēṟāka vaittu ataṟkēṟṟa accuppoṟikaḷai nuṇṇiya aṟiviyal muṟaiyil kāṇa vēṇṭum. viraintu kālakkōṭpaṭṭu māṟum poṟikaṭku nilaiyāṉa tamiḻmoḻiyai aṭimaippaṭuttak kūṭātu. muṉpōla iyalpāṉa vaḻakkāṟṟil varum māṟutalkaḷ vantu pōkaṭṭum valinta iyakkamāṉa eḻuttuppuraṭṭu vēṇṭā. aṅṅaṉam ceyvataṟkut tamiḻ putiya moḻiyō tiruntā moḻiyōyaṉṟu. eḻuttuvaṭivaṅkaḷ mutalāvatākak kaiyeḻuttukkum eḷitāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa potu nilaiyaip pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. poṟivaḻakkāṟṟiṉum kaivaḻakkāṟē moḻikku uyirppuṭaiyatu.
(2) tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ oru cumaiyalla. itu uṇmai, veṟum mayakkaṉṟu. vaḻivaḻi vanta tamiḻkkalvi muṟaippaṭi kuḻantaikaṭkuc collik koṭuttāl ellām eḷimaiyē. taṭamvāynta varivaṭivaṅkaḷait tiruttattirunta avai kuḻantaikaṭkup paḻaiyavaṟṟōṭu cērntu kūṭutalāki viṭukiṉṟaṉa. itu muraṇāṉa viḷaivu. vīṭṭil tamiḻ, pālar paḷḷiyil tamiḻ, palkalaikkaḻakam varai tamiḻ, āṭciyil tamiḻ, kōyilil tamiḻ, eṉṟa icaiyil tamiḻ, kaṭaiyil tamiḻ, uriya aṉaittilum tamiḻ eṉṟa urimainilai tamiḻukkut tamiḻakattu irukka vēṇṭum; itu iruntuviṭumāṉāl tamiḻ pōla iṉiya eḷiya moḻi tamiḻarukku vēṟoṉṟillai.
eḻuttut tiruttam kuṟitta ōru kālacceytiyai ittuṇai mukamākac colliyataṟkuk kāraṇam. tamiḻum tamiḻkkalviyum tamiḻkkāppum vaḻivaḻit toṭarcciyum iruntāṟṟāṉē tolkāppiyam caṅkavilakkiyam tirukkuṟaḷ cilappatikāram pāratiyam mutalāka ellām vāḻum; tamiḻiṉattukku naṉmatippum etirkālat taṉittaṉmaiyum uṇṭākum. ātaliṉ kālattiṟkēṟṟa ivvuraiyil tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivam paṟṟit tamiḻ makkaṭkut teḷivu ceyvatu eṉ ceṅkaṭamaiyāyiṟṟa.
ceytakka valla ceyakkeṭum ceytakka
ceyyāmai yāṉum keṭum
eṉṟār poyyil pulavar. eḻuttut tiruttam pōṉṟavai tamiḻukkuc ceyyat takātavai; eṅkum ellām mutaliṭam eṉpavai tamiḻukkuc ceyyat takkavai.
palvēṟu olinilaikkūṟukaḷaiyum cila aṟiviyal nuṭpaṅkaḷaiyum mayakkamaṟakkāṭṭutaṟku avvappōtu nuṇkuṟiyīṭukaḷ vēṇṭappaṭum. ivai uyarkalvikkum uyarāyvukkum vēṇṭiya ciṟappukkuṟikaḷ; varumiṭaṅkaḷum payaṉkaḷum vēṟāṉavai. ivai nām mēlē kūṟiya eḻuttu vaḻakkāṟalla; aṟivu vaḻakkāṟu.
nūṟpā 20
`meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉilai eṉpataṟku oru mey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkutal meymmayakkam; oru mey taṉ meyyōṭu tāṉ mayaṅkutalai uṭaṉilai mayakkam eṉa iruvakaippaṭum eṉṟu poruḷuraippa. avvāṟu iruvakai yeṉak kūṟuvatu karuttāyiṉ, “āyiriyala vuyirtta lāṟē” “meyyēyuyireṉa ṟāyī riyala” “āyīriyala puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē” eṉṟa naṭai amaintirukkum. teriyuṅkālai eṉpataṟku meymmayaṅkuṭaṉilai ceyappaṭuporuḷākum.
ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ
meymmayaṅ kuṭaṉilai teriyuṅ kālai
eṉṟa innūṟpāviṉ mutalīraṭikaḷaip piṉvarum meymmayakka nūṟpākkaṭ kellām tōṟṟuvāyākak koḷka.
tolkāppiyam
ciṟappuppāyiramum nūṉmarapum māṇikkavuraiyum muṟṟum.


iraṇṭāvatu
moḻimarapu - māṇikkavurai
iyalmuṉṉurai
nūṉmarapil eḻuttu nilaiyil taṉiyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ kūṟappeṟṟaṉa. ikkuṟikaḷ curukkamāka āḷutaṟkamainta āṭcic coṟkaḷākum. ivveḻuttukkaḷ poruḷtarum collākic collil mutalilō iṟutiyilō niṟkum nilaipaṟṟi moḻimarapu kūṟum. coṉṉilaiyil eḻuttukkaḷ paṭum ilakkaṇaṅkūṟutaliṉ moḻimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu. iḵtu eḻuttatikāram ātaliṉ moḻimarapu eṉpataṟkuc collil varum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ marapunilai eṉapporuḷ koḷka.
oli, eḻuttu, col eṉpaṉa pañcu, nūliḻai, āṭai pōṉṟavai. arici cōṟākum pōtutāṉ uṇpayaṉ tarum. eḻuttoli eṉṟa oliyaṉukkup poruṇmaiyuṇṭu. avvaḷavē. atuvē oru poruḷ kāṭṭātu. oliyaṉ ciṟupāṉmai taṉiyākavum perumpāṉmai eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarntum poruḷai nēraṭiyākak kuṟikkumpōtu col allatu moḻi eṉappaṭum. eḻuttukkum collukkum uriya toṭarpu kurutiyōṭṭamum nīrōṭṭamum okkum. col, moḻi, kiḷavi eṉṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ makkaḷ vaḻakkiṟ pēcappaṭum uyirppu moḻiyaik kuṟikkum.
eḻuttu col eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ mutaṟkaṇ olinilaikku uriyavai. ā eṉṟa eḻutatai olikka, āṭu eṉṟa collaic colluka eṉpatuvē oli mutaṉmarapu. ā eṉṟa eḻutatai eḻutuka. āṭu eṉṟa collai eḻutuka eṉpatu piṉ vanta varivaṭiva marapu. immarapum poruntum; eṉiṉum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ olinilaikkē uriyaṉa eṉpatai maṟavātu niṉaivu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
iyaṟ pakuppukkaḷ tammuḷ toṭarpuṭaiyavai. ōriyalukkuk kūṭutalāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷai aṭutta viyalilō piṉṉiyalkaḷilō āciriyaṉ colluvāṉ. itu malaippil neṟi eṉappaṭum. ōriyalil aṉaittaiyum colliviṭutal eṉpatu uṇṇum uṇavaṉaittaiyum mutalilēyē ilaiyiṟ pōṭṭuviṭuvatai yokkum.
nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiya cārpeḻuttukkaḷ, aḷapeṭai, meymmayakkam, varivaṭivu paṟṟiya mēlviḷakkaṅkaḷ moḻimarapiṉ toṭakkattu varuvaṉa.
moḻimarapiṟkē yuriya iṉṟiyamaiyāta īrilakkaṇaṅkaḷ moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷum moḻiyiṟuti yeḻuttukkaḷum ām. moḻivaralāṟṟiṭai ivaṟṟil cila varavum cila pōkkum ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa; ēṟpaṭuvatum iyalpē. eṉiṉum immāṟṟaṅkaḷ talaitaṭṭalō taraitaṭṭalō vārāmal viḻippākap pōṟṟik koḷḷattakuvaṉa. karai palliṭattu uṭaintāl pukuvatum pōvatum etu eṉṟu colla muṭiyumā? ellā moḻikaḷuḷḷum paḻantamiḻmoḻi ulaka vaḻakku aḻintoḻintu citaiyāmal cīriḷamait tiṟam kuṉṟāmal iṉṟum putumoḻiyāka vāḻvataṟku uriya kāraṇaṅkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu moḻi mutaleḻuttuk kaṭṭuppāṭum moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttuk kaṭṭuppāṭum ākum. piṉ iyalkaḷil varampupaṭuttum puṇarcciyellām inta irutaḷaṅkaḷiṉ mēl eḻuvaṉa. ittaḷam taḷariṉ tamiḻ taḷarum. ātaliṉ mutalīṟu eṉṟa amaippu tamiḻmoḻikku nalloḻukkamākum; cevviḻuppamumākum; eṉavē immoḻiyuyiriṉaic cevvaṉam ōmputal ilakkaṇattiṉ kaṭamai.
mutalum iṟutiyum muṭṭātu kākkum
atukāṇ tamiḻiṉ araṇ.
iyaṟkaruttu
kuṟṟiyalikaram `miyā’ eṉa oru moḻiyilum, vīṭiyātu nāṭiyātu eṉap puṇarmoḻiyilum varum. orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram āṟuvalleḻuttiṉ mēl ēṟi, nāṭu, ulaku, vāttu, pañcu, pāmpu, oṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu aṟuvakaiyāka varum. cukkukkōṭu eṉap puṇarmoḻiyilum varum eḵku, aḵtu, kaḵcu eṉṟavāṟu orumoḻi āytam kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉṉum uyirvalleḻuttukku muṉṉum varum. aḵṟiṇai, paḵṟuḷi eṉṟavāṟu puṇarmoḻiyilum varum. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil āytam moḻikkuṟippāka nīṇṭu eḻutappaṭuvatillai. nūṉmarapiṟ kūṟiya cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟum evvāṟu cārntu tōṉṟum eṉṟa cārpu nilaiyai moḻimarapil mutaleḻu nūṟpākkaḷ viḷakkuvaṉa.
icai kuṉṟumpōtu aḷapeṭai eṉṟa muṟai taḻuvap paṭumaṉṟē. aṅṅaṉam aḷapu eḻunta oli nīṭṭattaik kāṭṭuvataṟku neṭṭeḻuttukkup piṉ ataṟku otta kuṟṟeḻuttu (pōoy) pakkamāka eḻutappaṭum. ai, oḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭu neṭilukkum nēraṭiyāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttu illāmai yāl aikārattukku ikaramum oḷakārattiṟku ukaramum otta kuṟṟeḻuttāka aḷapeṭaiyiṟ koḷḷavēṇṭum.
tamiḻccoṟkaḷ ōreḻuttorumoḻi (ā, mā) eṉavum īreḻuttōru moḻi (āṉ,māṉ) eṉavum iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttuṭaiya toṭarmoḻi (āṇṭu, āṇṭāḷ, āṇṭavar) eṉavum mūṉṟu vakaippaṭum. ippākupāṭu tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattaiyum amaippaiyum ākkattaiyum aṟivikkavallatu.
mey mutaleḻuttāyiṉum piṟappil taṉittaṉmai yuṭaiya tāyiṉum colluṅkāl ka ṅa ca ña eṉṟu akarattōṭu collappaṭum.
nūṉmarapil iṉṉa meyyeḻuttukkuppiṉ iṉṉa meyyeḻuttu varal vēṇṭum eṉṟa varampiṉaik kaṟṟōm. enta meyyeḻuttum taṉṉaik kūṟumpōtu etaṟkup piṉṉum varalām eṉṟa kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa potu nilaiyuṇṭu. itu naṭaimuṟaikkotta ilakkaṇam.
meymmayakkattil pīrkku, vārppu, pāḻṅkiṇaṟu eṉa īroṟṟum varum. ivai ya ra ḻa eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉ piṉṉar tōṉṟum.
tamiḻc collamaippiṉpiṭi rakaravoṟṟum ḻakaravoṟṟum taṉikkuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉ varamāṭṭā. varkkam, varṇaṉai, karppiṇi eṉṟu colvatum eḻutuvatum tavaṟu. varukkam, varuṇaṉai, karuppiṇi eṉpatē takum.
ceyyuḷil `pōṉm eṉṟu īroṟṟu niṟkum. avviṭattu makaravoṟṟu kālmāttiraiyāyk kuṉṟi yolikkum.
eḻuttukkaḷaic colliṭaippaṭuttic coṉṉālum, colliṭai vitantu vēṟākac coṉṉālum, kūṭṭu vaṭivu peṟṟālum tam taṉmaiyiṉiṉṟu māṟupaṭā. taṉiyeḻuttāka iruntapōtu eṉṉa taṉmaiyuṇṭō attaṉmai vantāṉ iruntāṉ ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟu eḻuttukkūṭṭic colvataṟkāka māṟiviṭātu. vaṭivu māṟupaṭṭālum poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu. itu eḻuttiyal eṉappaṭum. itu moḻiyiṉ nilai viti. ai eṉṟa oliyaṉukkut taṉivarivaṭivu uṇṭu. ai ay eṉṟa kūṭṭu vaṭivukaḷum uṇṭu. ai ay eṉpaṉa cilaviṭaṅkaḷil māṟi varum. oḷa oliyaṉukku uriya vaṭivu uṇṭu. au eṉṟa kūṭṭu vaṭivum uṇṭu. ivai vaṭivu mayakkaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟāl poruḷ māṟupaṭuvatillai. eḻuttiyal tirivatillai.
moḻimarapiṉ mutaṉmaiyāṉa ilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷ iraṇṭē. tamiḻil moḻikku mutalākum eḻuttukkaḷ; moḻikku iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷ.
moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ
itu paṟṟip pattu nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. 12 uyirkaḷum moḻi mutalāki varum. meykaḷ moḻimutalākum eṉṟālum uyirōṭu kūṭiya uyir meykaḷtām aṅṅaṉam varamuṭiyum. itu tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ maṉṉiya amaippu. ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷai orukāl taḻuviṉālum, ivvamaippukku uṭpaṭṭē tiyāṉam, niyāyam, kirupai eṉṟu varavēṇṭum.
mutalāku meykaḷukkum avaipeṟum uyirkaḷukkum varampuṇṭu. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷuḷ ka ca ta pa eṉa valliṉattil nāṉkum ña na ma eṉa melliṉattil mūṉṟum ya va eṉa iṭaiyiṉattil iraṇṭum āka oṉpatu meykaḷ moḻi mutalākum. ivaṟṟuḷ,
12 uyiroṭum varuvaṉa 		k t n p m 
9 a ai oḷa nīṅka 			c
8 u ū o ō nīṅka 			v
3 ā e o cērntu 			ñ
1 ā cērntu	 			y
meymmayakkattiṟkuc colliyatupōla mutalākā eṉappaṭṭa ellā veḻuttukkaḷumē taṉṉaiccuṭṭum pōtu (ṇo, ḷo, ṭo eṉṟavāṟu) moḻimutalāka varum eṉpatu naṭaimuṟaikku otta potuviti. ituvē moḻiyiṟutiyākavum koḷḷappaṭum.
nuntai, nuntāy eṉa moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟu uṇṭu. itaṟkum nakara meyyē mutal.
moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷ
itu paṟṟip patiṉāṉku nūṟpā uṇṭu.
oḷakāram nīṅkiya patiṉōruyirum iṟutiyāka varum. oḷakāramum kau veḷa eṉa ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷil īṟākum. ekara vuyir īṟākātu. no eṉṟa ōriṭattu okaram īṟām. ñē ñō eṉa ñakaram īrāka vārātu. ucu, mucu eṉa īriṭattuc cukaram īṟām. tapu eṉṟa oru collil pukaram īrāki irunilaiyil aḻuttam peṟum. patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷuḷ ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉa melliṉattuḷ aintu ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉa iṭaiyiṉam āṟu ākap patiṉoru puḷḷiyum moḻikku īṟāka varum. verin, porun eṉa irucoṟkaḷil nakaramey īṟām. uriñ eṉṟu ñakaram oru collil īṟām āṉāl iruporuḷ aḻuttam peṟuvatillai. aviv uv tev eṉa vakaram nāṉku coṟkaḷil kaṭaiciyāka varum.
aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷil ṉakarameykku īṟāka makaramey (puṟaṉ - puṟam) vantuniṟkum; eṉiṉum oṉpatu coṟkaḷ vilakkākum.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram
(31)	kuṟṟiya likaram niṟṟal vēṇṭuma
	yāveṉ ciṉaimicai yuraiyacaik kiḷavik
	kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē.
iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ cārpunilai kūṟum.
akalavurai
cārntu varalmarapiṉ eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṉ mutalnūṟ pāviṟ kūṟiṉāraṉṟē. kuṟukutaṟku eccollil etaṉaic cārntu varum eṉa uriya iṭaccūḻnilai kāṭṭukiṉṟār.
miyā eṉṟa uraiyacaiyiṭaic col oṉṟu uṇṭu. itaṉ kaṇ yā eṉṟa eḻuttukku mēlē avviṭattu makarameyyaic cārntu kuṟṟiyalikaram naṭuvē niṟkum.
urai - pēccu vaḻakku. niṟṟal vēṇṭum - iṭaippaṭṭu naṭuvē niṟka vēṇṭum.
vaḻakku: miyā - itu pēccu vaḻakkiṭai muṉṉilaiyil varum ēvaṟ col. kēṇmiyā, coṉmiyā, kēḷappā, collappā eṉpatu poruḷ.
tiṟaṉurai
nūṟpāvil eṭuttukkāṭṭē tantu ilakkaṇam viḷakkum nūṟpā itu. itu kāṭṭuneṟi eṉappaṭum. ākārattōṭu kūṭiya yakaravuyirmeykkum makaravoṟṟukkum iṭaippaṭṭu nerukkuṇṇumpōtu ikaram araimāttiraiyākak kuṟukum eṉpatu cūḻviti. yā eṉṟa ākāravuyirmey muḻutum piṉpaṟṟukkōṭu; oṟṟu muṉpaṟṟukkōṭu eṉka. ivvitiyai yoṭṭi viyāḻaṉ eṉṟa collil ikaram ikkālattuk kuṟukuvatai uṇaralām. viyāti, viyācam, tiyāṉam, piyāṇṭu vāttiyam eṉṟa piṟamoḻi varukaiyilum iccūḻnilaiyāl ikaram kuṟukuvataik kāṇka. eṉavē iccūḻviti ulakamoḻip potuviti pōlum.
coṟku uṟuppāka varum olikaḷaic ciṉai eṉpatu oru kuṟiyīṭu. kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉai eṉa uyir mayaṅkiyalilum cuṭṭuc cuṉai nīṭiya ‘īṟuciṉaiyoḻiya eṉa’ īriṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyalilum ikkuṟiyāṭci varum.
`kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟa oru talainaṭai ippaṭic cārpu iruntāṟṟāṉ ikaram kuṟukum eṉṟa vaṉpuṟaiyākum. miyā miyā eṉṟu varum pūṉaiyiṉ poruḷil olikkuṟippai nīkkutaṟku. uraiyacaik kiḷavi eṉap paṭṭatu. payaṉilaiyai muṉṉum eccattaip piṉṉum kūṟiya naṭaiyōṭṭam itu. ilakkiyampōl tolkāppiyattu ivvāṟu naṭaiyōṭṭaṅkaḷ palavuḷa.
miyā eṉṟa uraiyacaic col piṉṉāḷ vaḻakku vīḻntatu.
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram
(32)	puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalu murittē
	uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum.
itu kuṟṟiyalikaram irumoḻippuṇarcciyiṭaik kuṟukum eṉakkūṟum.
akalavurai
kuṟṟiyalikaram miyā eṉpatu pōl oru moḻiyiṟ kuṟukutalōṭu irucoṟpuṇarcciyil kuṟukutalum uṇṭu. moḻimarapu oru collaḷaviṟkuk kūṟum iyalātaliṉ īṇṭu virinturaippatu poruntātu. ippuṇarmoḻik kuṟukkam piṉpu kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil veḷippaṭum.
vaḻakku: nāṭu + yātu - nāṭiyātu; mācu + yātu - māciyātu
tiṟaṉurai
cārpeḻuttukkāṉa kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytameṉṟa mūṉṟum oru collilum varum; puṇarmoḻi yilum varum. moḻimarapu oru collil varum eḻuttukkaṭku ilakkaṇam kūṟum iyalpiṟṟu ātaliṉ puṇarmoḻiyuḷ cārpeḻutta varuvaṉa paṟṟi avai uṇṭu eṉa īṇṭuc cuṭṭiṉārēyaṉṟi iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum viritturaikka villai tolkāppiyar. `uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum eṉap puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiya likarattukkum, `kaṭappāṭaṟinta puṇariyalāṉa’ eṉap puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattukkum, `īriyal maruṅkiṉum’ eṉap puṇarmoḻi āytattukkum uriya vēṟiyalkaḷiṟ kāṇka eṉṟār āciriyar. ivvāṟu ilakkaṇat teḷivupaṭa nūṉmuṟai vakuttiruppataiyē `mayaṅkāmarapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi’ eṉap pāyiram pārāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
“yakaram varuvaḻi yikaraṅ kuṟukum
  ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu” 
eṉavarum
kuṟṟiyalukara nūṟpāvaittāṉ `uṇarakkūṟiṉ piṉṉart tōṉṟum’ eṉpatu cuṭṭum; eṉavē oru moḻik kuṟṟiyalukāc coṟkaḷ nilaimoḻiyāka niṟka varumoḻi yakaram yakaram puṇarumpōtu kuṟukiya ikaram tōṉṟum eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. yakaram varumoḻi eṉṟu kūṟiṉālum, `āvōṭavallatu yakaram mutalātu’ eṉṟapaṭi, yā eṉṟa ākāravuyir meyyē koḷḷappaṭum.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum cūḻnilaiyil ōr oṟṟumaiyuṇṭu. miyā nāṭiyātu eṉa īriṭattum yā eṉṟa olikkumuṉ ikaram kuṟukukiṉṟatu.
muṉṉar eṉpatu etirkālattaik kuṟikkum. muṟpōkku eṉpatu pōla, `puṇariyal nilaiyiṭaik kuṟukalum urittē eṉṟa ummaikkuk kuṟukāmalum varum eṉpatu poruḷaṉṟu. oru moḻiyilēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyilum kuṟurum eṉṟa eccavummaiyākum. `iṭaṉumāruṇṭē eṉṟu varum nūṟpā naṭaikkum ituvē poruḷ.
īṇṭu orunal viḷakkam
nāṭiyatu eṉṟa puṇarmoḻi nāṭu + yātu eṉavum nāṭi + yātu eṉavum pirippataṟku iṭaṉākiṉṟatu. vēṟupāṭu kāṇpatu eppaṭi? nāṭu eṉpatu nilaimoḻiyāyiṉ avviṭattu ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaramāy araimāttiraiyāy olikkum. eḻuttuvaṭivil nāṭiyātu eṉa ikarattiṉ mēl puḷḷipeṟum. nāṭi eṉpatu nilai moḻiyāyiṉ ikaram kuṟṟukaramāy oru māttirai icaikkum. nāṭi yātu eṉa eḻuttu vaṭivil puḷḷipeṟātu. emmoḻiyilum ittakaiya mayakkaṅkaṭku iṭaṉuṇṭeṉiṉum pēccuc cūḻnilai nōkkip poruḷtāṉē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. māṭiyātu vaṇṭi yātu, kāciyātu, kokkiyātu eṉpaṉavum attaṉmaiyaṉa. tūciyātu eṉṟa puṇarcciyil tūcum tūciyum oru poruḷaṉavātaliṉ ilakkaṇattāl poruḷ vēṟupaṭavillai eṉa aṟika.
ivvilakkaṇam piṟkālattu vaḻakku vīḻntatu.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram
(33) 	neṭṭeḻut timparum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum
	kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.
iḵtu orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ cārpunilai kūṟum.
akalavurai
`cārntuvaral marapiṉ eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiya varicaippaṭi kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟukutaṟku evaṟṟaic cārntuvarum eṉakkāṭṭukiṉṟār. kuṟṟiyalukaram taṉi neṭṭeḻuttukkup 	piṉ āṟu valleḻuttukkaḷ mēl ēṟivarum. toṭar moḻikku iṟutiyil atupōl āṟu valleḻuttukkaḷaic cārntu varum.
impar - piṉpu. toṭarmoḻi - eḻuttukkaḷ toṭarnta oru col vallāṟu - ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṟa valliṉam.
vaḻukku: pōku, mācu, āṭu,  ōtu, kaipu, āṟu - neṭil
	pōkku: vīccu, āṭṭu, cāttu, kāppu, māṟṟu - vaṉṟoṭar
	aṅku, añcu, aṇṭu, antu, ampu, aṉṟu - meṉṟōṭar
	eytu, mārpu, palku, māḻku, māḷku - iṭaittoṭar
	eḵku, kaḵcu, paḵtu - āytattoṭar
	paḻaku, tarācu, taviṭu, pātīṭu, kaḻuku, vallūṟu, colleṭu, nāḷēṭu, niṟaipu. - uyirttoṭar.
tiṟaṉurai
kuṟṟiyalukaram, neṭil, uyirttoṭar, vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar, iṭaittoṭar, āytat toṭar eṉa āṟu vakaippaṭum eṉak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaṟkaṇ vakaiceyvar āciriyar ivvakaikkēṟpap puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutalai av viyaliṟ kāṇka. moḻimarapu eṉṟa ivviyalil kuṟṟiyalukaram cārpeḻuttākum moḻic cūḻnilaiyē kūṟuvar.
tolkāppiyattaik kaṟpōr `toṭarmoḻi’ eṉṟa kuṟiyīṭṭiṉai mayaṅkātu viḷaṅkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. iraṇṭeḻuttukku mēl toṭarntu orucol eṉpatu poruḷ. makaṉ, pāṭṭu, piṇaṅku, vaḷḷuvar, tolkāppiyar ivaiyellām toṭarmoḻikaḷ ā, vā, pō, tī ivai ōreḻuttorumoḻi eṉappaṭum. aṭai, atu. nām kācu ivai īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉappaṭum. piṟkālattum ikkālattum toṭarmoḻi eṉpatu coṟkaḷ toṭarnta naṭaiyaik kuṟikkum. citamparaṉār cekkiḻuttār; citamparaṉār ciṟaiccālaiyil nāṭṭu viṭutalaikkākac cekkiḻuttār; pāratiyār ivar perumaiyaip pāṭiṉār. ivaiyellām toṭarmoḻi eṉa ikkālattup peyarpeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvēṟṟumaiyaik karuttuṭkoṇṭu tolkāppiyattiṟaṉaik kaṟkavēṇṭum. nām iṉṟu toṭarmoḻi eṉpataṉait tolkāppiyar puṇarmoḻi eṉpar. itaṉmēl viḷakkattai `ōreḻuttoru’ moḻi eṉap piṉvarum nūṟpāvuraiyiṟ kāṇka.
neṭṭeḻuttimpar eṉpatu nāṭu. pēca, kātu mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum. ivai  īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷ. toṭarmoḻiyīṟu eṉpatu paṭam. paṇṭu, mārpu, eḵku, pārppu, kūppāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum. ivai iraṇṭukkum mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttuṭaic coṟkaḷ. ataṉālaṉṟō orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉappaṭṭaṉa. `neṭṭeḻuttimpar eṉṟataṉāl atu. paṭu, pacu eṉat taṉik kuṟṟeḻuttāl iṟuvaṉa kuṟṟiyalukaram ākā eṉpatum `vallāṟu ūrntu’ eṉpataṉāl tummu, maṇṇu, tāvu, puḷḷu, celavu, varavu eṉpaṉa mellīṟum iṭaiyīṟum koḷvatāl kuṟṟiyalukaram ākā eṉpatum teḷiyappaṭum. ātaliṉ innūṟpā vilakkiyuṇarttum naṭaiyuṭaiyatu eṉakkoḷka.
innūṟpāvil payaṉilai illai. niṟṟalvēṇṭum eṉṟa muntaikku muntiya nūṟpāt toṭaraip payaṉilaiyāka māṭṭēṟṟal iyaipaṉṟu. tōṉṟā eḻuvāy pōla aritākat tōṉṟāp payaṉilaiyum koḷḷalām. naṉṉūlārum pataviyalil `āyīṟaiyum īyīṟikaramum’ eṉṟu muṭiyātu muṭittiruttalai oppunōkkuka. `ellām maḻai’ eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ naṭaiyum itaṉpāṟpaṭum. itaṉai avāynilai eṉpar ilakkaṇiyar.
iṉṉoru vakaiyākavum immuṭipiṉaic cintikkalām. malaiyāḷattil viṉai muṟṟukkaḷ pālīṟukaḷ peṟuvatillai. paṇṭu tamiḻil vaḻakkilirunta viṉaimuṟṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷ piṉpu tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟu iṉṟu malaiyāḷattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kēraḷapāṇiṉīyam eṭuttakkāṭṭiyapaṭi, vallāṟu ūrntu eṉpataṉai pālīṟupeṟā muṟṟākavum karutikkoḷḷa iṭaṉuṇṭu. itu cintaṉaikkuriyatu.
iṉṟu evvakaic coṟkaḷilum iṟutiyukaram ōraḷavēṉum kuṟukiyolippataik kēṭkiṉṟōm; eṉiṉum puṇarccikkaṇ muṉpōlavē vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa.
puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaram
(34)	iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.
itu kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻippuṇarcciyiṭaik kuṟukum eṉṟu kūṟum.
akalavurai
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaṭku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum mēlē kūṟiṉār. accoṟkaḷ nilaimoḻiyāka varumoḻiyāka puṇarumpōtu ukaram iṭaippaṭumallavā? avvāṟu varum ukaram kuṟukum iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu; avaṟṟai muṟaiyākac colla vēṇṭiya kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaliṉ kaṇṇē kāṇalām.
iṭaippaṭiṉ 	-	naṭuvē variṉ.
kaṭappāṭu	-	collattakum iṭam. 	āṉ - iṭattu.			vaḻakku 
nāṭu ciṟakkum, aḻaku vaḷarum	-	alvaḻittoṭar
nāṭṭuk kaṭamai, aḻakuk kalai	-	vēṟṟumaittokai
tiṟaṉurai
itu moḻimarapātaliṉ oru moḻiyaḷavukkē ilakkaṇam kūṟappaṭum. puṇarmoḻikku uriyaviyalkaḷiṟ kūṟuvatē kaṭappāṭu. ātaliṉ, `kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyal eṉṟār. aḵtu oṉpatām iyalāṉa kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal ākum.
`allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
ellā viṟutiyum ukaram niṟaiyum
eṉa āṅku varum nūṟpāvē īṇṭuk kuṟikkap peṟuvatu eṉak koḷka.
nāṭu ciṟantatu eṉpatil kuṟṟiyalukaram īṟāka, iruntālum puṇarmoḻiyil naṭuvēyiruttaliṉ `iṭaippaṭiṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. 
`iṭaṉumāruṇṭē' eṉpatu arukivaruvataik kuṟikka villai. kuṟṟiyalukaram oru moḻiyil iruppatu pōlap puṇarmoḻivilum uṇṭu eṉpatē poruḷ. iḵtu eccavummai.
orumoḻiyāytam
(35)	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar āytap puḷḷi
	uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē.
iḵtu orumoḻiyāy tattiṉ cārpu nilaikūṟum.
akalavurai
`cārntu varal marapiṉmūṉṟu' eṉṟa varicaippaṭi āytam kūṟukiṉṟār. āytappuḷḷi kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkuppiṉṉē uyirmey valleḻuttukkaḷukku muṉṉē varum.
micaintu - iṭappakkamāka aṭuttatu.
vaḻakku: (e.ku) (eḵku) ka.cu (kaḵcu) a.tu (aḵtu) pa. ṟi (paḵṟi)
tiṟaṉurai
āytattiṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum varum irupāṟ cūḻnilai kūṟuvatu innūṟpā. itaṉ amaippuppaṭi oru moḻiyāytam e.ku (eḵku) ve.kā (veḵkā) eṉa mūṉṟeḻut taḷavil aṭaṅkum. aḵtāvatu iraṇṭiṟanticaikkum toṭar moḻi vakaiyākum. aṟuvakaik kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ āytat toṭarmoḻiyum oṉṟu eṉpatai niṉaika.
muṉṉē kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉpataṉāl (e.ku) (eḵku) eṉpatu pōlat taṉikkuṟilākavō ka.cu. (kaḵcu) eṉpatu pōla uyirmeyk kuṟilākavō irukkalām; eṉiṉum ataṟkumuṉ iṉṉoru eḻuttut toṭarvatillai eṉpatu teḷivu.
`uyiroṭu puṇarnta varalāṟu' eṉpataṉāl āytattiṟkup piṉpu enta oṟṟeḻuttum vārātu eṉpatum valleḻuttu variṉum avai uyiroṭu cērnta uyir meyyākavē varum eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. uyiroṭu eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyamaiyāl ve.kā. (veḵkā) a.tai (aḵtai) pa.ṟi. (paḵṟi) eṉa entavuyirum toṭaralām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻiṟ palavuṇṭu. avaiyellām oru mutaleḻuttirukkak kūṭutalāka uṭaṉ varupavai. kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum mutaleḻuttākiya ikara vukara vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ mēlē puḷḷiyum peṟum. āytat tiṟkup puḷḷiyallatu mutaleḻuttu vaṭivam iṉmaiyiṉ, `āytappuḷḷi' eṉṟē peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu. iḵtu ōrāṭkuḻu eṉṟu collum iṉṟaiya vāḻkkai ottatu.
iṉṉoru viḷakkam: puḷḷikkuṟikaḷ niṟkum iṭaṅkaḷil vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. āytappuḷḷi eṉṟa eḻuvāykku `micaittu' eṉṟa oṉṟaṉpālviṉai muṟṟu iruttaliṉ, puḷḷi oṉṟē eṉpatum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum āṟaṉ micaittum eṉpataṉāl naṭuvē iṭappakkattu aṭuttu niṟkum eṉpatum pōtarum. kāṇka: e.ku. ka.cu a.tu. `tattamicaiḷ ottaṉa' eṉa nūṉ marapilum, `vakāra micaiyum' eṉappuḷḷi mayaṅkiyalilum `attum āṉmicai' eṉa avviyalilum iṭappakkattu aṭuttu eṉṟa poruḷpaṭa varutalaiyuṇaralām.
orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram oru collaḷavil varutaliṉ miyā eṉṟa accollaiyē nūṟpāvil vaittu viḷakkiṉār āciriyar. orumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramum orumoḻi yāytamum palacoṟkaḷ uṭaimaiyāṉ, ilakkaṇa vakaiyāl varu¨cūḻnilaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
āytattaic cilavāṇṭukaḷāka itaḻkaḷilum nūlkaḷilum viḷamparaṅkaḷilum eḻuttuvaḻakkil tavaṟākak kaiyāḷum oru tīya pōkkiṉaic cuṭṭik kāṭṭi iṭit turaittuk kaḷaiya virumpukiṉṟēṉ. iḷaitāka muḷmaram kaḷaiyāviṭṭāl ayaṟkaḷaikaḷ mēvit tamiḻnāṟṟukkaḷ iṭampeṟā.
kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉṉum uyirmeyvalleḻuttukku muṉṉum iraṇṭiṉ cārpāka āytavoli tōṉṟum. iru ciṟakeḻuppa eḻum uṭalatu pōl eṉṟu uraiyāciriyarkaḷ uvamai kūṟuvar. āytam nuṇukiya nalivōcaiyātaliṉ kuḻantaiyaik tūkkuvatupōla irukaikaḷālum eḻuppa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟē eḵku, paḵtu, veḵkāmai, aḵkāmai, aḵṟiṇai, paḵṟoṭai, paḵṟuḷi mutalāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ amaintuḷa.
iṉṟō āṟāvatu āṅkilavolikku nikarāka āytattai moḻimutalākak kaiyāḷum pōkku orupakaṭṭākap peruki varukiṉṟatu. pulliya ippōkkāl āṟāvatu oliyuṭaiya pala āṅkilac coṟkaḷ appaṭiyē tamiḻeṉap pukuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. viciṟi, kaṭai, nitiyakam, tiraippaṭam, nilaivaippu, cīṭṭukkaṭai, cutti, paṭipputavi, paṭivam, kōppu, kuḷirppoṟi, tūyālai, toḻiṟcālai, aḻaki, tantai. tīppeṭṭit toḻil, nitimuṭakkam eṉṟa poruḷuṭaiya āṅkilac coṟkaḷ ellām āytattai mutalākak koṇṭu oruvāṟu tamiḻeḻuttāl eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ivvoru olikkaḷaiyāl aṇmaikkālattut tamiḻukku viḷaiyum ūṟukaḷ pala. ivvāṟu ceṉṟāl āṅkilavakarātiyil āṟāvatu oliyuṭaiya eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷum āytamutalākat tamiḻakarātiyil ēṟumallavā? ēmāntāl vēṟu olikaḷum ēṟalāmeṉṟu patam pārkkumallavā? āṟāvatu oli iṭaiyilum iṟutiyilum varum āṅkilac coṟkaḷ kūṭa varavarak kalakkiṉṟaṉa. nalla tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṅkūṭa āytam iruppatāl āṅkila āṟāvatu oliyākap paṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl tamiḻākkam ceyvatu taṭaippaṭuvatōṭu iṉṟoru aṭimuraṇum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. eḻuttukku oli, collākum pōtu ōr oli, col lolippu muṟaiyellām āṅkilattukku uṇṭu. tamiḻukku aṉṉanilaiyillai. eḻuttoliyaik kūṭṭip paṭittāl collaic colliviṭalām. coṟkaḷaic colluvataṟ keṉṟu taṉiyolippup payiṟci vēṇṭiya tillai. itu nam moḻiyiṉ iyalpu. ayalolikaḷaiyum ayaṟcoṟkaḷaiyum tamiḻeḻuttil eḻutiviṭuvatāl, tamiḻākiviṭātu; tamiḻākkamum ākātu.
iṉṟō āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu oliyai mutalilō iṭaiyilō iṟutiyilō pōṭuvatāl tamiḻmuṟaippaṭi eḻuttuk kūṭṭip paṭikkamuṭiyātu. ippaṭi iccollaic colla vēṇṭumeṉṟu taṉiyilakkaṇam teriyavēṇṭum. āṅkilattaiccuṭṭit tamiḻc collaik kaṟpittāka vēṇṭum. paṇpaṭṭa centamiḻukku viḷaintu varum kīḻaṟai itu. eḵku, aḵṟiṇai eṉa iṭaiyil irukka vēṇṭiya aṭuppukkūṭṭu vaṭivu moḻikku mutalil vantirukkum tīyūḻkkālam itu. ilakkaṇa varampumikka cemmoḻikku aṟiviyal vaḷarum ikkālattu, orucārār iḻaittu varum aṭṭūḻiyam itu. ātaliṉ, `allatu ceytal ōmpumiṉ' eṉṟapaṭi iṉiyēṉum nām kaṭaippiṭikka vēṇṭiya moḻiyoḻukkaṅkaḷ yāvai?
1. 	āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu olikku nikarāka nam āytavoliyai evviṭattum payaṉpaṭuttakkūṭātu.
2. 	attaku āṅkilac coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku muṉṉarē takka tamiḻccoṟkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟai āḷavēṇṭum. uriya coṟkaḷ ilaveṉiṉ tamiḻākkam ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
3. 	tolkāppiyam mutal ellā ilakkaṇikaḷum āytam kuṟṟeḻuttukkup piṉṉum uyirmey valleḻuttukku muṉṉum varum eṉṟu kūṟiya varampiṉaik kaṭaippiṭikka vēṇṭum.
4. 	tamiḻil eḻuttukkūṭṭic coṟkaḷaip paṭittuviṭum iyalmuṟaiyai eṉṟum pōṟṟa vēṇṭum.
moḻikkāppu eṉ urainōkkaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟātaliṉ āytappōkkiṉaic cuṭṭi neṟippaṭuttuvatu eṉ kaṭamaiyāyiṟṟu. muṉ eḻunta uraikaḷum itu takum itu takātu eṉṟu moḻiyaṟam cuṭṭi neṟippaṭuttiyiruppatai nām aṟiyātavarkaḷallar.
āyta nilaiyai yaṟika ilakkaṇañ
cāytal tamiḻukkuc cāyvu
puṇarmoḻiyāytam
(36) 	īṟiyal maruṅkiṉum icaimai tōṉṟum.
iḵtu āytam irumoḻip puṇarcciyilum varuvatu kūṟum.
akalavurai
mēṟcolliya oru moḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum puṇarcciyilum āytaccārpoli tōṉṟum.
icaimai - olittaṉmai.
vaḻakku: 
a. ṟiṇai - aḵṟiṇai	(al + tiṇai)
pa. ṟuḷi - paḵṟuḷi	(pal + tuḷi)
pa. ṟoṭai - paḵṟoṭai	(pal + toṭai)
ka. ṟītu -kaḵṟītu	(kal + tītu)
mu. ṭītu -muḵṭītu	(muḷ +tītu)
a. kaṭiya - aḵkaṭiya	(av + kaṭiya)
tiṟaṉurai
mūṉṟu cārpeḻuttukkaḷumē oru moḻiyilum puṇarmoḻiyilum varumeṉa itukāṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. moḻimarapu orumoḻi paṟṟiyatākaliṉ immūṉṟum uriya paṟṟukkōṭukaḷai īṇṭē kūṟimuṭittār. puṇar moḻik kuriyavaṟṟai uriya puṇariyalkaḷiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟum niṉaivūṭṭiṉār. `kaṭappāṭaṟinta puṇariyalāṉa' eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukarattukkuk kūṟiyatai iṅkum ēṟṟikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
“takaram varuvaḻi āytam nilaiyalum
  pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulamai yōrē”
“āytam  nilaiyalum varainilai yiṉṟē
  takaram varūuṅ kālai yāṉa”
“vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yāyta mākum”
eṉṟu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyalil varum mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷē puṇarmoḻiyāytam paṟṟiyavai.
ikaravukaraṅkaḷ mutaleḻuttākavum iruttaliṉ kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukarameṉa ivviraṇṭum kuṟumaiyaṭai eytiṉa. āytattiṟku aṅṅaṉam oru mutaleḻuttiṉmaiyiṉ kuṟṟāytam allatu kuṟṟiyalāytam eṉṟa peyarukku iṭaṉillai, kuṟukkam eṉṟa karuttumillai. araimāttiraip pēṟu ataṉiyalpu.
puṇarmoḻikkaṇṇum āytam orumoḻiyiṟ pōlavē kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum uyirmey valleḻuttiṉ muṉṉum varum. kāṇka: aḵṟiṇai, kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu. īṇṭuvarum valleḻuttu takaram tirinta ṟakaramākavō ṭakaramākavō irukkum `īṟiyal maruṅkiṉ' eṉpataṉāl īṟu tirintu āytamākum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
paltamiḻkkaḻakam paḵṟamiḻkkaḻakam eṉavum paltoḻiṟkallūri paḵṟoḻiṟkallūri eṉavum paṇṭai ilakkaṇappaṭi varavēṇṭum. piṟakkālattu vaḻakku vīḻnta puṇarcciyilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟātaliṉ, pal tamiḻkkaḻakam, paltoḻiṟkallūri eṉa iṉṟu eḻutuvatu poruntum; iḵtu aṭippaṭaikku māṟupāṭaṉṟu.
moḻikkuṟippu
(37)	uruviṉum icaiyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum
	moḻikkuṟip pellām eḻutti ṉiyalā
	āyta maḵkāk kālai yāṉa.
itu varivaṭivillāta āytanilai kūṟum.
akalavurai
niṟam paṟṟiyum ōcai paṟṟiyum aruki varuvaṉa moḻikkuṟippu eṉappaṭum. ivai nēr poruṇmaiyuṭaiya coṟkaḷ alla. ātaliṉ eḻuttukkaḷāl eḻutikkāṭṭa muṭiyā. ivaṟṟil varum āytavōcaikaḷ naliyāmal nīṇṭolippaṉa.
āytam naliyā iṭattu eḻuttuppeṟā eṉṟa etirmaṟaiyāl āytam nalintaviṭattum poruṇmaiyuṭaiya coṟkaḷ eḵku, kaḵcu eṉa vaṭivaveḻuttup peṟum eṉpatu karuttu.
urai : āytam araimāttiraiyāka naliyātu nīṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷil niṟattilum ōcaiyilum ciṟappāka varum moḻikkuṟippellām, coṟkaḷ eḻuttil eḻutappaṭuvatu pōla, eḻutamuṭiyā.
vaḻakku:
kaḵṟu eṉṉum kallatarattam - niṟam paṟṟiyatu.
cuḵṟu eṉṉum taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇai - ōcai paṟṟiyatu.
tiṟaṉurai
kuṟṟiyalikarattukku irunūṟpā, kuṟṟiyalukarattukku irunūṟpā kūṟiya āciriyar āytattukku mūṉṟu nūṟpā ceyvar. mutaliru nūṟpākkaḷ āytam araimāttirai koṇṭa coṟkaḷil varumpōtu ilakkaṇam kūṟuvaṉa. ivai moḻikaḷēyaṉṟi moḻikkuṟippillai. nalivuṭaiyāy tam ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭai.
niṟakkuṟippum ōcaikkuṟippup pulappaṭutta āytavōcai varum iṭaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. ivai moḻikkuṟippu eṉappaṭum. ivviṭaṅkaḷil ivvōcai māttiraikku uṭpaṭātu colvāṉ aḻuttattukkēṟpa nīḷum. aḷapeṭaipōla eḻuttiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil nīḷāmal, ōcai taṉṉaḷavil nīṇṭolikkum. aḷapiṟantuyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum icaittamiḻil eḻuttup peṟāmaiyai oppiṭuka.
varukiṉṟa nūṟpāaḷapeṭai paṟṟiyatu. aḷapeṭai nīṭcikkaṇ aḵtāvatu, aḵkākkālai, moḻivayiṉ otta kuṟṟeḻuttu vaṭiviṉāl eḻutappaṭum eṉṟa ilakkaṇam kūṟuvatu. ataṉōṭu innūṟpāvai iṇaittuk kāṇa vēṇṭum. kāṇiṉ, uruviṉum icaiyiṉum varum āytavōca moḻikkuṟippātaliṉ eḻuttukkuṟikku vārā eṉpatu pōtarum. aḷapeṭai mutaleḻuttukkē yātaliṉ ivvāyta nīṭci aḷapeṭaiyeṉalākātu. “kaṇṇ ṭaṇṇaṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅkēṭṭum” eṉṟa malaipaṭukaṭām oṟṟaḷa peṭaipōla āytam iraṇṭu peṟutaṟkumillai. āytam tāṉē cārpāka irukkumpōtu tāṉ iṉṉoṉṟukkuc cārpāka utavum eṉpatu poruttamaṉṟu. irunalivu nīṭciyumākātu. ātaliṉ āytavōcaippaṭa varum niṟam oli paṟṟiya coṟkuṟippu nīṇṭōcaippaṭuṅkāl eḻuttuvaṭivukku uṭpaṭṭu vārā eṉpatum, eḻuttuvaṭivukku uṭpaṭuvaṉa mutaliru nūṟpākkaḷil cuṭṭiya araimāttirai koṇṭa poruṭ coṟkaḷē eṉpatum immūṉṟām nūṟpāviṉ teḷiporuḷām.
ottatu kūṟal eṉṟum neṟiyāl evvakaimoḻik kuṟippum nēriya eḻuttu vaṭivam peṟā eṉakkoḷka.
uṇṭuṇṭuṇ ṭeṉṉum uṇarviṉāṟ cāṟṟumē
ṭoṇṭoṇṭoṇ ṭeṉṉum paṟai
eṉpatu nālaṭiyār,
icainiṟai
(38)	kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟicai niṟaikkum
	neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
iḵtu icainiṟaippum ataṟkuk kuṟiyum kūṟum.
akalavurai
cila coṟkaḷil icai kuṟaiyumpōtu neṭṭeḻut tukkup piṉṉē ataṟkiṉamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ vantuniṉṟu icaiyai niṟaivuceyyum.
vaḻakku: vāaymai, kurīi, āṭūu, cēey, aḷaii, pōoy, kauovai, tuṉpuṟūum, eṭuppatūum, ceṟāaay.
tiṟaṉurai
eḻuttilakkaṇakkūṟukaḷuḷ innūṟpāviṉ karuttu ciṟappuṭaiyatu; naṉku ārāyntu teḷitaṟkum uriyatu.
icai kuṟaivatu ceyyuḷil eṉṟō icai niṟaippatu aḷapeṭai eṉṟō ekkuṟippum nūṟpāvil illai. eṉavē innūṟpā vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potuveṉak koḷḷa vēṇṭum. vaḻakkil āṭūu makaṭūu marūu eṉa varutalum eṉpatūum eṉa urainaṭaiyil varutalum kāṇka. cēymai viḷiyil ellām icainiṟaivu varum. ceyyuḷiluṅkūṭa iṉṉicaiyaḷapeṭai eṉpatu yāppu nōkkiyataṉṟu eṉavum vaḻakkiṟpōla iṉṉōcai niṟaivukku varuvatu eṉavum koḷḷavēṇṭum. ivvāṟu collōcai yiruttaliṉ `kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār āciriyar. kuṉṟutal eṉpatu yāppukkākavum kuṉṟalām, cevikku iṉṉōcaiyum kuṉṟalāmaṉṟō.
icai kuṉṟuvatu col. ataṉai niṟaikkuṅkāl, ac collil mutalilō iṭaiyilō iṟutiyilō irukkum neṭṭeḻuttē uriya iṭamākum. an neṭṭeḻuttukkup piṉ uriya kuṟṟeḻuttu vantu icai niṟaivu ceyyum.
vāaymai - mutalil - `ā' neṭṭeḻuttukkuppiṉ `a' 						kuṟṟeḻuttu vantatu
pulāal - iṭaiyil avvāṟu vantatu
paṭāa - iṟutiyil avvāṟu vantatu
varūum, tarūum, paṭūum eṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, varum, tarum, paṭum eṉṟu kuṟṟukaram neṭṭeḻuttāki aḷapeṭaiyatāl kāṇka.
icai niṟaikkum ikkuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ a, i, u, e, o  eṉṟa uyir vaṭivilēyē neṭṭeḻuttiṉpiṉ eḻutappaṭum. tamiḻil coṟkaḷukku iṭaiyilō iṟutiyilō uyir vaṭivu varutalillai. aḷapeṭaiyil varumpōtu aṟikuṟi eṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvataṟkē uyirvaṭivu niṟkum. kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉpatu uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttu vaṭivaṉ eṉṟu koḷka. niṉṟu eṉpataṉāl innūṟpā varivaṭivu kuṟittatām.
ituvumatu
(39)	ai oḷa veṉṟum āyīreḻuttiṟku
	ikara ukaram icainiṟai vākum.
iḵtu iraṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttukku otta kuṟṟeḻuttuk kūṟum.
akalavurai
neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu eṉṟa neṭuṅkaṇakkil ai, oḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttukkum otta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ illaiyallavā? icai niṟaikkuṅkāl, aṟikuṟiyāvaṉa yāvai? eṉpatu viṉā. ataṉait teḷivikkat tōṉṟiyatu innūṟpā.
ai eṉṟa neṭilukku ikaramum, oḷa eṉṟa neṭilukku ukaramum otta kuṟṟeḻuttākik kuṉṟicai moḻiyiṭattu icai niṟaikkum.
vaḻakku: vaḷit talaiiya tīyum; aivaroṭu ciṉaii.
tauuvai, mauuval.
tiṟaṉurai
aiyaṅkaḷaivatu innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ. ai eṉṟu olikkum pōtu ikaramum oḷa eṉṟu olikkum pōtu ukaramum iṟutiyil iḻumeṉ ōcaiyāka viḻuvataic cevi karuviyāka uṇaralām. ātaliṉ ivviru kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum iyalpāka ottaṉavē.
muṉnūṟpāvilum innūṟpāvilum kūṟiyapaṭi coṟkaḷē icainiṟaivukkuriyaṉa; neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ alla. eṉiṉum anta niṟaivai īraḷapuṭaiya neṭilkaḷ mēlum nīṇṭu niṟaikkum eṉavum anta ōcaicceytiyaik kāṭṭum aṟikuṟiyāka uyirkkuṟil vaṭivaṅkaḷ niṟkum eṉavum teḷika.
ivviru nūṟpāvilum aḷapeṭaik karuttu veḷippaṭaiyāka villai. icai niṟaivu eṉpatum ōr ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭākum. aḷapeṭai icainiṟaivukkuḷ aṭaṅkiyatu. mēlum icai niṟaivu eṉpatu oli nīṭciyiṉ payaṉaik kuṟikkum.
neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻaṉuḷ ai oḷa eṉṟa iraṇṭum cilavaṟṟil nerukkam uṭaiyavai. `āyīreḻuttiṟku' eṉṟa naṭai itaṉaik kāṭṭum.
orumoḻi
(40)	neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōreḻut torumoḻi
	kuṟṟeḻut taintum moḻiniṟai pilavē.
iḵtu uyireḻuttukaṭku moḻittaṉmai kūṟum.
akalavurai
nūṉmarapil ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa eṉpaṉa neṭṭeḻuttu eṉṟār. neṭṭeḻuttām avvoliyaṉkaḷē niṉṟāṅku poruṇmai kuṟikkum ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷāka varum.
kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ nilai eṉṉa? a i u e o eṉṟa aintu oliyaṉkaḷum niṉṟāṅku poruṇmai kuṟikkum coṟkaḷāka muḻumai peṟuvatillai.
īṇṭu neṭṭeḻuttu kuṟṟeḻuttu  eṉpaṉa uyirkaḷaiyē kuṟikkum. uyirmeykaḷaik kuṟiyā. niṟaipu - niṟaital, toḻiṟpeyar.
vaḻakku:ā	-	pacu mutaliya poruḷ
	ī		- 	koṭu, paṟakkum ī mutaliya poruḷ
	ū 		- 	tacai
	ē 		-	ampu
	ai 		- 	talaivaṉ, viyappu
	ō 		- 	matakunīrtāṅkum palakai, oḷi
	oḷa 	- 	kavvutal, kōṇal.
tiṟaṉurai
kuṟil neṭileṉa uyir iruvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ neṭilkaḷ kiṭantāṅkē collākavum varum ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa.
icciṟappuk kiṭappiṉaip pulappaṭuvatē innūṟpāviṉ nōkkam. neṭilkaḷ ivvāṟu varumeṉiṉ, uyiriṉ iṉṉoruvakaiyāṉa kuṟilkaḷiṉ nilai eṉṉa? eṉṟa viṉā eḻavē ceyyum. avviṉāviṟku viṭai colvatu pōla amaintatē `kuṟṟeḻuttaintum  moḻi niṟaipilavē' eṉṟa aṭi.
aintum eṉpatu muṟṟummai. a i u e o eṉṟa enta uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttum poruṭporuḷ  kāṭṭā. a i u eṉpaṉa cuṭṭupporuḷaiyum e tēṟṟattaiyum o ciṟappiṉaiyum kuṟikkumeṉiṉum avai taṉimoḻiyān taṉmaiyila. itaṉai niṉaintē āciriyar moḻiyākā eṉṉāmal `moḻi niṟai pila' eṉṟār. mēlai nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyapaṭi kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ icai niṟaippaṉa. moḻi niṟaippaṉavalla. moḻi muḻumaiyalla eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ teḷivu.
mūṉṟu moḻinilai
(41) ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticaikkun toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē
itu tamiḻc coṟkūṭṭattil mūṉṟu vakaiyaik kūṟum.
akalavurai
oru moḻi - oru col. toṭarmoḻi - iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭiya orucol. moḻinilai - col vakai.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ pallāyirakkaṇakkāka iruppiṉum, aṉaittaiyum mūṉṟuvakaiyāka aṭakkik koḷḷalām. ippakuppu puṇarcci nōkkiyatu. piṉvarum puṇarcci yilakkaṇaṅkaḷ inta mūṉṟiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ ataṟku muṉṉōṭiyāka accoṟ pākupāṭukaḷaiyum kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum innūṟpā potintu vaittuḷḷatu.
urai: ōreḻuttorumoḻi (ā,kā.); īreḻut torumoḻi (kaṇ, kāṭu) iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭivarum toṭar orumoḻi (tamiḻ, aṉpu, teyvam, ñāyiṟu) eṉṟu coṟkaḷ puṇarcciyiṭai niṟkum nilaivakai mūṉṟēyām.
vaḻakku: ā. mā; ī, kī, ū, pū: ē, cē; ai, kai; ō pō;
kau, veḷa, tu, no - ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷ.
pal, pāl, pāti, pala cila, kaṭu, kāṭu - īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷ.
kuṟaḷ,kūttu, vayiṟu, kuraṅku: kaṭavuḷ, kapilaṉ, avinayaṉ, - iraṇṭiṟat ticaikkum toṭarmoḻi, aḵtāvatu nīṇṭa oru col.
tiṟaṉurai
immuccoṟ pākupāṭṭil tamiḻppuṇarccikaḷ vēṟupāṭuṟuvataik kaṇṭaṟintavar paṇṭai ilakkaṇa nūlōr eṉpatu `mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē' eṉṟa naṭaippiṭiyāl aṟiyalām. inneṟiyappaṭṭavarē tolkāppiyar.
kā, mā, yā, pū, cē, nī eṉṟāṅku pala ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷum; āl, vēl, pīr, tāy, mīṉ, vicai, ñemai, irā, nilā, paṉi, puḷi, eṭu, ceru eṉṟāṅku pala īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷum; ellām, āvirai, ātaṉ, pūtaṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, patakku, veyil, makkaḷ, vēṭkai, illam eṉṟāṅku pala toṭar orumoḻikaḷum tolkāppiyattil pākupaṭap puṇarkkappaṭuvataik kaṟkiṉṟōm.
kuṟṟiyalukaram aṟuvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟaik kuṟikkum pōtu,
“īreḻuttorumoḻi uyirttoṭar iṭaittoṭar
  āytam toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar
  āyiru mūṉṟē ukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ”
eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil ippākupāṭṭiṉai niṉaivūṭṭuvar tolkāppiyar. īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉpatu kācu, kūṭu, kūṟu eṉṟu neṭiṟkuṟṟiyalukaramām. neṭiṟṟoṭar eṉṟu itaṉaip peyarppaṭuttavillai. ēṉaiya aintiṉaiyumē toṭar eṉṟa kuṟiyāṟ cuṭṭuvar. neṭiṟkuṟṟiyalukarattaip palaviṭaṅkaḷil neṭṭeḻuttimpar eṉavum kuṟippiṭuvarēyaṉṟit toṭar eṉṟu maṟantum kūṟiyatillai. piṟkāla ilakkaṇikaḷ neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟilukaram eṉṟu  ēṉai aintiṉaiyoppa itaṉaiyum kuṟikkalāyiṉar. tolkāppiyattu āḷappaṭum ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ palappala. avai mayakkamaṟṟavai. nallurai ceyvataṟku ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ paṇṭaip poruḷaik kūrntu aṟiyavēṇṭum.
innūṟpā ciṟappu mikkatātaliṉ iṉṉoru muṟaiyum eḻutic cintippōm.
ōreḻut torumoḻi īreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticaikkum toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
ōreḻuttuccol, īreḻuttuccol, iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa col eṉa eḻutteṇṇikkaiyil col mūṉṟu vakaippaṭum eṉpatu karuttu. ātaliṉ iraṇṭiṟantu icaikkum toṭarmoḻi eṉṟiruntālum toṭar orumoḻi aḵtāvatu palaveḻuttut toṭarum orucol eṉṟutāṉ poruḷpaṭum.
orumai paṉmai allatu oṉṟu pala eṉṟa tamiḻeṇ vaḻakku payaṉilaiyāka varum viṉaimuṟṟukku uriyatu. peyarccoṟkaṭku ellām oruvar iruvar mūvar nālvar eḻuvar paṉṉiruvar aṟupattumūvar eṉa ellā eṇkaḷum; orumoḻi, irukaṇ, aintamiḻ, eḻupiṟappu, eṇkuṇam, patiṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟitaḻ, āyiravūḻi eṉa ellā eṇṇaṭaikaḷum varum.
`irupeyar palpeyar aḷaviṉ peyar' eṉa eccaviyalil tolkāppiyarum, `orunāṭ cellalam irunāṭcellalām, palanāṭ payiṉṟu palaroṭu celliṉum' eṉpa (101) puṟaþnāṉūṟum `oṉṟeṉa iraṇṭeṉa mūṉṟeṉa nāṉkeṉa ainteṉa āṟaṉe eḻeṉa eṭṭeṉat toṇṭeṉa' eṉa (3)p paripāṭalum kūṟupa. ātaliṉ `iraṇṭiṟanticaikkum toṭarmoḻi' eṉṟa innūṟpā naṭai tamiḻvaḻakkēyākum.
īṇṭu eḻuttu eṉpatu oliyaik kuṟikkumā? varivaṭivaik kuṟikkumā? nalla viṉā.
 ā	-	ōreḻuttorumoḻi	varivaṭivu : 1	oliyaṉ	ā =1
kā	-	mēṟpaṭi	mēṟpaṭi 		mēṟpaṭi 	k + ā =2
āṇ	 -	 īreḻuttorumoḻi 	varivaṭivu: 2 	mēṟpaṭi	ā + ṇ = 2
kāṇ - mēṟpaṭi	mēṟpaṭi 		mēṟpaṭi       ka+ā+ṇ=3
kāṭu - mēṟpaṭi	mēṟpaṭi		mēṟpaṭi	k + ā + ṭ
						+ u = 4
kāppu - toṭarmoḻi 	varivaṭivu: 3	mēṟpaṭi	k + ā + 						                 	 p + p + u =5
kapilaṉ -mēṟpaṭi	varivaṭivu:	4	mēṟpaṭi     k+a+p+ i 						                    +l+a+ṉ=7
eḻuttunilaiyil uyirum meyyum taṉiyāka iruntālum taṉi vaṭivukaḷ peṟṟiruntālum coṉṉilaiyil uyirmeyyākavē oṟṟumainayampaṭak kalantuviṭutaliṉ, uyirmeyyai ōreḻuttākak koḷvatē peruvaḻakku. pala, cila, nil, kal eṉpavaṟṟai iraṇṭeḻuttākavum tamiḻ, aṉpu, kuṟaḷ eṉpavaṟṟai mūṉṟeḻuttukkākavum kallātārum eṇṇuvar. `arumaṟai maruṅkiṉ aintiṉum eṭṭiṉum' eṉṟa cilappatikārattil im mantiraṅkaḷ uyirmeykaḷai ōreḻuttākavē koḷkiṉṟaṉa. eḻutteṇṇum kaṭṭaḷai yaṭiyil uyirmey ōreḻuttākavē kaṇippup peṟum. āṇ, kāṇ eṉpaṉa nēracaiyākavum avai, cuvai eṉpaṉa niraiyācaiyum ceyyuṭkaṇ alakupeṟum.
tamiḻmoḻikkaṇ olikkum varivaṭivukkum tolpaḻaṅ kālattē iṇainta cemmaiyaiyum eḻuttuvaḻakkum nalliṭaṅkoṇṭa āṭciyaiyum, ataṉāl muppatu oliyum muppatu eḻuttu eṉṟē peyarpeṟum oṟṟumaik kāṭciyaiyum ivvurai paṉmāṇum varalāṟṟu muṟaiyiṟ cāṟṟi varukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ ōreḻuttuc col īreḻuttuccol iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa col eṉṟu eḻuttunilaiyil mūṉṟuvakai ceytatu varaṉmuṟaip poruttamākum.
iṉṉoru muṟaiyākavum teḷiyalām. kā, ke, kē, ko, kō, kau eṉpaṉa ōruyir meyyāyiṉum vaṭivil iraṇṭu mūṉṟākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum īreḻuttu mūveḻuttu eṉṟu yārum; colvatillai. :ōruyirmey eṉṟa olinilaiyai nōkki ōreḻuttu eṉṟē colvōm. kākkai nāleḻutteṉṟō veḷavāl āṟeḻutteṉṟō collōm. eṉavē ōreḻuttoru moḻi mutalāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ olittaṉmaiyiṉaiyum uṭkoṇṭaṉavām.
ippākupāṭu iruvakai vaḻakkum eḷimaiyum teḷivum paṟṟi koḷḷappaṭṭatu. `ōreḻuttiyal patam' eṉavum, `pakāppatam ēḻum pakupatam oṉpatum eḻuttīṟākat toṭarum' eṉavum pavaṇantiyār muṉṉōr muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi yiruttal pōṟṟattakum.
taṉimeyyiyakkam
(42) 	meyyi ṉiyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum.
itu taṉimeyyaic collu muṟaiyum eḻutumuṟaiyum kāṭṭum.
akalavurai
nūṉmarapil “meyyiṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal” eṉṟa puḷḷiyoṭuniṟkum iyaṟkai meykaḷaip paṭṭāṅku eḷitāka olikka muṭiyātallavā? ataṉāl ammeyyoli yaṉkaḷ akavoli cērttu olikkappaṭum aṅṅaṉam oliyoṭu iyaṅkiṉum puḷḷiyuṭaiya meyyākavē karutap paṭum. akara uyirmeyyākā. itu col vaḻakkiṟkum eḻuttu vaḻakkiṟkum ēṟṟa naṭaimuṟai.
vaḻakku: 
valleḻut teṉpa	ka caṭa ta pa ṟa	tol 17
melleḻut teṉpa	ṅa' ña ṇa na ma ṉa	mēṟpaṭi 18
iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa	ya ra la va ḻa ḷa	mēṟpaṭi 19
immuṟaiyai nūl muḻutuṅ kāṇalām.
ikku, iccu, ittu, ippu, iṇṇu eṉṟavāṟum kkaṉṉā, ccaṉṉā, ṭṭaṉṉā, eṉṟavāṟum. kaṉa, ṉaṅa, caṉa, ñaṉa eṉṟa vāṟum pēccuvaḻakkil meykaḷ iyakkam peṟutalum uṇṭu eṉṟālum ivai taṉimey eṉa makkaḷ purintu koḷvar.
tiṟaṉurai
piṟappil ovvoru meyyum taṉakkeṉṟa poruṇmai yuṭaiya mutaleḻuttu; uyirukku nikarāṉa mutaleḻuttu; eṉiṉum eḷimaiyākac colvataṟkum kēṭpataṟkum aṟivataṟkum akaram tuṇaiyoliyākum : avvaḷavē 
aṉā āvaṉṉā iṉa īyaṉṉā eṉa uyirum kāvaṉṉā kīyaṉṉā kōvaṉṉā kauvaṉṉā eṉa uyirmeyyuṅkūṭat tuṇaiyoli peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
muṟkūṟiyāṅku ovvoru meykkum urivaṭivum ataṉmēl oṟṟu eṉpataṟkuriya puḷḷivaṭivum uṇṭu. urivaṭivukaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum ēṟi uyirmeyyākum pōtum ka kā ki kī ku kū ke kē kai ko kō kau eṉṟu naṭuvuruvāka niṟkum. oṟṟu eṉpataṟkuriya patiṉeṭṭu meykkum potuvāṉa puḷḷivaṭivu maṭṭum nīṅki uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭukkum. ammuṟaippaṭi, meyyiṉ iyakkattukaku akaram varumpōtu mēṟpuḷḷi nīṅkum eṉṟu koḷka. `puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum uruvuru vāki akaramō ṭuyirttalum' eṉṟu nūṉmarapiṟ coṉṉa vitiyai niṉaika.
eḻuttu meyyākuka. uyirākuka, naṭaimuṟai yiyakkattiṟkuc cila cāriyaikaḷ peṟum.  innūṟpāvil `akaramoṭu civaṇum' eṉac karaccāriyai vantiruttal kāṇalām. ṉaḵkāṉ, ṟaḵkāṉ aikāram, oḷakāram, takaram, vakaram, ḷakāram, lakāram, rakāram eṉavum varum.
akaraccāriyaiyuṭaiya kakarattiṟkum akara vuyirmeyyāṉa kakarattiṟkum vēṟupāṭu naṭaimuṟaiyil uṇarappaṭum.
`itaṉ naṭaimuṟaiyitu’ eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatu innūṟpāviṉ payaṉ.
meymmayakkam
(43)	 tammiyal kiḷappiṉ ellā veḻuttum
	meynnilai mayakka māṉa millai.
itu meymmayakkap potunilai kūṟum.
akalavurai
nūṉmarapil iṉṉa meykkuppiṉ iṉṉamey varum eṉṟu virivākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. atu coṉṉilaiyil amainta eḻuttut toṭarcciyākum. ataṟku mēl naṭaimuṟai viḷakkamāka eḻuntatu innūṟpā.
ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum meyyeḻuttu eṉṟa peyar nilaipeṟumpōtu, etaṟkuppiṉ etu vantālum kuṟṟamillai. taṉṉaiccuṭṭi varumiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya varaiyaṟaiyillai.
ellāveḻuttum - ellā meyyeḻuttukkaḷum,
māṉam - kuṟṟam, varampu.
vaḻakku: 	avaṉ makaram eḻutiṉāṉ
	avaḷ lukaram eḻutiṉāṉ	     pēccuvaḻakku
	avar nikaram eḻutiṉār	
“ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum”
“oṟṟavum varuṭavum lakāra ḷakāram”
“varuṭa rakāram ḻakāram”
“tām varūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē”
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷ.
tiṟaṉurai
vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷil kāraṇam collic ciṟappu vitikūṟuvatu tolkāppiya neṟi. `tammiyal kiḷappiṉ' eṉpatu kāraṇa naṭai. mey eṉpatu eḻuttāyiṉum taṉṉaik kuṟikkum nilaiyil peyarākic collākum. mayaṅkutaṟku uriyavākā eṉṟu muṉvarampiṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷum intak karuttaḷavil poruntiya mayakkamākak koḷḷattakum. ivvarampil varampu ilakkaṇanūl kaḷiṟṟāṉ varum muḻutum naṭaimuṟaippāṭu nōkkic collappaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ ituvoṉṟu. “meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum” eṉṟa mēlainūṟpāvum itaṉaic cārntatu itu naṭaimuṟaippāṭu eṉṟa neṟippeyar peṟum.
oru coṟpoḻivuk kūṭṭattiṟku maṉaivi makkaḷ mutalāṉa uṟaviṉarkaḷum vantirukkalām. appōtum coṟpoḻiñaṉ eṉ aṉpāṉa uṭaṉpiṟappukkaḷē eṉṟu taṉpoḻivait toṭaṅkukiṉṟāṉ ituvē naṭaimuṟaip pāṭu eṉappaṭum.
meymmayakkam paṟṟi mīṇṭum oru teḷivai niṉaittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum. laṇṭaṉ, leṉiṉ, lūtar, ṭarpaṉ, ṭākkā, ṭelli, raṅkūṉ, raciyā eṉpaṉavellām poruḷ kuṟikkum coṟkaḷātaliṉ, tammiyal kiḷappaṉavākā. ivaṟṟait tamiḻ marapuppaṭi uyir cērttō vaṭivu tirittō tamiḻmaiyākkik koḷḷavēṇṭum.
īroṟṟuṭaṉilai
(44)	ya ra ḻa veṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ oṟṟak
	ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟ ṟākum.
iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉilai kūṟum; ituvum meymmayakkattaic cārntatu.
akalavurai
nūṉmarapil meymmayakkam virivākak kūṟappaṭṭatu. aṅkuk kūṟiyavai ōroṟṟuṭaṉilai meymmayakkamākum. appōtu mutalmey puḷḷiyaṭaiyatākum. aṭuttuvarumey uyirmeyyāka irukkum (aṉpu) eṉa viḷakkiṉēṉ. innūṟpā kūṟuvatu eṉṉa? iraṇṭupuḷḷi allatu iraṇṭoṟṟu irukkum.
aṭuttuvarumey muṉpupōl uyirmeyyāka irukkum (vāḻttu)
urai: y r ḻ eṉṉum mūṉṟum muṉ oṟṟāka irukka k c tp ṅa ña n m eṉpavai mīṇṭum oṟṟāka vara īroṟṟup peṟum.
yk, yc, yt, yp, yṅ, yñ, yn, ym:
rk, rc, rt, rp, rṅ, rñ, rn, rm:	      eṉa iraṇṭiraṇṭu
ḻk, ḻc, ḻt, ḻp, ḻṅ, ḻñ, ḻn, ḻm;      oṉṟāka varum.
oṟṟu - meyppuḷḷi, puḷḷi eṉpatu meyppuḷḷi. cārpuppuḷḷi, ekara okarappuḷḷi, makarak kuṟukkappuḷḷi eṉap palavaṟṟaiyum kuṟikkum. oṟṟu eṉpatu meyppuḷḷi oṉṟaṉaiyē cuṭṭum.
vaḻakku	:	k    c 	t   p	 ṅ    ña 	n    m 
y	:	vāykkāl	pāyccu	cēytatu	vāyppu
		vēyṅkuḻal	tēyñcu	māyntu	moympu
r	:	īrkku	cārcci	cārttu	vārppu
	ārṅkōṭu	kūrñcilai	kūrntu	kūrmpaṭai
ḻ	:	vāḻkkai	vīḻcci	vāḻttu	kāḻppu
	pāḻṅkiṇaṟu	pāḻñcuṉai	vāḻntu	vīḻmpaṭai
tiṟaṉurai
“yaraḻa veṉṉum puṉṉi muṉṉar
  mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum”
eṉa nūṉṉmarapiṟ kūṟiyatu (āyka, ārka, āḻka) ōroṟṟu meym mayakkamākum. innūṟpā vēykkuṟai, vērkkuṟai, vīḻkkuṟai, eṉa īroṟṟup paṟṟiyatu eṉṟu teḷivupaṭuttu kiṉṟatu. `īroṟṟākum' (irupuḷḷipeṟum) eṉpataṉāl vaṭivu nōkkiyum innūṟpā amaintatu eṉakkoḷḷalām.
y r ḻ eṉṟa mutaloṟṟukkup piṉ varuvaṉa k c t p ṅ ñ n m. iraṇṭāvatu oṟṟukkuppiṉ varuvaṉa evai eṉpataṉai nūṉmarapiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.
meymmayakkam eṉpatu iṉṉa meyyoliyaṉukkup piṉ iṉṉa meyyoliyaṉ varum eṉa eḻuttaḷavil kiṭakkai nilai terippatu eṉavum itu oru collukkum, puṇar moḻikkum potuveṉavum oṟṟukku aṭuttuvaruvatu uyirmeyyāka irukkum eṉavum muṟkūṟiya karuttukkaḷai īṇṭum niṉaivukoḷka.
`mūṉṟēmoḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē' eṉṟa coṉṉilaiyamaippuk kūṟukiṉṟārātalāṉum, īroṟṟu toṭarmoḻiyuḷ varuvatātalāṉum innūṟpā moḻi marapāyiṟṟu. “īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi iṭaittoṭarākā” eṉṉum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal nūṟpā itaṟkuc cāṉṟu. iṉṉoru kōṭpāṭuṅkoḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. nūṉmarapu iyalpāṉa aṭippaṭaiyilakkaṇaṅkūṟum; iyalpukku kūṭutalāṉavaṟṟaiyum iyalpukku vēṇṭiyamēl viḷakkaṅ kaḷaiyum moḻimarapu kūṟum. ivvāṟu koḷḷiṉ iyalvaippum nūṟpāvaippum iṉṉum teḷivutarum.
r ḻ amaippu
(45)	avaṟṟuḷ
	rakāra ḻakāram kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.
itu rakaravoṟṟum ḻakaravoṟṟum mutaṟkaṇ varumnilai kāṭṭum.
akalavurai
meymmayakkam eḻuttukkiṭakkai nilai paṟṟiyatēṉum coṉṉilaiyil vaittuttāṉē viḷakkañ¨ ceyya vēṇṭum.
ya ra ḻa eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ rakaramum ḻakaramum mutaṟkaṇ taṉikkuṟilukkup piṉ oṟṟāka vantunillā. aḵtāvatu ar par eṉavōaḻ, paḻ eṉavō vārā. eppaṭi varum? ār, pār,  āḻ, pāḻ eṉa neṭilukkup piṉṉum pakar, tamiḻ eṉak kuṟiliṇaikkup piṉṉum oṟṟāka varum. ivvāṟē pukār, paiṅkūḻ eṉap palaveḻuttut toṭarmoḻiyil ellām varum. taṉikkuṟiṟkīḻ varumpōtu uri tiru karai eṉavum aḻi viḻu maḻai eṉavum uyirmeyyākavē varum. taṉikkuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟē vārā eṉa itaṉāl vilakkappaṭṭatu avvāṟu vilakkuvatu eḷitākaliṉ, yakaravoṟṟē mey, poy, cey, koy eṉa kuṟṟoṟṟākavum varum eṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
kuṟṟoṟṟu - mutaṟkaṇ taṉi uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttu allatu taṉi uyirmeykkuṟṟeḻuttu.
vaḻakku: nīr, nakar; pāḻ; pukaḻ eṉavarum.
aruccaṉai, aruccuṉaṉ, narumatai, taruppam, curuṇaṉ eṉṟa ayaṟcoṟkaḷai āḷa vēṇṭiṉ ivvamaippuppaṭi eḻutal tamiḻām. karuppiṇi, varuṇaṉai  varukkam, karuppūram, marumam, karumam, karuttaṉ eṉṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷai ivvāṟē piḻaiyiṉṟi eḻutuka.
tiṟaṉurai
aikāra oḷakāram pōl iṭaiyiṉaṅkaḷuḷ rakaramum ḻakaramum oru taṉmaiyaṉa.
“meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
  tammuṟṟām varūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē”
eṉṟa nūṉmarapil rakara ḻakaram taṉmuṉ tāṉ vārā eṉṟu vilakkiyatai niṉaika.
itukāṟum iṉṉameykkuppiṉ iṉṉameyvarum eṉṟu meymmayakkam kūṟivanta āciriyar iṉṉa vuyirukkup piṉ iṉṉa meyvārā eṉa innūṟpāvil uyiroṭu meymmayaṅkāmai kūṟum oru moḻiyamaippaik kaṟkiṉṟōm.
īṇṭu oru viḷakkam
`ār ār pa veṉa varūu mūṉṟum'
`ir īr miṉ eṉa varūu mūṉṟum
eṉa viṉaiyiyaṟkaṇ rakaram kuṟṟoṟṟāka vantuḷatē eṉṟu viṉavalām. ām; ar ir eṉa vantuḷa. āṉāl ivai collamaippil coṟkiṭaiyē varum uṟuppukkaḷ teḷiyiṟkākat taṉittukkāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. poṟimutalāṉa poruḷkaḷiṉ pākaṅkaḷaik kaṟpippataṟkāka avvamayam vēṇṭumeṉṟē taṉiyuṟuppukkaḷaip pirittuk koḷkiṉṟō mallavā? ceṭiyiṉ narampukaḷai, malariṉ itaḻkaḷai, āṭaiyiṉ iḻaikaḷai, paṭaiyiṉ tirukukaḷai. ūrtikaḷiṉ cērkkaikaḷai utirikaḷākap pākuppaṭuttik kāṇpatai oppiṭuka.
iṅṅaṉam vārā, varakkūṭātu eṉṟu collumiṭaṅ kaḷilum tavaṟāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷait tantutāṉ viḷakkuvatum vilakkuvatum uṇṭu. kalvineṟi eṉṟu itaṉaik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. cevvaṉ vaṭivu eṉṟa ikkāla viṉāmuṟaiyum inneṟittu.
toṭarmoḻi viḷakkam
(46)	kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
	toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻut tiyala.
itu toṭarmoḻikku oru viḷakkam ceyyum.
akalavurai
ōreḻuttorumoḻi, īreḻuttorumoḻi, iraṇṭiṟat ticaikkum toṭarmoḻi eṉṟu tamiḻc colliṉai muvvakaiyuḷ aṭakkik koṇṭār tolkāppiyar. avaṟṟuḷ toṭar moḻikku oru putuviḷakkam taruvatu innūṟpā.
kuṟumai eṉpatu neṭumai eṉpatum eḻutteṇṇikkai aḷaviṟ koḷḷutalāl ellāt toṭar oru moḻikaḷum neṭilōṭiya iyaṉṟaṉavākak karutappaṭum.
neṭṭeḻuttu - iraṇṭiṟkumēl nīṭiya eḻuttukkaḷ, neṭṭōṭṭam kaḻineṭil pōl.
vaḻakku: 	pukaḻ, aḻakaṉ, aṇṇāmalai, paṉampāraṉ
tiṟaṉurai
ilakkaṇa nūlil ellāviyalkaḷum vaṉkaraiyuṭaiyaṉa valla; oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ceytikaḷ tantuppiṉṉiyalkaḷukku varampu kāṭṭupavai. itu paṭikkaṭṭuneṟi eṉappaṭum. mīṇṭum niṉaivu kūrvōm.
ōreḻuttorumoḻi	-	ā, mā
īreḻuttorumoḻi	-	āṉ, māṉ, cila, palā, āṭu, ūtā
toṭarmoḻi		-	nalam, nāṉku, aṇaṅku, maturai, 								iḷaṅkō, tiruvaḷḷuvar
muvvakai moḻikaḷilum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟālum avaṟṟai innūṟpā poruḷākak koḷḷa villai. varivaṭivāṉa eḻutteṇṇikkaiyai aḷavāka vaittup pārkkumpōtu toṭarmoḻitāṉ iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa palaveḻuttuṭaiyavai. oru vaṭivu koṇṭatu ōreḻut torumoḻi: iru vaṭivu koṇṭatu īreḻut torumoḻi: kuṟiyīṭukaḷē taṟcāṉṟu. īṇṭu eḻuttu eṉpatu varivaṭivaik kuṟikkum eṉa uriya nūṟpāvil muṉteḷivu ceyyappaṭṭatu.
innūṟpā māttirai paṟṟiyataṉṟu eṉpataṟku aḷaviṟ kōṭal eṉṟa col amaintiruppatu oru cāṉṟu. īṇṭu aḷavu eṉpatu eḻutteṇṇikkaiyaik kuṟikkum. `eḻuttaḷaveñciṉum cīrnilaitāṉē' eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal ivvuraikku araṇceyvataiyuṇarka.
piṉvarum iyalkaḷ puṇarmoḻicceykai kūṟuvaṉa. puṇarmoḻi eṉpatu moḻikaḷiṉ iyaipātaliṉ, moḻi marapil puṇarmoḻikkut takka pāṅkukaḷai aṭikōluvar āciriyar. mūvvakaic coṟpākupāṭu innōkkiṉatu.
“kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
  aṟiyat tōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi”

“kuṟṟeḻut timparum ōreḻuttu moḻikkum
  niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukarak kiḷavi”
“īreḻut torumoḻi uyirttoṭar iṭaittoṭar”
“makarattoṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
  ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa”
“palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
  ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa”
“toṭaral liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
  lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē”
eṉap parantu varum iṭaṅkaḷil immūvakaik kuṟikaḷ āḷappaṭuvatait teḷiyalām. `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' eṉpatu īreḻuttorumoḻiyaik kuṟikkum.
pōṉm
(47)	ceyyu ḷiṟutip pōli moḻivayiṉ
	ṉakāra makāram īroṟ ṟākum
	ṉakārai muṉṉar makāraṅ kuṟukum.
iḵtu īroṟṟum kuṟukkamum peṟum oru collaik kāṭṭum; ituvum meymmayakkamākum.
akalavurai
“ya ra ḻa veṉṉum mūṉṟumuṉ oṟṟak
  ka ca ta pa ṅa ña na ma īroṟ ṟākum”
eṉṟu parantupaṭṭa nilaiyil vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum potuvāka īṟoṟṟumayakkam kūṟiṉār. innūṟpāvil pōlum eṉṟa oru collaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi, atu pōṉm eṉa īṟoṟṟāka varum. ceyyuṭkaṇṇē varum. varuṅkāl makaram kuṟukum eṉṟu mūṉṟu ilakkaṇam kūṟukiṉṟār.
urai: ceyyuṭkaṇ iṟutiyaṭiyil iṟuticcīril `pōlum' eṉṟa viṉaimuṟṟuc col pōṉm eṉakkuṟuki niṟkumpōtu `ṉm' eṉa īroṟṟākum. avviṭattu ṉakarattiṉ piṉvarum iṟuti makaram kālmāttiraiyākak kuṟukkam peṟum.
vaḻakku:
“citaiyuṅ kalattaip payiṉāṟ ṟiruttum
  ticaiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm”	 (paripā.10)
“neñcam piḷantiṭṭu nērār naṭuvaṭṭaṉ
   vañciṉam vāyttāṉum pōṉm”	 (kali.101.)
itu pōṉma eṉṟa vaṭivupeṟum. makarattiṟku uḷḷē (ma) puḷḷiyirukkum.
tiṟaṉurai
innūṟpāviti cevvarampuṭaiyatu, iṭanteḷivāṉatu. ivvīroṟṟu vaḻakkil varātu, vēṟucollil vārātu, ceyyuṭkaṇṇum vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil vārātu eṉṟa etirnilai peṟappaṭum.
ceyyuḷiṟuti eṉa iṭaṅkūṟuvatāl pōlum eṉpatu ceyyum eṉṟa muṟṟārum; peyareccam iṟutiyil nillātaṉṟē.
itukāṟum kaṟṟa meymmayakkaṅkaḷil ōroṟṟukkup piṉ (vāḻka) uyir meyyum, īroṟṟukkuppiṉ (vāḻttu) uyirmeyyum irukkum. pōṉm eṉṟa inta īroṟṟil piṉ etuvumillai; īroṟṟaḷavākavē niṉṟu makaram nalikiṉṟatu. itaṉal makarattiṟkuk kāl māttiraik kuṟukkam tōṉṟum.
“araiyaḷavu kuṟukal makara muṭaittē
  icaiyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kālai
  uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē”
eṉṟa nūṉmarapu nūṟpāvai niṉaivu koḷka.
oru viḷakkam: pōṉm eṉṟa muṟṟu ceyyuḷiṭaiyil vantāl īroṟṟum ākātu; kuṟukkamum peṟātu.
“peruṅkal laṭārum pōṉmeṉa virumpi”	(puṟam. 19)
maḻaiyuṇ māmati pōṉmeṉat tōṉṟumē 	(cīvakacin.127)
iṅṅaṉam iṭaiyil varum `pōṉm' eṉṟa muṟṟuc coṟkaḷ.
ṇa ṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉark
kacañapa mayavav vēḻum uriya
eṉṟa ilakkaṇappaṭi ōroṟṟu mayakkamākum.
arantiṉvāy pōṉmpōṉm pōṉm
