meyyppuḷḷi 	-	11
						170 īṟukaḷ
170 īṟukaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇikkai peritākat tōṉṟiṉālum puṇarccikku nilaimoḻiyil niṟkum uṇmaiyāṉa īṟukaḷ uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉoṉṟum āka irupattumūṉṟēyām. kuṟṟiyalukaramum cērumpōtu irupattu nāṉkām. “āvi ñaṇa namaṉa yaralava ḻaḷamey cāyu mukaram nālāṟu mīṟē” eṉṟu naṉṉūl eḷiya iṉiya varampukāṭṭum.
eḻuttu allatu oliyaṉ eṉpatu poruṇmai yuṭaiyatum poruḷ vēṟṟumai ceyvatum ām. ituvē itaṉ talaiyilakkaṇam; moḻi mutalilum varalām. iṟutiyilum varalām. vārāmalirukkalām; aṅṅaṉam vārātavai iṭaiyilāvatu varum; eṅkō ōriṭattu vantu poruṇmai tōṟṟum. ṅakaram mutalilum iṟutiyilum vārā toḻiyiṉum iṭaiyil vantu paṅkam ō pakkam kaṅku ō cukku; pāṅku ō pākku eṉap poruḷ vēṟṟumait tiṟaṉ ceykiṉṟatu. moḻip parappil aritāka ōriṭattut tōṉṟit taṉakkeṉap poruṇmai kāṭṭiviṭiṉ, atupōtum ataṉai oliyaṉ eṉṟu matippataṟku; ataṉāṟṟāṉ ṅakaram muppatu eḻuttiṉuḷ oṉṟāyiṟṟu.
nakarameyyiṟuti
(74)	ucca kāramoṭu nakārañ civaṇum.
itu nakarameyyiṟutikku varaiyaṟai kūṟum.
akalavurai
cu eṉṟa iṟuti irucollaḷavil vantatupōla nakaramey yiṟutiyum iru collaḷavil varum. aḵtāvatu nakaraviṟuti cukarattaiyokkum.

vaḻakku
porun - ottal. otta vēṭaṅkoḷḷal; viṉaiccol
verin - mutuku; peyarccol.
tiṟaṉurai
porunaṉ eṉṟa col ottavaṉ, oppuṭaiyavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuṅkāl, porun eṉpatu pakutiyākum; vīraṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuṅkāl poru eṉpatu pakutiyākum eṉṟa vēṟṟumaiyuṇarka. māṇṭāṉ eṉpatu māṇpuṭaiyāṉ eṉṟakkāl māṇpakuti eṉṉavum iṟantāṉ eṉṟakkāl māḷ pakuti eṉavum varutalai oppunōkkuka.
verin eṉṟa col paṇṭu mikka payiṟciyuṭaiyatu eṉpataṉaip puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal kāṭṭum. cilappatikāram varai iccolvaravaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. itu uyartiṇai mutukaik kuṟiyātu. ceṭikoṭi vilaṅkukaḷiṉ piṉpuṟattai allatu mēṟpuṟattaik kuṟikkum eṉpataṉai avaiyanūl āṭcikaḷāl aṟiyalām. vaḻakkiṟkuk koṇṭu varattakum aṟiviyaṟcol itu.
ñakara meyyiṟuti
(75)	uppa kāramoṭu ñakaraiyum aṟṟē
	apporu ḷiraṭṭā tivaṇai yāṉa.
itu ñakarameyyiṟutikku varaiyaṟai kūṟum.
akalavurai
pu eṉṟa iṟuti oru collaḷavil vantatu pōla ñakarameyyīṟum oru collil varum. intavaḷavil ñakaram pukarattai yokkum. pukaram pōlat taṉpāl piṟaṉpāl eṉap poruḷiraṭṭutal ñakarattiṟku illai.
vaḻakku:	uriñ - uriñcutal, viṉaiccol
tiṟaṉurai
porun poruntu eṉavum uriya uriñcu eṉavum uriya valliṉam peṟṟup piṟkālattu nīṇṭaṉa. puṟam puṟampu eṉa varutalai nōkkuka.
māṇākkarkku muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉaivūṭṭi oppumai kāṭṭi nūl muḻutum karuttōṭac ceytal kalvi muṟai yātaliṉ, tolkāppiyam māṭṭēṟṟiṉai nirampap pōṟṟiyatu. ottataṟital, ovvāmaiyaṟital, āyvukāṇal ivai māṭṭēṟṟiṉ payaṉkaḷ.
vakara meyyīṟu
(76) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu.
itu vakarameyyīṟṟukkuc colvaraiyaṟai kūṟum.
akalavurai
vakarameyyeḻuttu nāṉku collil īṟāka varum.
vaḻakku: 	av iv uv tev
mutal mūṉṟum aḵṟiṇaip paṉmaic cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ; avai, ivai, uvai eṉpatu poruḷ. tev - pakai; peyarccol.
tiṟaṉurai
malaiyāḷam tōṉṟiya kālattu uv eṉpatu vaḻakkiṉmaiyiṉ ilīlā tilakam av, iv, tev eṉṟa mūṉṟiṉaiyē uṭaṉpaṭum. av, iv eṉpaṉa uṭampaṭu meyyalla eṉpatum innūl teḷivikkum karutturai.
tolkāppiyattiṉaip piṉpaṟṟi naṉþṉūl nāṉmoḻiyīṟu collum. kav, vav eṉṟa pōli vakaravīṟukaḷai naṉṉūl kuṟittālum, uṇmaiyākak koṇṭu meyyīṟṟuppuṇariyalil puṇarccikku mēṟkoḷḷavillai.
kavvaiyāṟ kavvitu kāmam atuviṉṟēl
tavveṉṉum taṉmai yiḻantu
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷil, kav, tav eṉa vakaravīṟukaḷ vantiruntum piṟkāla ilakkaṇattār ivaṟṟaic cērkkavivlai. pōli eṉṟu kavvaiyum olikkuṟippeṉat tavvaiyum karutiviṭṭaṉar pōlum; allatu kavvu, tavvu eṉa ukaravīṟākak koṇṭu mirukkalām.

ṉakara makara mayakkam
(77)	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa.
itu ṉakaravīṟṟu meykku mayakkamum varaiyaṟaiyum kūṟum.
akalavurai
tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ uyiriṟutikaḷaiyum meyyiṟuti kaḷaiyum varaṉmuṟai ceytuvanta āciriyar meyyiṟutikkaṇ ōr olippōliyai innūṟpāvil iyampuvar.
aḵṟiṇaip peyarc collākat toṭarmoḻiyil varum ṉakarameyyīṟu makara vīṟāka mayaṅkutaluṇṭu. itu potuviti eṉiṉum oṉpatu ṉakarap peyarkaḷ maṭṭum avvāṟu mayakkam peṟuvatillai eṉṟu pulappaṭuppar pulavar.
pukar aṟakkiḷanta - kuṟṟamiṉṟit teḷivākac collappaṭṭa
mēṉa - mēlē, iṭattu.
vaḻakku:	potuviti: nilaṉ -nilam; aṟaṉ - aṟam; kalaṉ -kalam; valaṉ -valam; akaṉ - akam; puṟaṉ- puṟam; tiṟaṉ - tiṟam. ivvāṟu pala.
mayaṅkātaṉa oṉpatu
	1. ekiṉ 	-	aṉṉam, puḷi
	2. payiṉ 	-	piciṉ
	3. aḻaṉ 	-	piṇam
	4. kuyiṉ 	-	mēkam
	5. vayāṉ 	- 	kārippuḷa
	6. cekiṉ 	- 
	7. viḻaṉ		poruḷ teriyavillai
	8. puḻaṉ 
	9. vayāṉ 	-	.

aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, ekiṉ, kuyiṉ eṉpavai tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil āḷappeṟṟuḷa.
tiṟaṉurai
immayakkam aḵṟiṇaip peyaril toṭarmoḻiyil varum eṉpatu varampu.
toṭarmoḻi iraṇṭiṟanticaikkum collātalitaṉ, valiyaṉ, kaluḻaṉ, alavaṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟaiyum kuṟikkumāyiṉum iruvakai vaḻakku nōkki, nilaṉ, akaṉ, puṟaṉ, uraṉ eṉa vāṅku mūveḻuttuc coṟkaḷē ēṟpuḻik koḷḷappaṭum. muṉpē viḷakkiyapaṭi poruḷ vēṟṟumai ceyyāmai olippōliyiṉ ilakkaṇamaṉṟō.
makarattōṭu mayaṅkiṉālum ṉakaravīrākavē vaittup puṇarkkappaṭum eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ aṟivippu.
mayaṅkāta coṟkaḷ oṉpatu: mayaṅkum coṟkaḷ tolkāppiyakkālattē mikappala vaḻakkiliruntaṉa. ātaliṉ makara vīṟākap puṇarkkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷum tolkāppiyattu uḷa. maṟaṅkaṭai, curameṉamoḻipa, nilampeyarntu, maṭampaṭa eṉpaṉa cāṉṟukaḷ.
iṭaikkālattu makaravīṟṟu vaḻakku marapāyiṉamaiyiṉ piṉṉilakkaṇikaḷ makaram ṉakaramāka mayaṅkum eṉac cuṭṭiṉar. ituvum keṭutalillai, poruḷait tākkāmaiyiṉ.
oru karuttiṉaip pulappaṭutta moḻikkaṇ col vaṭivaṅkaḷum ilakkaṇavaṭivaṅkaḷum kūṭutalāka iruppatu makkaṭku eḷimaiyākum; moḻikku vaḷamākum; naṭaikku āṟṟalākum.
moḻiyiṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷaik kūṟum tolkāppiyam ōreḻuttorumoḻi, īreḻuttorumoḻi, toṭar moḻi eṉṟa mūṉṟu moḻi nilaikaḷil vaittuc cila varaiyaṟai ceytiruppataik kaṟkiṉṟōm. innūṉmuṟai piṉṉūlkaḷil illai.


moḻi marapu - iyal muṭivurai
nūṟpā : 34
kuṟṟiyalukaravilakkaṇam paṟṟip palvēṟu koḷkai kaḷum ataṉāl cila kuḻappaṅkaḷum vaḻivaḻi vantuḷḷaṉa. itaṉait taṉipporuḷāka iṅku muḻutuṟa āytal iṭaṉaṉṟu. ātaliṉ cintaṉaikku uramākum cila kuṟippukkaḷait taruvēṉ mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaḷ nam viḷakkattukku uriyavai.
“iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa”
itu moḻimarapu nūṟpā.
“allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟuimaik kaṇṇum;
   ellā viṟutiyum ukaram niṟaiyum”
“valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
   tollai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu muṟittē”
ivviraṇṭum kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal nūṟpā.
`ukaram niṟaiyum' eṉa iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum, `ukaram nilaiyum' eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pāṭaṅ koḷvar.
piṟarkūṟum karutturaikaḷ
1. `iṭaṉumāruṇṭu' eṉpataṉāl kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyil ciṟupāṉmaiyāka aruki varum.
2.	 vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉē varumāyiṉ araimāttirai yākum. kālmāttiraiyākum. oru māttirai yākum eṉṟa mūṉṟu karuttukkaḷ uḷa. cekkukkaṇai cukkukkōṭu eṉpaṉavē mūvarum kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ.
3. 	kuṟṟiyalukaram irumoḻippuṇarccikkaṇ alvaḻiyilum vēṟṟumaiyilum orumāttiraiyāka niṟaiyum eṉṟa karuttum araimāttiraiyākavē nilaiyum eṉṟa karuttum uḷa.
ivvēṟupāṭukaḷuḷ nām mikavum karutattakkatu puṇarmoḻikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram oru māttiraiyāki muṟṟukaram ām eṉpatu. urakam niṟaiyum eṉṟu pāṭaṅ koḷḷum iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum avvaḻiyiṉarum ikkaruttuṭaiyar. itu poruntumā?
coṟkaḷ ellām puṇarmoḻippaṭupavai. puṇar moḻikku vārāta coṟkaḷ illai. kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumaip puṇarmoḻiyākuṅkāl oru māttirai yāka niṟaintuviṭum eṉiṉ, taṉimoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaṭkup payaṉillai. uyirīṟṟup puṇariyalil ukara vīṟṟilē aṭakki viṭalāmē. kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal eṉat taṉip pēriyal vēṇṭumā? nilaimoḻiyiṉ iṟuti kuṟṟiyalukaramāka niṉṟāṟṟāṉē, taṉip puṇariyalukkup poruḷuṇṭu. kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkut taṉippuṇarcci vitikaḷum vēṟṟumaikaḷum uṇṭu eṉpavai ellārum uṭaṉpaṭa vēṇṭiya kōṭpāṭukaḷām.
“iruvakai yukaramō ṭiyaintavai variṉē
  nērpum niraipum ākum eṉpa”
eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyalil iruvakaiyum eṉṟa vēṟupāṭu paṭumikaiyākiviṭumaṉṟō? ceyyuḷiṟ coṟkaḷ acaiyākic cīrākiyaṭiyākit toṭarcci peṟṟākumpōtu, nērpu niraipu eṉṟa kuṟṟilukara vāypāṭukaṭku iṭam yāṇṭuṇṭu? muṟṟukaramākumeṉiṉ, `pōntu pōntucārntu cārntu' eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷaiyaṭiyil eṭṭeḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭa vēṇṭumaṉṟē? pērāciriyar itaṟkuk kūṟum amaiti nam uḷam toṭavillai. cārpeḻuttu puṇarcciyil mutaleḻuttākiviṭum eṉṟa kōṭpāṭu aṭimuraṇ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
puṇarmoḻikkaṇ kuṟṟiyalukaram kāl māttirai yākumeṉṟāl oru māttirai yākumeṉṟālum nūṉmarapil `avviyal nilaiyum ēṉai mūṉṟē'eṉṟataṉaiyaṭuttu ivai collappaṭṭirukkum; vaṭiva nilai paṟṟiyum colliyirukka vēṇṭum. makarattukkuc colliyiruppatai niṉaivukoḷka. `iṭaippaṭiṟkuṟukum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē' eṉṟumoḻi marapiṟ colliyatupōla, `iṭaippaṭiṉ mikum iṭaṉumuṇṭu' eṉṟoru nūṟpā yāttirukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō? `ukaram niṟaiyum' eṉṟa oru collai vaittup pala ilakkaṇak kūṟum paḻutākumpaṭi karutturaippatu cevvitaṉṟu. `nilaiyum' eṉṟa oru pāṭam uṇṭu. `tollaiyiyaṟkai nilaiyalum urittē' eṉṟu aṭuttuvaruvatai nōkkiṉ, ippāṭamum poruntum pōlum. eṉiṉum nacciṉākkiṉiyarukku muntiya uraiyāciriyar iruvarum `niṟaiyum' eṉṟē oru talaiyākap pāṭaṅkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ātaliṉ atuvē valiyuṭaittu.
`ukaram niṟaiyum' eṉṟa pāṭattiṉaiyē koḷvōm. ataṟkāka muṟṟukaramāka, oru māttirai niṟaiyum eṉṟa poruḷ koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉpatillai. pērāciriyar ipporuḷiṉar eṉpatu teḷivu.
ukaram niṟaiyum eṉpataṟku māttirai niṟaiyum eṉṟa poruḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭā. `kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟicai niṟaikkum' eṉpatu pōla icainiṟaivākak karutalām. atāvatu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻiyilum taṉakkuriya araimāttiraiyōcai niṟaiyākavē peṟum eṉpatu karuttu. īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉṟu toṭaṅkum muntiya nūṟpāvil `āyiru mūṉṟē ukaram kuṟukiṭaṉ' eṉa varuvatālum ikkaruttu valiyuṟum. `āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟaintē niṟkum' eṉṟa iḷampūraṇar uraiyum ikkuṟippē koṇṭatu. ivvuraiyil māttiraik kuṟippillai.
mēlē mutaṟkaṇ kāṭṭiya mūṉṟu nūṟpākkaṭkum nāṉ koḷḷum poruḷ varumāṟu:
1. 	orumoḻiyilēyaṉṟik kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇar moḻiyilum kuṟukutal uṇṭu. kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil itaṉ viḷakkattaik kāṇalām.
2. 	aṟuvakaik kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟukaḷum puṇarmoḻiyil alvaḻiyilum vēṟṟumaiyilum araimāttirai yōcai  niṟaintu niṟkum. araimāttiraiyiṉiṉṟu kuṟaitalō, araimāttiraiyiṉum mikutalō irā.
3. 	cekkukaṇai, cukkukkōṭu pōṉṟa valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikku muṉ valleḻuttu varumpōtu, kuṟṟiyalukaram mutaleḻuttu ukarampōla oru māttirai peṟutalum uṇṭu.
ivai maṉa niṟaivu tarum uraiyalla eṉiṉum uḷḷavaṟṟuḷ ottatu kāṇal neṟiyākum.
ilakkaṇap perumpulavar vēṅkaṭarāculu eḻutiya `kuṟṟiya lukaram' eṉṟa āyvupperuṅ kaṭṭurai māṇavar pārvaikkuriyatu.
nūṟpā 38
icainiṟai kūṟum innūṟpāviṟkum,
“nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ avvaḷa puṭaiya
  kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṟa aḷapeṭai nūṟpāviṟkum iyaipuṇṭu. vēṇṭu meṉṟāl vēṇṭum aḷapuṭaiya olikaḷaik kūṭṭi olittuk koḷḷalām eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. īṅkum ceyyuḷukkē eṉṟa kuṟippillai eṉṟālum ceyyuṭkē peruvaravākum eṉpatil aiyamillai.
icainiṟai allatu aḷapeṭai eppaṭi uṇṭākiṉṟatu. eppaṭip payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu? mūvaḷapu icaittaltāṉ ēṟakkuṟaiyap peruviḻukkāṭu viḷinilaikaḷil kūṭutal varum; `ceṟāaay vāḻiyeṉ neñcu' eṉṟaviṭattu nāṉku māttirai eṉpataṟkāka `ceṟāāy' eṉa mēlum oru neṭṭeḻuttu varuvatillai. `neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta kuṉṟeḻuttu’ eṉṟa innūṟpāviṉ teḷivāl, evvaḷavu aḷapeḻuntālum kuṟṟeḻuttuttāṉ piṉvarum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aḷapeṭai kuṟittu iru koḷkaikaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟai īṇṭut teḷivatu poruntum:
1. neṭṭeḻuttē mūṉṟu nāṉku eṉa aḷapeṭukkum. aṟikuṟikaḷākavē otta kuṟṟeḻuttu varum. `nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ kūṭṭi eḻūutal' eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. nīṭṭam eṉpataṉālēyē neṭṭeḻuttiṉ nīṭṭam eṉpatu teḷivām; naṉṉūl `neṭil aḷapeḻum avaṟṟavaṟṟiṉak kuṟil kuṟiyē' eṉṟu veḷippaṭai ceyyum. aḷapeṭai vanta coṟkaḷaic collumpōtu kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉṟapaṭi neṭilai nīṭciyākavē mūṉṟu māttiraiyaḷavu olikkiṉṟōm. iraṇṭum oṉṟumāka viṭṭolippatillai. āṉāl oru cikkal ceyyuṭkaṇ alakiṭumpōtu karāam varūum eṉpataṉai nirai nērākap pirittatu vāypāṭu kūṟuvatē yāppilakkaṇac cemmaiyākum. mūṉṟu māttiraiyai ōr eḻuttāka vaikkum nilai yāṇṭum illai.
2. iṉṉoru koḷkai: neṭṭeḻuttu aḷapu niṟaikka villai. otta kuṟṟeḻuttuttāṉ niṟaikkiṉṟatu eṉpatu iṉṉoru koḷkai. `kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ' eṉṟa inta nūṟpāvil otta kuṟṟeḻuttu niṉṟu icai niṟaikkum eṉṟu eḻuvāy payaṉilai amaintuḷḷaṉa. icai niṟaippatu kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ ceyalākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. `neṭṭeḻut timpar' eṉpataṉālum itu peṟappaṭum. icainiṟaivil kuṟṟeḻuttukkut taṉimatippaḷittāl `karāam' eṉpataṉai nirainēr, puḷimā eṉa alakiṭum yāppilakkaṇattukkum ottuvarukiṉṟatu eṉpatu ikkoḷkaiyar viḷakkam.
ivviru koḷkaikaḷum tammuḷ muraṇāṉavai; oṉṟaiyē nām uṟutippaṭutta vēṇṭum. eḻuttu, kuṟil, neṭil, mey, valmelliṭai, cārpu ellāmē oli nilaiyil, kēḷvinilaiyil viḷakkappaṭuvaṉa. varivaṭivai vaittu oliyilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭuvatillai eṉṟa pēruṇmaiyaip pōṟṟavēṇṭum. nākarika moḻivaḷarcciyil varivaṭivat tiṟkup pērātikkam uṇṭeṉiṉum, oli nilaikku nikarāṉa matippu varinilai peṟuvatillai.
ōraḷapicaikkum kuṟil, īraḷapicaikkum neṭil eṉṟu aḷapu kūṟivanta tolkāppiyar mūvaḷapu paṟṟiyum avvaḷapuṭaiyaṉa kūṭṭi olittal paṟṟiyum toṭarntu kūṟuvar. itaṉāl varivaṭivu kuṟitta eṇṇattukku iṭaṉillai eṉpatu teḷivākum eḻuttu, kuṟṟeḻuttu, neṭṭeḻuttu eṉpaṉavellām oliyaṉ, kuṟṟoliyaṉ, neṭṭoliyaṉ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum. eṉavē toḻāar, paṭāa, varūum eṉpaṉavaṟṟai mūṉṟoliyaḷavākavē nīṇṭolikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivātalōṭu ivvāṟē iṉṟum olikkumuṟai poruntum eṉpatum pōtarum. mēlum mūvoliyākumpōtu poruṇmai yuṭaiya oliyaṉillai eṉpatum muṉviḷakkiyapaṭi uṇarappaṭum. `kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ' eṉpataṉāl icai kuṉṟiṟṟēyaṉṟip poruṇmai kuṉṟavillai eṉpatum icai niṟaikkum eṉpataṉāl poruṇmai niṟaivillai eṉpatum viḷaṅkum.
“kuṉṟicai moḻivayiṉ niṉṟicai niṟaikkum
   neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē”
eṉṟa nūṟpāvai maṟukālum cintippōm. tolkāppiyar muṉpē tamiḻukku varivaṭivum cemmaiyāyiṟṟu eṉavum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkum oru nikarāka moḻiyappaṭutaliṉ aṉṟē eḻuttuvaḻakkum mutaṉmai peṟṟatu eṉavum ataṉāl tolkāppiyar vēṟṟumai teḷikkumuṟaiyil vaṭivaṉ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷaiyum iṭaimaṭuttuḷḷār eṉavum nām aṟivōm. varivaṭivai aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittu oliyilakkaṇam moḻiyappaṭutal illai eṉiṉum varivaṭivac ceyti varuvatu muraṇākātaṉṟē. “meyyiṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal” eṉṟāl atu meyyoliyaṉ ilakkaṇam ākātu. puḷḷivaṭivam oliyaṉilakkaṇattukku evvāṟṟāṉum toṭarpillai. ōr oliyaṉukku eḻuttulakil vanta varikkuṟiyaik kāṭṭum aḷavē ataṉ payaṉ atu pōṉṟu varivaṭivam paṟṟiyatē innūṟpā eṉa aṟika.
otta kuṟṟeḻuttu niṉṟu icai ciṟaikkum eṉṟa aṭiyil `niṉṟu' eṉṟa viṉaiyeccam kāṭṭuvatu eṉṉa? aṟikuṟiyāṉa varivaṭivaik kuṟippatākum. icai kuṉṟiya collil icainiṟaivukku aṭaiyāḷamāka neṭṭeḻuttu vaṭivukkup piṉ ottakuṟṟeḻuttu vaṭivam niṟkum. ataṉāl olinīṭci peṟappaṭum. iṉṉoru kuṟippum ivviṭattu uṇṭu. collukku iṭaiyilum iṟutiyilum taṉiyuyir vaṭivaṅkaḷ iruppatillai. itaṉaip `puḷḷiyillā ellāmeyyum' eṉṟa nūṉmarapu nūṟpāvil kaṟṟōm. `pōoy, toḻāar, keṭuppatūum, paṭāa eṉa icainiṟaikkum coṟkaḷil taṉikkuṟṟuyir vaṭivu niṟpatāl ivai niṟaikka vanta aṟikuṟikaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākum. itaṉāl `otta kuṟṟeḻuttu' eṉpataṟku otta taṉikkuṟilvaṭivu eṉapporuḷ koḷḷavēṇṭum. `ceṟāaay' eṉṟa nālaḷapu icai niṟaippiṉum anta aḷapukku camamāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē aḵtāvatu iraṇṭu kuṟil vaṭivaṅkaḷē varum eṉṟa kuṟippup peṟappaṭum. ātaliṉ kuṟṟeḻuttu niṟpataṉāl icai niṟaiyavillai eṉavum, icainiṟaiviṉaikkāṭṭum eḻuttuk kuṟiyē kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉavum koḷḷum kōṭpāṭē poruntum. mēṟkūṟiya kuṟikkōṭpāṭu okkum eṉiṉum ceyyuṭkaṇ alakiṭuvatupaṟṟi oru cikkal uṇṭu. ēṉaik kōṭpāṭṭiṉar viṉavum oru karuttukkut teḷivāṉa viḷakkam ituvarai kiṭaikkavillai eṉpataṉai uṭaṉpaṭuttu kiṉṟēṉ.
aḷapeṭaiyil oliyilakkaṇamē yaṭippaṭai eṉiṉ, kuṟṟeḻuttu aṟikuṟiyeṉiṉ - pōōy - nērnēr, tēmā; tōḻāar - nirainēr, puḷimā; ceṟāaay - nirai nērnēr, puḷimāṅkāy eṉa alakiṭṭu vāypāṭu colluvatu poruntumā? mūṉṟu nāṉku māttirai nīṭṭal aḷavukku oru vāyppāṭukaḷ vēṇṭumaṉṟō? mūṉṟiṉai iraṇṭum oṉṟumākavum nāṉkiṉai iraṇṭum oṉṟum oṉṟumākavum olinīṭciyai pirittu vāypāṭu kūṟuvataṟku ilakkaṇam ceyyuḷiyalilum illaiyaṉṟē. ivai karutavēṇṭiya viṉākkaḷē.
iccikkalāṉa viṉākkaṭkup piṉvarum viḷakkam oruvāṟu poruntiṉālum poruntum.
iraṇṭu māttirai neṭṭeḻuttē poruṇmai yuṭaiya oliyaṉ. ataṟku mēlē varum olinīṭṭam oliyaṉaṉṟu. neṭṭeḻuttup pōla aḷapeṭaiyeḻutteṉa oṉṟillai. ataṉāl oliyaṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa aḷapiṉait taṉiyāka alakuk kaṇippu ceytirukka vēṇṭum. pattukku mēṟpaṭa eṇṇum pōtu oru collāṟ collāmal patiṉoṉṟu paṉṉiraṇṭeṉac collum muṟaiyai oppu nōkkuka.
tamiḻukku eḻuttu vaṭivu tolpaḻaṅkālattē amaivupeṟṟu nalvaḻakkum eytiṟṟu eṉa ivvurai paṉmuṟai valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyar kālattum ceyyuḷ vaḻakku ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. vāyvaḻakkiṉaik kāṭṭilum ceyyuḷ vaḻakku eḻuttu vaṭiviṉaip perumpālum taḻuvi niṉṟatātaliṉ, acai cīr mutaliyaṉa kāṇappaṭum eḻutturuvai vaittum eḷimaiyākak kaṟpikka muṭintatu eṉak koḷḷavēṇṭum. ittaku kalvip payiṟci aḷapeṭaikkaṇ aṟikuṟiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa uyirvaṭivāṉa kuṟṟeḻuttuk kaḷait taṉiyacaiyāka aṟutticaikkum marapiṉai uṇṭākkiṟṟu. pērāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraikaḷ “aḷapeṭai” yacainilai yākalum urittē”eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyal nūṟpāviṉ kaṇ ikkaruttukku ūṉṟākiṉṟaṉa.
eḻuttuvaḻakkiṟ cila coṟkaḷ taṉivaṭivu peṟuvatai `nāṉ' eṉṟa āṅkilac collilum kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikkumpōtu avaṉ eṉṟa āṅkilac collum kāṇka. itukāṟum nām ārāytavaiyellām uyiraḷapeṭai kuṟittavai. ivvicai niṟaivu vaḻakkiṟkum perumpāṅku varumātaliṉ eḻuttatikārattiṟ kūṟiṉār. oṟṟaḷapeṭai ceyyuṭkē yurimaiyiṉ, ataṉ nilai ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum. cārpeḻuttukkaḷukku aḷapeṭai yākum taṉmaiyillai.
aḷapeṭai vaḻakkiṟkum ceyyuṭkum iṉiyatōr moḻikkaruvi. iṉṟuvaḻakkil iṉṉōcaipaṭak kūṟiṉālum urainaṭaikkaṇ kuṟikaḷ eḻutappaṭuvatillai. innūṟṟāṇṭuc ceyyuṭkaḷil aḷapeṭaiyāṭci illaiyeṉṟē collalām. icai kuṉṟumpōtu ataṉai nirapputaṟkeṉṟu paṭaikkappaṭṭa aḷapeṭai marapiṉaic piṉpaṟṟāmal, ikkālak kaviñarkaḷ coṟkaḷaic cērttu nirampukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl poruḷ niṟaikuṟaikiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ ōraḷavēṉum aḷapeṭai vaḻakkiṉaip pāṭṭukkaḷil uyirppittal pulamaiyākum.
nūṟpā 40
innūṟpā uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu eṉpatu uraiyāciriyarkaḷ karuttu. ataṉāl kā tī pū kau eṉa uyirmey neṭilākavum eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ eḻutiṉar. uyirmeykkum uriyatu eṉṟa karuttiṉāl `kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum' eṉpataṉai eccavummaiyākak koṇṭu cila kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikaḷām eṉṟu poruḷeḻuti, tu, no eṉak kāṭṭum tantaṉar.
uyirmey eṉpatu tolkāppiya nōkkamāyiṉ `ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻi” eṉṟa nūṟpā viṟkuppiṉ innūṟpā kiṭakka vēṇṭum. avvāṟiruppiṉ, mūṉṟumoḻi nilaikaḷuḷ ōreḻuttoru moḻikku maṭṭum eṭuttukkāṭṭuc colvāṉēṉ eṉṟa viṉāt tōṉṟum. uyir meyyāyiṉ neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻ eṇṇikkaiyil nillātu. naṉþṉūlār kūṟiyatupōla nāṟpatu neṭilkaḷ eṉṟu muḻutuṟac colliyirukka vēṇṭum.
uyirmey eṉpatu tolkāppiyar nōkkamāyiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē eṉṟu varuvatu kūṟiyatupōl, kuṟṟeḻuttil nakaravokaram oṉṟē ōreḻuttorumoḻi eṉap paṭṭāṅkuc colliyiruppar.
ivvāṟu uyirmey paṟṟi maṟuppuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.
ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉṟa nūṟpāvukku muṉpē kūṟiyiruttalālum `neṭṭeḻuttēḻē' “kuṟṟeḻuttaintum” eṉa vāḷā moḻintiruttalālum taṉiyuyirkaḷē innūṟpāviṉ poruḷ eṉakkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
avvāṟu koḷḷuṅkāl `kuṟṟeḻuttaintum' eṉpatu muṟṟummaiyāki enta uyirkkuṟilum muḻumoḻiyākā eṉpatu ēṟkappaṭum. neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē eṉpatil `oḷa' oṉṟumaṭṭum uyirmeyyākavē varutal kāṇkiṉṟōm. ēṉaiyāṟum uyiraḷavilē muḻupporuṇmaiyuṭaiyaṉa. oḷa eṉpatum oru muḻuccollāka aritākat tolkāppiyak kālattu vaḻaṅkiyirukkakkūṭum. `avvittu' eṉpataṉaip pōliyākak koṇṭāl `oḷa' viṉaic collākum. itu oru kuṟippē. eṉiṉum nūṟpāc curukkam karuti `neṭṭeḻut tēḻē' eṉṟu kūṟiṉārāka vaittuk koḷḷalām. iṅṅaṉam mutaṟkaṇ potunaṭaiyiṟ kūṟiviṭum pōkkut tol kāppiyattup palliṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭu.
cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai oḷa veṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
eṉṟa nūṟpā naṭai ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntuvarum eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppiṉum cila vīṟukaḷ vārāmai veḷippaṭai.
ituvarai ceyta toṭaiviṭaiyāl nām aṟiya muṭivatu eṉṉa? uyirneṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ, atāvatu uyir neṭiloliyaṉkaḷ niṉṟāṅkē collaṉkaḷākavum viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam poruṇmai cuṭṭumpōtu oliyil etuvum māṟṟamillai. ivvirupālamaippu uyirkaḷuḷ neṭilukku uṇṭu. kuṟilukkum uṇṭō eṉiṉ illai.
nūṟpā 46
piṟarurai: rakara ḻakaram kuṟṟoṟṟāka varuvatillai eṉpatu muṉ nūṟpāviṉ karuttu. akar, akaḻ, eṉṟāṅku kuṟṟeḻuttukku aṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉavē eṉṟu viṉavuvārkku. iṅṅaṉam vantaṉa valla; aka eṉṟa kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ vantaṉavākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpar. ēṉeṉṟāl kuṟiliṇaiyākumpōtu iraṇṭu māttiraiyaḷavu peṟṟu neṭilāki viṭukiṉṟatallavā? neṭiṟ kīḻ vantaṉavākak koḷḷalām eṉṟu innūṟpāvukkup poruḷ ceytuvarupa.
rakara ḻakaram ivvāṟu varum eṉṟu amaitikāṇa ippaṭi oru nūṟpā vēṇṭumā? kuṟiṟkīḻ vārā eṉa ōriṭam vilakkappaṭṭatu. neṭiṟkīḻākavō kuṟiliṇaikkīḻākavō eḻuttut toṭarntu piṟavāṟu varumeṉpatu tāṉē karuttu. aṅṅaṉam kūṟal vēṇṭiṉ neṭiṟ kīḻum toṭarmoḻiyilum varumeṉac cuṭṭiṉāṟ pōtumē.  attoṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻuttām eṉṟu camaṉkūṟum nōkkeṉṉō? rakara ḻakaram neṭiṟ kīḻallatu vārā eṉavum  ellāvaṟṟaiyum neṭiṟ kīḻākavē aṭakkik koḷka eṉavucam  viti vaḻakku uṇṭā? akal āl eṉṟum akaḻ āḻ eṉṟum marup paṭṭāl toṭarmoḻi īreḻuttoru moḻiyāki viṭumaṉṟō? aṅṅaṉam toṭarmoḻi yellām ākumā? ākumāyiṉ, toṭarmoḻi eṉṟa oru pākupāṭu eṟṟukku!
“meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ ellā veḻuttum
  tammuṟ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalaṅ kaṭaiyē” 	(tol.27)
eṉṟa meymmayakka nūṟpāvil raḻanīṅkalāka ēṉaiyavai tamakku muṉṉē tām varum eṉṟār. ataṉāṟ peṟappaṭuvatu eṉṉa? vilakkappaṭṭa rakara ḻakaram piṟavē peṟum eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. atupōlavē rakara ḻakaram kuṟṟoṟṟākā eṉṟāl vēṟuvakaiyil oṟṟāki varum eṉpatu urai. “avai neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām eṉṟa iḷampūraṇariṉ karutturai poruntum.
kuṟil neṭil eṉpaṉa muḻutum māttirai kāraṇamāka vantavaiyalla. iru kuṟil oru neṭilukkuc camam eṉavō oru neṭil irukuṟiliṉ iṇaippu eṉavō koḷvatu tavaṟu. muppatu eḻuttukkaḷum poruṇmaiyuṭaiya taṉiyoli yaṉkaḷ eṉṟu muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. ātaliṉ “kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ” eṉṟa aṭikaṭku muṉṉai yuraikaḷai ēṟpiṉ' oliyaṉaṭippaṭai kulaintuviṭum. aḷavu eṉṟa coṟku māttirai yaḷapu eṉṟu muḻutum karutik koṇṭamaiyāl ivvuraippiḻai nērntatu.
eṉṉurai
muvvakaic coṟpākupāṭu vakuttuk koṇṭatu puṇariyal vitikaḷai vakai tokai ceytaṟkē. puṇarneṟi paṟṟiya oru kōṭpāṭṭiṉai nām teḷintu koḷvōmāka. īṟu paṟṟip puṇarviti kūṟiṉālum atē īṟu ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākavō īreḻuttorumoḻiyākavō toṭar moḻiyākavō irukkum pōtu palaviṭaṅkaḷil tiripukaḷ vēṟupaṭum. aḵtāvatu īṟuṭaiya colvakaikkum puṇar vitikkum toṭarpum vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu. īkārayiṟuti, ukaraviṟuti, ṇakāraviṟuti, yakaraviṟuti eṉṟellām iṟutipaṭa naṭaiyiruntālum avviṟutikaḷ ōreḻuttoru moḻiyiṉ, īreḻuttorumoḻiyiṉ, toṭarmoḻiyiṉ uṟuppu eṉpataṉaiyum  eṇṇik koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam niṉaiya vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē ā mā kā pū ū nī mī eṉṟāṅku ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷaiyum; pala cila irā nilā paṉi paḷi eṉṟāṅku īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷaiyum: aḻaṉ puḻaṉ veyil iruḷ ātaṉ pūtaṉ patakku makkaḷ eṉṟāṅku toṭar moḻikaḷaiyum vitantu puṇarviti kāṭṭuvar tolkāppiyar.
“kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum ēreḻuttu moḻikkum
 aṟiyaṟtōṉṟum akarak kiḷavi”
“palavaṟ ṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
  ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa”
eṉṟavāṟu muvvakaik kuṟikaḷaiyum āṅkāṅku payaṉpaṭuttuvar. eṉavē puṇariyal kaṟkumpōtu moḻi marapiṟ colliya muvvakaic coṉṉilaiyiṉ iṉṟiyamai yāmai peṟappaṭum.
“kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ
  toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻut tiyala”
eṉṟa nūṟpāvai maṟupaṭiyum cintippōm. aḷavu eṉpataṟku eṇṇikkai eṉpatu poruḷ, ōreḻuttu, īreḻuttu toṭarmoḻi eṉṟa vakai eḻuttuvaṭiviṉāl amaintatu eṉṟu kaṇṭōm. “eḻuttaḷaveñciṉum” eṉṟa ceyyuḷiyalil varum kaṭṭaḷaic cīr ilakkaṇam itaṉait teḷivākkum. itteḷivupōtātu, iṉṉum teḷivu vēṇṭum.
“toṭarmoḻi yellām neṭṭeḻuttiyala”
eṉṟu colliyataṉ karuttum payaṉum eṉṉa? kuṟaḷaṭiyai nōkkac cintaṭiyum nēraṭiyum neṭiyavāyiṉum avaṟṟiṉ nīṇṭavaṟṟaiyē neṭilaṭi, kaḻineṭilaṭu eṉap peyariṭu kiṉṟōm. ivai eḻutteṇṇikkaiyaip poṟuttaṉa. atupōl toṭarmoḻi iraṇṭukaṭanta pala eḻuttukkaḷai yuṭaiyatātaliṉ, nīṇṭa vari eṉpatu pōla neṭṭeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu. tolkāppiyaṉ eṉpatu palaveḻuttuṭaiya neṭiya oru col eṉpatu karuttu. neṭṭeḻuttu - nīṇṭa col. “palavaṟṟiṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē” eṉṟa uyir mayaṅkiyal nūṟpāvil `nīṭumoḻi' eṉa āṭci peṟutalaiyum kāṇka.
ellāt toṭarmoḻiyum neṭṭeḻuttāka iyalum eṉpataṉāl puṇariyalil īṟu paṟṟi viti kūṟiṉālum toṭarmoḻi muḻutiṉaiyum orumaiyākak karutip pārkka vēṇṭum eṉpatu itaṉ payaṉ. itaṉai “īraḷapicaikkum neṭṭeḻuttu” eṉṟa kuṟiccolloṭu mayaṅkak kūṭātu.
iḷampūraṇar urai kuṟṟamillai; nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urai poruttamillai; eṉṉurai iṉṉum cintaṉaikkuriyatu. `neṭṭeḻuttiyal' eṉpataṟku maṉaniṟaiyum poruḷ kāṇavēṇṭum.
nūṟpā - 49 - 53 varai
ivvaintu nūṟpākkaḷum ai oḷa eṉṟa oliyaṉkaḷukkut taṉ vaṭivukaḷēyaṉṟik kūṭṭuvarivaṭiṅkaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟa oru poruḷnutaliyaṉa. mēlum `moḻippaṭutticaippiṉum' eṉṟu varum muṉnūṟpāvukku viḷakkamāka amaintaṉa ivai. iṅṅaṉam atikārap pōkkaik kaṇṭu poruḷ ceyvatu nallatu.
aintiṉuḷ nāṉku aikāram paṟṟiyaṉa eṉpatu eṉka ruttu. ātaliṉ, `ikara yakaram aikāram iṟuti viravum’ eṉpataṟku aikāram cārntu urai ceyya vēṇṭum ikkaruttu civañāṉa muṉivarkku uṭaṉpāṭākum. `akara ikaram aikāram ākum' eṉavum `akarattimpar yakarap puḷḷiyum aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉai' eṉavum colliyapiṉ, innūṟpā kiṭattalāṉum, nūṟpāvil vēṟoru cūḻnilai collamaiyāṉum, aikāravaṭivac cārpāṉatu eṉpatu teḷivu. vayiram tayilam eṉavum ayntu mayyam eṉavum koḷḷum uraiyē atikāra vōṭṭattukkup poruntum. nāy, nāyi, nāi, eṉṟāṅku karuttuk koḷvataṟku nūṟpāvilum iṭamillai, atikāra varaṉmuṟaiyumillai.
olippōli, eḻuttup pōli eṉa iruvakaiyuṇṭu. maṟaṉ - maṟam; aṟaṉ- aṟam; pētaimai - pētamai; uṭaimai; uṭamai; maiyal - mayal eṉṟa coṟkaḷil ōr oliyaṉukkup piṟitōr oliyaṉ vantu niṟkiṉṟatu. iḵtu oliyaṉmayakkam. poruḷ vēṟṟumai ceyyāmaiyiṉ pōliyāyiṟṟu.
vairam - vayiram; maintaṉ - mayintaṉ; aintu - ayntu; aivaṉam - ayvaṉam eṉṟa coṟkaḷil ai eṉṟa ōr oliyaṉukku uriya oru varivaṭivum irukkavum vēṟu iru vaṭivaṅkaḷ vantuḷa. iṅku oliyaṉ varivaṭivattāl vēṟupaṭavillai. uriya vari vaṭivattukku māṟāka atuvum iruvaṭivukaḷ varakkāṇkiṉṟōm. eṉavē iṅku `ai' eṉṟa vaṭivukkum `ay' eṉṟa vaṭivukkum viraval ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. ai eṉṟa oliyaṉaip paṟṟiya nōkkillai. ivvāṟē oḷakārattukkum koḷka.
“ammuṉ irakam yakara meṉṟivai
  eytiṉ aiyot ticaikkum avvō
  ṭuvvum vavvum oḷavō raṉṉa”
eṉṟa naṉṉūṟpā eḻuttuppōli kūṟuvateṉa mayilainātarum caṅkara namaccivāyarum uraippatu poruntum. `aiyot ticaikkum' `oḷavōraṉṉa' eṉpataṉāl mutal oliyaṉākiya aikāramum oḷakāramum tūyaṉa kalappaṟṟaṉa eṉpatu teḷivām. ammuṉ ikaram aiyotticaikkum; ammuṉ yakaram aiyotticaikkum eṉa niralē urai ceyyavēṇṭum. icai vaṭivu paṟṟiya eḻuttup pōlikaḷ. pōli eṉpatu iṉattuḷ varuvatu. varivaṭivukku iṉṉoru varivaṭivu pōliyāka muṭiyumēyaṉṟi, olivaṭivukku varivaṭivu vara muṭiyātaṉṟō. ātaliṉ eḻuttukku eḻuttu māṟṟāka varuvatu eḻuttuppōli eṉatteḷika.
cila oliyaṉkaḷiṉ cērkkaiyāl cila voliyaṉkaḷ piṟakkum eṉavum uyiroliyaṉkaḷil meyyoliyaṉkaḷ kalakkum eṉavum viritturaikkum civañāṉamuṉivariṉ karuttu mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatu eṉṟa moḻiyiṉ aṭittaḷat tukkup peritum muraṇākum. oliyaṉukku muṉpu yāṉ eḻutiya viḷakkam koṇṭu teḷika. vaṭamoḻikkum piṟa emmoḻik kuṅkūṭa muṉivar karuttup poruntātu. piṟitoṉṟaṟ killāt taṉakkeṉṟa poruḷāṟṟal uṭaiyatē eḻuttu allatu oliyaṉ eṉappaṭum. itaṉkaṇ kalappukku iṭaṉillai. muṉivar karuttiṉai uṭaṉpaṭṭāl, tamiḻukku uyiroliyaṉ mūṉṟē; akara mutalaveḻuttellām; eṉpataṉai nōkkiṉ akaram oṉṟē eñciniṟkum. tamiḻ nūṟpayiṟci yoṉṟumē yuṭaiyār poruḷuṇarār eṉṟu muṉivar perumakaṉ uraiyāciriyar uḷḷiṭṭōraip paḻitturaittālum, ettuṇai moḻip payiṟciyāḷarum tamiḻiṉ uṇmai kāṇpatē meynneṟiyākum.
nūṟpā: 62 mayilainātar karuttu
“kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ
   oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum”
eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār ivvāṟu kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalām eṉṟārōveṉiṉ.
nuntai yukaraṅ kuṟuki voḻimutaṟkaṇ
vanta teṉiṉuyirmey yāmaṉaittum - cantik
kuyirmutalā vantaṉaiyum meyyuṇarcci yiṉṟi
mayalaṇaiyu meṉṟataṉai māṟṟu.
ivaṟṟai viritturaittu vitiyum vilakkum aṟintu koḷka” eṉa mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu naṉþṉūl uraiyāciriyar mayilainātar tolkāppiyattai vēkamākac cāṭuvar.
nakarameyyē moḻimutal eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṉ poruḷ eṉṟu naṭainōkkilum puṇarcci nōkkilum innūṟpāviṉ tiṟaṉuraiyil muṉpu nāṉ viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. nūṟpāviṉ poruḷ ituvātaliṉ mayilainātariṉ tolkāppiyak kuṟṟappāṭṭukku iṭaṉillai.
iṉi mayilainātar kūṟṟaiyum vēṟuvakaiyāl viḷakkuvōm. kuṟṟiyalukarattaip poṟuttavarai ukarattaiyum ukaram ēṟiya valleḻuttai yum oṟṟumai nayamākak koḷkiṉṟōm. aḵtāvatu ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu eṉṟa iṟuti uyir meykaḷai ōreḻuttākak karutukiṉṟōm. ataṉālaṉṟō eḵku, paricu, pāṭu, eytu paṇpu, paṟṟu eṉpavai īṟṟayal pārttu aṟuvakaik kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷāka eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟukkaḷai īreṉa ōreḻuttākak karutāviṭṭāl ukaramē īrākak koṇṭāl. vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram oṉṟutāṉē irukkamuṭiyum.
itaṉāl nām aṟiyakkiṭappatu eṉṉa? kuṟṟiya ṉukarattaiyum atu ēṟiyameyyaiyum oṉṟākak karutuvatē ilakkaṇak kōṭpāṭām. atupōl nuntai eṉṟa moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum `nu' eṉpataṉaip pirittu nakaiyāṭāmal, oṉṟākak koḷḷavēṇṭum. `kuṟṟiyalukaram nakaramoṭu mutalum' eṉṟu oru naṭaiceyta tolkāppiyak karuttum ituvē.
cuṭṭu viṉā vaḻi akarattai oṭṭi ṅakaram mutalāka varum eṉṟu naṉṉūl kūṟum ariya varavai etirtiṟaṉ ceyyāta mayilainātar kuṟṟiyalukarattai yoṭṭi nakaram mutalākum eṉṟa ariya ilakkaṇattaiyum ēṟpatē poruntum.
nūṟpā 63
moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram nuntai muṟaip peyaraḷavil amaintu niṟṟalāṉum vēṟupāṭiṉmaiyāṉum taṉippuṇarcci viti vēṇṭiyatillai. kuṟṟiyalukara nakarap puṇarcci vitikaḷē cālum eṉpatu eṉ uraimuṭivu.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urai: `kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟpōla nuntai eṉṟu itaḻkavittu muṟṟakkūṟiya viṭattum itaḻ kuviyāmal kuṟaiyat kūṟiyaviṭattum oru kūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē takkavāṟu kāṇka” eṉpatu ivar urai. nuntai paṟṟi eḻutiya karuttu okkum; āṉāl kātu kaṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramāyiṉ viṉaiyākavum kuṟṟukaramāyiṉ peyarākavum poruḷpaṭum eṉṟu oru putiya koḷkaiyaik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ilakkaṇap perumpulavar vēṅkaṭa rācaṉārum ikkoḷkaiyai uṭaṉpaṭuvar. “moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram peyarccollilē niṟṟalāṉ muṟṟukaramāka olikkappaṭiṉum poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu” eṉṟataṉāṉē moḻiyiṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramākavum olikkum eṉpatum avvāṟu olittal viṉaic colliṉ kaṇṇē eṉpatum āṇṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉpatum pōtarum” eṉpatum ivar tarum viḷakkam. “appeyar maruṅkiṉ” eṉpatu nūṟpā. akarac cuṭṭai otukki viṭṭu peyar maruṅkiṉ eṉa vaittukkoṇṭu viḷakkañ ceykiṟār. itu virittāyntu maṟukkavēṇṭiya koḷkaiyātaliṉ, ivvurai takumiṭaṉaṉṟu; cila cuṭṭiyamaivēṉ.
“neṭṭeḻut timparum toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum
  kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē”
eṉpatu kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟum moḻimarapu nūṟpā. ivvukaram peyarccoṟkaḷil varum eṉṟa kuṟippumillai; viṉaiccollil neṭṭeḻuttimparum toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟum varum ukaram muṟṟukaramāka olikkum eṉṟa kuṟippum illai. peyarviṉai paṟṟiya pēccē illai. kuṟṟiyalukaram varum cūḻnilai itu eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ poruḷ. iccūḻnilaiyil nāṭu eṉpatu peyarāka varuka, viṉaiyāka varuka. evvāṟu variṉum kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpatuvē muṭivu. nāṭaṭaintāṉ eṉap peyarāka iruppiṉum ataṉai nāṭeṉṟāṉ eṉa viṉaivāka iruppiṉum puṇarcci muṭipu oṉṟātal kāṇka.
“muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāatu
  appeyar maruṅkiṉ nilaiyiya lāṉa”
eṉṟa nūṟpā nuntai muṟṟiyalukaramākavum olikkum eṉṟu kūṟavillai. avvāṟu koḷvōmēl moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉpatu valuviḻantu vēṇṭāta oṉṟeṉa ākiviṭātā? `iraṇṭutalaiyiṭṭa mutalākirupaḵtu” eṉṟu puṇariyalil moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟ ēṉai mutaṉilai yoppa naṉmatippuk koṭuttaliṉ muṟṟiyalukaramākavum olikkum eṉpatu poruntātu. muṟṟiyalukarattiṟkuriya nakarap puṇarcci viti peṟum eṉavum appuṇarcci yoṟṟumaiyāl immuṟaip peyar poruḷ vēṟṟumaip paṭātu eṉavum teḷivippatē innūṟpāviṉ karuttu.
innūṟpāvai moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya nuntaiyaḷaviṟ koḷḷātu. īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkuk koḷvatum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramellām muṟṟiyalukaramum ākum eṉak koḷvatum, aṅṅaṉam ākuṅkāl iruvakai yukaramum peyarum viṉaiyumākap poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉak koḷvatum ellām karuttuc cumattalākum. inta nūṟpā ivvaḷavu perum moḻikkoḷkaikku iṭaṉ tarumā?
`appeyar maruṅkiṉ nilaiyiyalāṉa' eṉṟu muṟaip peyarccuṭṭu teḷivāka iruppavum akaraccuṭṭai viṭuttu, `peyar maruṅkiṉ' eṉa pirittukkoṇṭu, kuṟṟiyalukara mellām peyarām; muṟṟiyalukaramellām muṉṉilai viṉaiyām eṉṟu palapaṭak kūṟuvatu uraiyikal eṉka. iṉṉa vīṟellām peyarākum. iṉṉavīṟellām viṉaiyākum eṉṟa pākupāṭum tamiḻiyalpaṉṟu.


nūṟpā 71
iḷampūraṇar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraikaḷ:
tapu eṉpatu ōcaivēṟṟumaiyāl iruporuḷ tarum eṉavum paṭuttuccolla nī cā eṉat taṉviṉaiyām; eṭuttuc colla nī oṉṟaṉaic cāvi eṉap piṟaviṉaiyām eṉavum uraiceykuvar. ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāl ilakkaṇa vēṟṟumaiyō poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyō yuṟutal tamiḻiyalpaṉṟu. tattam kuṟippum poruḷ tarum um ē ō eṉṟa iṭaiccoṟkaṭkuk kūṭa āciriyar ōcai vēṟṟumaippāṭu collavillai. peyaraiyum viṉaiyaiyum terinilaiviṉaiyaiyum kuṟippu viṉaiyaiyum viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyaraiyum toṭar nilaiyālallatu ōcaiyāl vēṟupāṭuṇaralām eṉṟu tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟiṟṟilatu. innūṟpāvilum; `itu ceyal vēṇṭum' eṉṟa viṉaiyiyalnūṟpāvilum iruvayiṉ nilaiyai ōcai kāṭṭum eṉṟu cuṭṭak kāṇōm. eṉavē mīyoliyaṉ eṉṟa olikkūṟu tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyillai eṉpatu teḷivu.


nūṟpā: 57. peruviḷakkak kaṭṭurai - iṇaippu
tamiḻil moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varampu
(itu tamiḻppalkalaikkaḻakam 28-3-1985 il kūṭṭiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇak kōṭpāṭṭu vaḷarccik karuttaraṅkil va.cupa.māṇikkam paṭitta āyvukkaṭṭurai)
tamiḻmoḻikku uriya eḻuttukkaḷ ittuṇaiya eṉavum avai uyir, mey, cārpu eṉṟa perumpirivum, kuṟil neṭil valliṉam melliṉam iṭaiyiṉam eṉṟa uṭpirivum paṭum eṉavum ellā ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum toṭakkattē kaṟpip pataik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ataṉoṭu amaiyātu coṟku mutalāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṟutiyāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṭainilai mayakkākavarum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉavāṅku mutalīṟiṭai nilaikaḷ niralākac cuṭṭappaṭuvataiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ciṟuvakuppukkut tamiḻ payiṟṟum ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilum mutal īṟu iṭainilai paṟṟiya varampukaḷ iṭampeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. ayalnāṭṭavarkkuk kaṟpikkum payiṟci kāṭṭikaḷilum im muttaṉmaikaḷ olikaḷiṉ āyvoṭum viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tolkāppiyam mutalāvatu nūṉmarapil iṭainilai mayakkattaik kūṟi iraṇṭāvatu moḻimarapil mutaleḻuttu īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷai virivāka iyampum. naṉṉūl eḻuttiyalil mutaṉilai iṭainilai iṟutinilai mūṉṟaiyum ivvaricaiyiṟ kūṟum. vīracōḻiya mutalāṉanūlkaḷum immarapaip pōṟṟi moḻikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mutalīṟu iṭai eṉṟa mukkūṟu eḻuttilakkaṇa vitikaṭkellām aṭicamayam eṉpatu oruvāṟu uṇarappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷmoḻi mutaleḻuttiṉ varalāṟum vaḷarcciyum varampum etirnōkkum paṟṟi ik kaṭṭuraiyil cila neṟikaḷai ārāyvōm.

ippaṭṭiyaṟkāṭciyiliruntu aṟiyattakuvaṉa:
a. vēṟupāṭiṉmai : (80 eḻuttukkaḷ)
1. paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum mutaṉmoḻiyātal
2. ka ta na pa ma ellāvuyiroṭum varutal
3. vakaram (u ū o ō nīṅkalāka) eṭṭuyiroṭum varutal
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻp pērakarātiyililum māṟāvit taṉmaiyaik kāṇalām. ay, av eṉṟa pōlikku mutaṉmai koṭuttu, ai, oḷa eṉṉum neṭilkaḷai vilakkavillai eṉpatu kuṟippiṭat takum ivvāṟu mūvāyiram āṇṭukku mēlākat tolkāppiyar kālamutal oru peṟṟittāka innalvaravai aṟikiṟōm.
ā. vēṟupāṭu
cakarameymmutal tolkāppiyam	9	a, ai, oḷa nīṅkalāka
	ilakkaṇa viḷakkam	10	ai, oḷa nīṅkalāka

ilakkaṇaviḷakkamum tamiḻnūlum tamakku muṟpaṭṭa vīracōḻiyam nēminātam naṉṉūl mutaliyavaṟṟaip piṉpaṟṟa villai. aḵtāvatu caiyum cauvum mutalāvatai uṭaṉpaṭṭila.
ñakarameymmutal

i. viḷakkam :	(1)	ā

muttuvīriyam	(6)	a ā u ū o oḷa
tamiḻnūl	 (1) : ā
ilakkaṇaviḷakkamum tamiḻnūlum iṭaippaṭṭa nūlkaḷaic cārātu tolkāppiya vaḻic cellukiṉṟaṉa tamiḻppērakarāti, vīracōḻiya naṉṉūl pōla yakarattukku āṟuyirkoḷḷum. yippōtu, yeppōtu eṉac cila eḻuttu vaḻakku iruppiṉum avai ēṟkappaṭavillai. 
tolkāppiyam kūṟiya nuntai eṉṉum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram piṉṉar vīḻcciyāyiṟṟu. ivvilakkaṇattai uṭaṉpaṭṭāl meymmutaṟpuṇarcci eṉṟa nilai illātu pōkum eṉṟu mayilainātar orupāṅku maṟutturaippar. naṉṉūlār koḻukompupaṭa moḻinta ñakara mutaṉ moḻiyum piṉṉar kaṭaipōkavillai. `avvai oṭṭi ṅavvum mutalākum' eṉṟa tiṟaṉil naṭaiyē ṅakaram ēṉai meykaḷukku nikarāṉataṉṟu eṉpataik kāṭṭum eṉac caṅkara namaccivāyar teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷār.
uraiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ
mēṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuḷ cakara ñakara yakara mutaṉmoḻikaḷiṭai ilakkaṇikaḷ vēṟupaṭak kāraṇam yātu? ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal' eṉpatu vitiyātaliṉ, ilakkiyam iruppavum vēṟupaṭal takumā? uḷḷattaip paṭṭāṅku uṭaṉpaṭuvatu ilakkaṇamā?  uḷḷatait tiṟaṉāyntu cīrtūkkuvatu ilakkaṇamā? ivai vampāṉa viṉākkaḷ alla. karuttu vēṟṟumai matikkum viṉākkaḷ.
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum caṅkappaṉuvalkaḷ, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai, cintāmaṇi, peruṅkatai teyvap pācuraṅkaḷ mutalāṉavaṟṟil putiya moḻi mutalkaḷ palavāṟu pukuntukiṭappatai aṟiyātārallar.
yūkamoṭu māmuka mucukkalai (muruku.300)
yūpam naṭṭa viyaṉkaḷam palakol (puṟam.15)
yūpa neṭuntūṇ vēta vēḷvi (puṟam.224)
piṇaiyūpam eḻuntāṭa (maturaik. 27).
talainumin teñciya poṉmaliyūpamoṭu (patiṟ. 67)
uñcaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya yūkiyantaṇaṉ (maṇi.15)
nāka naṉṉāṭṭu nāṉūṟi yōcaṉai (maṇi.9)
pakuvāy ñamaliyoṭu paimputal (porun.112)
tāḻaṭumpu malainta puṇarivaḷai ñarala (patiṟ.30)
terikōl ñamaṉṉ pōla (puṟam.6)
vari ñimiṟu imiṟum mārpupiṇi makaḷir (patiṟ.50)
illat tuṇaikkup pāleyta iṟaiyamaṉ (pari.11)
yavaṉar ōtima viḷakkiṉ (perumpāṇ. 315)
valipuṇar yākkai vaṉkaṇ yavaṉar (mullaip. 61)
yavaṉar iyaṟṟiya viṉaimāṇpāvai (neṭunal. 101)
iṉṉaṇam pērilakkiyaṅkaḷil vantukiṭakkum palvēṟu (yū, yō, ñī, ya) moḻi mutalikaḷaiyum cakaṭam, camam, cantu, cantam, cavaṭṭum, caivam, caiṉam mutalāṉa varavukaḷaiyum nam tolluraiyāciriyarkaḷ kāṇātār eṉṟuaṟiyāmai ēṟṟa muṭiyumā? cevvaṉ kaṇṭuvaittu iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum eḻuttatikāravuraiyil koḷḷāmaikkuc cila viḷakkaṅkaḷ varaintuḷḷaṉar. avai varumāṟu:
1. 	cakaṭam eṉavum caiyam eṉavu; vilakkiṉavum varumāveṉiṉ avaṟṟuḷ āriyac citaivallātaṉa kaṭi collillai eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. (iḷam)
2. 	ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāveṉiṉ avai aḻivaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka. (iḷam)
3. 	yavaṉar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāveṉiṉ avai āriyac citaiveṉṟu maṟukka. (iḷam)
4. 	caṭṭi cakaṭam camaḻppu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa kaṭi collillai eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. caiyam cauriyam eṉpavaṟṟai vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka (nac)
5.	‘ñamalitanta maṉavuc cūluṭumpu’ eṉpatu ticaiccol, ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa iḻivaḻakku (nac)
6.	yavaṉar yutti yukam yōkam yauvaṉam eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka (nac)
7. 	ñeṇṭu eṉpatu ñaṇṭu āyp piṉṉar naṇṭu eṉa marīiyiṟṟu (nac.col)
8. 	campu, caḷḷai, caṭṭi, camaḻppu eṉa varum. ivai toṉṟutoṭṭu vantaṉavāyiṉ mutalākāvaṟṟiṉ kaṇ 

cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai oḷa veṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē
eṉa vilakkār āciriyar. ataṉāṉ avai piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya collēyām eṉpatu (cēṉā)
uraineṟiyaṅkaḷ
tolkāppiyavuraiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ kuṟippiliruntu, moḻi mutaleḻuttukkōḷ paṟṟic cila neṟiyaṅkaḷai avar tam karuttākak koḷḷalām.
a. vaṭacoṟkaḷ
ā. āriyaccitaivukaḷ
i. ticaiccoṟkaḷ
ī. aḻi vaḻakkukkaḷ
u. maruviya vaṭivaṅkaḷ
tollilakkiyaṅkaḷil yūkam, yūpam, yūki, yōcaṉai eṉpaṉa payiṉṟiruntālum mutaṟkāraṇattāl maṟukkap paṭṭaṉa. ñāla, ñamaṉ, ñimīṟu pōṉṟaṉa tirivaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟa karuttāl oppup peṟavillai pōlum. maruviya urumāṟikaṭku ilakkaṇa vīṟu koṭuttāl, nāḷ vaḻakkil viraivu paṭṭum uṇarccivayappaṭṭum uṟuppuk kuṟaipaṭṭum pēṇātu pēcum olittiri pellām mutaleḻuttāki vantuviṭum. reṇṭu, rempa, rompa, rēḻi, rāppiccai, rāvutal, rampam, rāttiri, rāvāṇṭai, rācaṉ, rattam, rakkai, rāppi, lakku, lāttu lāvāṇṭai eṉṟāṅku tamiḻccoṟkaḷē innilai eytumeṉiṉ, ayaṟcoṟkaḷiṉ tākkam tamiḻmuḻakkamāki viṭātā? eṉavē nam paṇṭai uraiyāciriyarmārkaḷ koccai yuruttamiḻum vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻiṉ moḻimutal ilakkaṇavarampukku aṭippaṭaiyākā eṉṟu  koṇṭa moḻic cemmai teḷivākum. iṭaik kālattup putiya moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ taḻuvi vīracōḻiya mutalāṉa nūlkaḷaip pārttum vaṭamoḻi mikuntu pukunta ilakkiyap paṉuvalkaḷaip pārttum ataṉpiṉṉarē ivvāṟu oru naṉmuṭivu kaṇṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻi matippu mikka uraip perumakaṉ nacciṉārkkiṉiyar patiṉāṉkāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭamoḻi pērātikkam ceyta kālattil:- “kālam, ulakam eṉpaṉa vaṭacol alla; āciriyar (tolkāppiyar) vaṭacoṟkaḷai eṭuttōti ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟār ākaliṉ” (kiḷavi.58) eṉṟu aṟuti yiṭṭuraittiruppatu eṉṟum naccutaṟkuriya iṉiya eḻuttākum.
iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum piṟkāla vaḷarcciyil caṭṭi cakaṭam pōlum cakara mutaṉmoḻi yoṉṟai ottuk koṇṭārkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟavaṟṟai uṭaṉpaṭavillai. vaṭamoḻic cārpuṭaiya cēṉāvaraiyarum `kaṭicollillai kālat tuppaṭivē' eṉṟa nūṟpāvil cakaramutalaic cuṭṭiṉārēyaṉṟi cai, cau, ya, yu, yū, yau mutalāyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭavillai. ilakkiyaṅkaḷil putiya mutaleḻuttukkaḷ peruki vantiruntum ittollurai yāḷarkaḷ avaṟṟait taḻuvāmaikku mēṟcuṭṭiya neṟiyaṅkaḷē kāraṇamākum. cakarattai maṭṭum taḻuvik koṇṭataṟku iccakaramoḻital tamiḻiliruntē piṟantaṉa eṉpatu kāraṇamām. iṉṉōr neṟimaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi ilakkaṇa viḷakka nūlārum tamiḻnūlārum moḻi mutaleḻuttu vakuppārāyiṉar.
vīracōḻiyam nēminātam naṉṉūl, cāminātam muttuvīriyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ, pukuntavai vaṭacolleṉap pārātu. ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam kūṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa potu neṟiyil cai, cau, ya,yu, yū, yō, yo eṉṟa mutalikaḷaiyum ēṟpārāyiṉar. tolkāppiyattukkuppiṉ ivvaṭamoḻikaḷum ticaiccoṟkaḷum tamiḻiṉkaṇ payiṟṟu vantamaiyāl koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu eṉpar civañāṉa muṉivar. ivarkaḷ muttiraiyē piṟkālap peruvaḻikkāyiṟṟu. īṇṭuṅkūṭa 11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya vīracōḻiya kāla mutal 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta muttuvīriyamvarai vēṟu putumoḻi mutalkaḷukku iṭamillai eṉpatu niṉaiyattakum.
maṉṉiya meyvarampu
itukāṟum kūṟiya karuttut tokutiyiliruntu moḻimutal varalāṟṟil maṉṉiya oru pēruṇmaiyai eṭuttukkāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tolkāppiyak kālatti liruntu ituvarai vanta moḻimutal eṇṇikkaiyeḻuttai nōkkiṉ 94 - 105 eṉṟa vaṭṭattuḷ vēṟupāṭu aṭaṅkum. cakarattil mūṉṟum yakarattil aintum piṟavaṟṟil mūṉṟumē kūṭiyavai eṉa aṟiyappaṭum. mūvāyiram āṇṭumoḻi varalāṟṟil māṟṟam 10 viḻukkāṭu cālap peritaṉṟu. innilaiyilum māṟiya iṭam etu? māṟāta iṭam etu eṉṟu cīrtūkka vēṇṭum.
tolkāppiyar kūṟiya uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum moḻi mutalākum eṉṟa ilakkaṇam māṟavē illai. atu maṭṭumaṉṟu enta meykaḷ moḻi mutalākumeṉṟu aṉṟu vitittārō avaiyē kūṭutal kuṟaiviṉṟip piṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. eṉpatu viyappāṉa ceytiyākum. ka, ca, ta, pa, ña, ma, ya, va eṉṟu valliṉattil nāṉkum melliṉattil mūṉṟum iṭai yiṉattil iraṇṭum āka oṉpatu meykaḷ tolkāppiyattiyal varampuppaṭṭaṉa. piṉ vanta enta ilakkaṇa nūlāvatu ṟa, ṭa, ṇa, ṉa, ra, la, ḻa, ḷa eṉṟa mey mutalāka varum eṉṟu mēlum cērntuḷḷatā? illaiyē. innūlkaḷ cērttaṉavellām tolkāppiyam vēliyiṭṭa meyttaḷattil, cakarattiṉmēl mūṉṟuyirum yakarattiṟku aintuyirum kūṭutalāka ēṟivarum eṉṟa uyirp perukkamēyām. eṉavē moḻi mutalākum paṉṉīruyirilum oṉpatu meyyilum māṟāviyaṟkaiyai aṟutiyākak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ikkaṭṭurai moḻimutalāyvil putitākak kaṇṭa muṭipu itu. tamiḻmoḻi eṉṟum viḷaṅkun taṉmaiyum cemmaiyum peṟṟiyiruppataṟku moḻi mutalvarampu peruṅkaraiyākum.
moḻimutal immeyvarampu, vaṭamoḻi evvaḷavu kalappiṉum, uṟutiyāka mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉpataṉai ilakkiyattālum ilakkaṇattālum teḷikiṉṟām. vaṭamoḻiyil ellāveḻuttum collukku mutalāki varuvatum taṉimeyyum mutalāki varuvatum uṇṭu. ammoḻiyiṉ iyalpu atu. avai tamiḻukku varuṅkāl enta vaṭaveḻuttu entat tamiḻeḻuttāka māṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu cuṭṭi olivēli kōliya perumai puttamittirarkkum pavaṇantiyārkkum uṇṭu. irumoḻikkum uyirilum meyyilum potuveḻuttāka uḷḷa nilai cikkalaṟṟatu ātaliṉ ciṟappu nilai eḻuttukkaḷukku māṟṟuvaḻi kāṭṭiṉar.
vaṭanūl marapum taḻuvit tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟuval eṉṟu veḷippaṭac colliya puttamittiraṉār ulōpam, vekuviriki, taṟpuruṭaṉ, cāmacam, tuvantuvam, kaṉmatārayaṉ viyañcaṉam, pirakiruti, tattitam, kāritam eṉavāṟu vaṭamoḻik kuṟiyīṭukaḷai aḷḷik koṇṭa pōtum, tamiḻoli varampaik kaṭṭik kāttār.
“kūṭṭeḻut tippiṉ yaralakkaḷ tōṉṟiṭiṟ kūṭṭiṭaiyē
  ōṭṭeḻut tākap peṟumo rikāram vavvukko ruvvām
  mīṭṭeḻut tuttami ḻallaṉa pōmvēṟu tēyaccolliṉ
  māṭṭeḻut tummita ṉālaṟi maṟṟai vikārattiṉē”	 (59)
eṉṟa kārikaiyilum vēṟu iraṇṭu kārikaikaḷilum altamiḻ oliccoṟkaḷ mutal īṟu iṭainilaiyil tamiḻāntaṉmaiyai vitikkiṉṟār. `tamiḻallaṉa pōm' eṉṟu ātti cūṭi pōla oliyilum kūṟukiṉṟār.
vīracōḻiyattai ivvakaiyil cevvaṉ piṉpaṟṟum pavaṇantiyār vaṭamoḻic colveḷḷam karaiyuṭaittu ōṭuvataik kaṇṭu pataviyalil nāṉku nūṟpākkaḷ yāttaṉar. iṉṉa vaṭacol varalām. varakkūṭātu eṉṟu poruḷpaṟṟik kūṟavillai eṉpatum, olinilai māṟṟattukkē varampiṭṭār eṉpatum, tamiḻiṉa olik kāppukku ivvarampu cempiṭṭuc ceyta,¨iñciyāyiṟṟu eṉpatum niṉaiyattakum.
“ravviṟ kammuta lāmuk kuṟilum
  lavviṟ kimmuta liraṇṭum yavviṟ
  kiyyu momuḻita lāki muṉ varumē” 	(148)
ra, la, ya eṉpavai tamiḻil illāta meykaḷalla, uḷḷavaiyē. eṉiṉum maṉṉiya meyvarampu kākkum nōkkōṭu pavaṇantiyār arakkaṉ, arataṉam ilāpam, irāku, ulōpam, iyakkaṉ eṉa uyireḻuttukkaḷai oṭṭiṉār. ittakaiya māṟṟam pārvaikkuc ciṟiyatākat tōṉṟiṉum, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ kaṭṭukkōppukkup pēraraṇāyiṟṟu. moḻiyiṉ amaippu ituvātaliṉ, maṉṉiya meykaḷ māṟāmal ilaṇṭaṉ, ilēcu, urōmāpuri, uruciyā, urūpā, ireyil, iracītu, iliṅkam, irāci, irēkai, irāṇuvam, irāmaliṅkam, irāmacāmi, irāmāṉucam, irāypakatūr, irāmēccuram, iraṅkūṉ eṉa makkaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil iyalpāka eḻutuvataiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
tamiḻ olikkūṭṭamaippu
iṭaikkālak kāppiyaṅkaḷilum piṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum ūr vakaiyālum peyarvakaiyālum tuṟaivakaiyālum vaṭamoḻit tākkam mikuti eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. irāmaṉ, ilakkuvaṉ, irāvaṇaṉ, iraṇiyaṉ, uruttirattaṉ, patumāvati, cītattaṉ, cītaraṉ. irācamāpuram, iratanūpuram, irattiṉa pallavam, titi, puṉaruṟpavam, cakacamalam, attuvitam, piraṇavam mutalāṉa coṟkūṭṭamellām vaṭacollā yiṉum, vīramāmuṉivariṉ tamiḻttōṟṟampōla, mutalīṟu iṭainilaikkaṇ tamiḻurup peṟṟu amaikiṉṟaṉa.
cīvaka cintāmaṇi, peruṅkatai, cūḷāmaṇi, irāmāyaṇam. pāratam ellām taḻuvu kāppiyaṅkaḷāyiṉum poruḷaiyum collaiyum taḻuviṉavēyaṉṟi vaṭamoḻi yolik kaṭṭamaippait taḻuvavillai eṉpatum, puṟavoliyamaippait tamiḻoliyamaippukku uṭpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉa eṉpatum nām karutavēṇṭiya teṟiyākum. vaṭamoḻikkum tamiḻmoḻikkum ilakkaṇam oṉṟē; aḵtāvatu vaṭamoḻiyilakkaṇamē tamiḻilakkaṇamām eṉṟa poyyāṉa orumaikkōṭpāṭu kāṇattuṭitta pirayōka vivēka nūlāciriyarum ivvoli māṟṟattai aṟavē otukkiviṭa muṭiyavillai. “potuveḻuttāyp paṟpala vākit tirivatum cāṟṟiṉar paṇṭuṇarntōr” eṉṟu uṭaṉpaṭuvar.
“ilayitta taṉṉilila yittatāma lattāl
   ilayitta vāṟuḷatā vēṇṭum - ilayitta
   tattitiyi veṉṉi ṉaḻiyā tavaiyaḻiva
   tattitiyu mātiyumā maṅku.
lakaram tamiḻil moḻi mutalātu eṉpataṉāl, iccivañāṉa pōta veṇpāvil tavamutalvar meykaṇṭār ikarattaic cērttu ilayittatu eṉṟu varampu naṉikaṭaip piṭittiruppatu moḻimutal ilakkaṇattiṉ oḻuṅkaikkāṭṭum. iv varampiṉai aṭi toṭai koṇṭa ceyyuḷiṭattēyum nam muṉṉōr māṟātu pōṟṟiṉar eṉṟāl, nekiḻccimikka urainaṭaiyil kaikkoḷvatu maṉamiruntāl naṉi eḷitu eṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
“vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut  torīi
   eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta colla kummē” 	(tol.884)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam vaṭamoḻit taḻuvilakkaṇattaic curukkamākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. vīracōḻiyam naṉṉūl pōla iṉṉa vaṭaveḻuttu iṉṉatamiḻeḻuttākum eṉṟu taṉi vitappuc ceyyavillai. eṉiṉum“eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta col”eṉṟa nuṭpattil cila kuṟippu aṭaṅkiyuḷa. īṇṭu eḻuttu eṉpatu eḻuttilakkaṇattaik kuṟittu niṟkiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam varum vaṭacol moḻimutal, moḻiyīṟu, moḻiyiṭai eṉpavaṟṟilum tamiḻccol vaṭivupeṟal vēṇṭum eṉavum olimāṟṟam tamiḻc collamaippukku ottirukka vēṇṭum eṉavum koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ikkuṟippiṉai uṇarntē vīracōḻiyarum naṉþṉūlārum ivvilakkaṇattai mēlum virivupaṭuttiṉar.
vēṟu ayaṉmoḻikaḷ
vīracōḻiyam `vēṟu tēyac colliṉ māṭṭeḻuttum itaṉālaṟi' eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyallāta vēṟu ayalmoḻi kaḷiṉ kalappiṟkum etiratu nōkkik kaṭṭamaippuc cuṭṭi yuḷḷatu. ulakap pēruṟavil palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ tākkam ceytik karuvikaḷāl taṭukkavoṇṇātu vaḷarntu varum, iññāṉṟu, moḻi mutaṉilaikaḷ paṟṟi muṉṉōrtam neṟiyaṅkaḷai nām cīrtūkkak kaṭamaip paṭṭirukkiṉṟōm. piṟa tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ, āṅkilam, piṟa ayaṉmoḻikaḷ ivaṟṟilellām vaṭamoḻi yotta olikaḷē perumpālum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻiyil illāta olikaḷ piṟa moḻikaḷil cilaviṉum cilavē. ātaliṉ nammuṉṉōr vaṭamoḻi māṟṟukkuk kūṟiya olippaṭu varampukaḷai vēṟu ayalmoḻikaṭkum ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām. ataṉpiṉṉum cila moḻikaṭkuc cila ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷ irukkumeṉiṉ, tēvaippaṭiṉ, iṉṉatamiḻettākat tiriya vēṇṭum eṉṟu tēvaikkēṟpa viti ceytu koḷḷalām. evvāṟāyiṉum, tolkāppiyamum piṟkāla ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum māṟātu teḷinta moḻi mutal eḻuttukkaḷaiyum, maṉṉiya meym mutalikaḷaiyum pōṟṟik kāttu avvaṭṭattiṟkuḷ ayaṉmoḻi varavai aṭakkikkoḷḷavēṇṭum.
ulakam peritāyiṉum ovvoru viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkum matikkattakka iṭavarampum veḷi eṉṟu collattakka matippilāp puṟam pōkkum iruppatupōla moḻikaṭkum uṇṭu. atilum ivvarampu tamiḻukkuc ceṟivāka uṇṭu. piṟamoḻikaḷil iṉṉa ivveḻuttut tāṉ moḻi mutalākalām. ātaṟkillai eṉṟa vitivilakku iruppatāka yāṉaṟintavarai kāṇōm. ammoḻikkuriya akarātikaḷaip pārttāl itu pulappaṭum. ammoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷil, uyireḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu, neṭil, kuṟil, iṉṉavakaip pākupāṭu cuṭṭappaṭuvataṉṟi, moḻi taṉilaikaḷ kūṟappaṭa villai.
puṇarccikkōḷ
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nilaivēṟu. mutaṟkaṇ kuṟittapaṭi moḻi mutal, moḻiyīṟu, moḻiyiṭai eṉṟa mukkaḷaṉ tamiḻil eḻuttilakkaṇattukku iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra oṉpatu iyalkaḷil piṉṉuḷḷa ēḻiyalkaḷ puṇarccic ceykai paṟṟiyaṉa, nūṉmarapu, moḻi marapu eṉṟa mutaliraṇṭum puṇarnilaikku vēṇṭiya karuvi kaḷaiyum aṭittaḷaṅkaḷaiyum varampaṟukkum iyalkaḷākum.
puṇarcci eṉpatu nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu iyaivatu, iyaiyuṅkāl peṟum iyalpukaḷaiyum tiripukaḷaiyum virivupaṭak kūṟuvatu.
“uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
  uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum” 	(tol.107)
“niṟutta colliṉ īṟā keḻuttoṭu
  kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyaiya” 	(tol.108)
“niṉṟacol līṟum varuñcol mutalum niravi”	(vīra. 9)
“meyyuyir mutalīṟām iru pataṅkaḷum” 	(naṉ. 151)
eṉa varum palvēṟu puṇarvu nūṟpākkaḷil col paṟṟip pēcātu īṟum mutalum paṟṟiya aṭippaṭaiyil puṇarcci cuṭṭappaṭuvatāl, evveḻuttu īṟākum, evveḻuttu mutalākum eṉṟa moḻikkaṇippu tamiḻukku oruvantam vēṇṭaṟpāṟṟu. moḻi marapu eṉṟa iraṇṭāvatu iyal pattu nūṟpākkaḷāl mutaṉilai paṟṟiyum, patiṉāṉku nūṟpāk kaḷāl iṟutinilai paṟṟiyum kaṟpikkiṉṟatu. itupārvaikkuc ciṟṟilakkaṇamākat tōṉṟiṉālum oṉṟu mutal pattuvarai eṇṇa aṟiyātāṉ katiyē inta ilakkaṇa vaṟivilakku ēṟpaṭum.
`meṉmai yum iṭaimaiyum varūuṅkālai'	(tol.130)
`moḻimuta lākum ellā veḻuttum
varuvaḻi niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum 	( 147)
`aḷaviṟkum niṟaiyiṟkum moḻimuta lāki
uḷaveṉap paṭṭa oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē' 	(170)
`valleḻut tiyaiyiṉ avveḻuttu mikumē' 	(  357)
`melleḻut tiyaiyiṉavveḻut tākum' 	( 380)
iṉṉaṇam varum iṭaṅkaḷil ellām melleḻuttu, iṭaiyeḻuttu, valleḻuttu, moḻi mutaleḻuttu eṉa varumoḻi curukkamākac cuṭṭapaṭṭatēṉṟi iṉṉa taṉiyeḻuttu eṉṟu cuṭṭavillai. ataṟkuk kāraṇam moḻimarapil ivai paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ uṇṭu. eṉavē tamiḻppuṇarccikku īṟṟu nilai, mutalnilai eṉṟa irumuṉaiyil vaḷarntuḷḷa māṟā, māṟṟaviyalā varalāṟṟamaippiṉai nām uṇarkiṉṟōm.
eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷuḷ eḻuttatikāram īṟu mutal iṇainta puṇarcciyatikāramākum. tamiḻil moḻiyiyalpum oliyiyalpum mayaṅkātu terikkum kaṭṭumāṉa atikāramumākum eḻuttum collum moḻi paṟṟiya irukūṟukaḷē. moḻiyatikāram eṉṟa oru peyarāl ivviraṇṭiṉaiyum aṭakki ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyirukkalāmē. ēṉ aṅṅaṉam muṉṉōr kūṟavillai? eḻuttatikāram mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭi tamiḻukku virintu paranta iṉṟiyamaiyāp puṇarccik kaṭṭamaippiṉaik kūṟuvatātaliṉ, taṉiyatikāram eṉṟa ciṟappu aḷikka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu.
ippēruṇmaiyai niṉaiviṟ koṇṭāl moḻimutal eḻuttu varalāṟṟil ēṟpaṭṭa maṉṉiya oḻuṅkiyalaip purintu koḷḷaviyalum. paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum ka ca ta pa ña na ma ya va eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum tolkāppiyar kāla mutal maṉṉiya mutalkaḷāka varuvataik kaṇṭōm. tamiḻil taṉimey mutalāka varātu. `uyirmey yallaṉa moḻi mutalākā' eṉṟapaṭi vyāti, tyāṉam, krupai, praṇavam eṉṟu vārāmal, vantāl, viyāti, tiyāṉam, kirupai. piraṇavam eṉṟu uyirmeyyākavē varum. eṉiṉum varumoḻi mutal kūṟumpōtu kaṇ + pārvai; tamiḻ +nūl; vaḷḷai + koṭi; koṭi + yātu eṉpavaṟṟil p, n, k, y eṉat taṉimeykaḷaiyē puṇarccikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷākak karutukiṉṟōm. tamiḻamaip piṉpaṭi, uyirmeyyē collukku mutalāka varumeṉṟālum, puṇarnilaikku vēṟṟumai nayattāl meyyaimaṭṭum koḷkiṉṟōm. ātaliṉmoḻi mutaleḻuttu eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭuṅkāl taṉimeykaḷē kaṇakkiṭappaṭum. muṉṉarē maṉṉiyameykaḷ eṉṟu ivaṟṟaik kuṟippiṭṭikkiṟēṉ.
tolkāppiyarukkuppiṉ kūṭutalāṉa cilavuyirkaḷ cakarameykkum yakarameykkum vantuḷa. meymmēl ēṟiya evvuyirkkum puṇarcciyilakkaṇattu matippillai. muṉṉiṉṟum meyyaḷavukku iyalpum tiripum kūṟappaṭum. ātaliṉ, tolkāppiyattukkum piṉþṉūlkaḷukkum moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷil cila vēṟṟumaikaḷ iruntālum avvēṟṟumaikaḷ ēṟiya uyirpaṟṟiyaṉavātalāl, putiya puṇarccittākkattukku iṭamillai eṉpatu uṇaravēṇṭiya oru karuttu. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ tolkāppiyattup puṇarccikku varumoḻiyākak kūṟappaṭṭa ka ca ta pa ña na  ma ya va eṉṟa maṉṉiya oṉpatu meykaḷē piṉṉūlkaḷilum mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭaṉa. eṉavē varumoḻip puṇarkōṭukaḷ māṟāmai teḷivākum.

putiya moḻi mutal?
toṉṟutoṭṭu itukāṟum palvēṟu māṟutal koṇṭa tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil moḻimutalmey maṭṭum orupeṟṟittāka niṟpataṟkē kāraṇam puṇarccikkōḷ eṉpatu eṉ tuṇivu. itaṉai vēṟoru vakaiyākak kāṇalām. ravai, reṭi, roṭṭi, rēṭiyō, rauṭi mutalāṉa rakarac coṟkaḷaiyum; laṭṭu, lāṭam, luṅki, lēvātēvi eṉṟiṉṉa lakarac coṟkaḷaiyum; ṭamāram, ṭāṇā, ṭilli, ṭelipōṉ mutalāṉa ṭakarac coṟkaḷaiyum ivvāṟē piṟa mutaṟ coṟkaḷaiyum moḻimutalākum eṉṟu ēṟṟukkoṇṭāl eṉṉa? vēṟṟumoḻikaḷil ellām neṭuṅkaṇakkil uḷḷa ellā eḻuttukkaḷum moḻi mutalākumpōtu, tamiḻum avvaḻicceṉṟu vaḷarntāl eṉṉa? eṉṟu nallaṉparkaḷē viṉavalām. viṉavukiṉṟaṉar. viṉavik koṇṭutāṉiruppar. innalviṉāviṟku muḻumaiyāka etirmaṟumoḻikūṟuvataṟku ikkaṭṭurai poṟpaṉṟu eṉiṉum, mūvāyiram āṇṭukku mēlāka ellā ilakkaṇanūlum oru paṭittākap pōṟṟi vanta marapaiyē nāṉ mīṇṭum cuṭṭik kāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟēṉ. rakara lakara ṭakaram pōṉṟa mutaṉmoḻikaḷaik koṇṭāl eḻuttilakkaṇattil ipputu mutalkaṭkup puttilakkaṇam vakukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō?
avar + ravai vāṅkiṉār
cāppāṭu + reṭi
vīṭṭil + rēṭiyō
palar + rauṭiyāyiṉar
pattu + laṭṭu vāṅki vā
kutiraikku + lāṭam kaṭṭiṉar
nālu + luṅki vēṇṭum
kār + ṭayar
avaṉ + ṭilli ceṉṟāḷ
iṅṅaṉam rakara lakara ṭakara mutalāya coṟkaḷ varumpōtu enta nilaimoḻikku muṉ evvāṟellām māṟum eṉa ovvoru meykkum puṇariyalkaḷil viti collavēṇṭum. varumoḻi mutal eṉa oṉṟaik koṇṭāl tamiḻilakkaṇam ataṟkeṉa ōriṭam aḷittu vaḻi cuṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu marapu. ittakaiya marapiṉait tamiḻccol vaṭṭattukkuḷḷē vaittuk koṇṭāl ilakkaṇam taṉittaṉmaiyuṭaiyatākavum curukkamākavum kaṟpārkku vēṇṭumaḷavu viḷakkamākavum irukkum. piṟamoḻik kalappāl varum eḻuttu mutalkaṭṭum eḻuttīṟu kaṭkum ellām puṇarcciyilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭṭāl orumoḻikku ilakkaṇam vakuppatu, varampu kāṭṭuvatu eṉa ākāmal ūrmoḻikkellām vantavāṟu ētō vitippaṟai aṭippatākavē pōymuṭiyum. ōṭivarum nīraiyellām kuṭikkalāmā? oru moḻiyiṉ ilakkaṇa nalamākāl ilakkaṇat toḻunōy ākiviṭum eṉṟu teḷinta nam ilakkaṇa maruttuvarkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālamutal vanta maṉṉiya meykaḷai varumoḻiyākap pōṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. ēṟum uyirkaḷ aḷavil puṇarcci iṭaiyūṟillā māṟṟaṅkaḷait taḻuvikkoṇṭaṉar. eṉavē tamiḻ moḻimutal varampu puṇarcci varampākum eṉṟu ikkaṭṭuraiyāl aṟiyalām.
āytakkēṭu
itteḷinilaiyil cila āṇṭukaḷākap puṟṟīcal pōlap perukivarum varum oru moḻikkēṭṭiṉai īṇṭuccuṭṭuvatu eṉ kaṭamaiyum poṟuppum ām. āytam cārpeḻuttu eṉavum aḵtu eḵtu eṉṟāṟpōlak kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉṉum valleḻuttiṉ muṉṉum iṭaiyē nalintuvarum eṉavum nām aṟivōm. mutaleḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭṭa ṭakara ṟakara lakara ḻakaraṅkaḷē moḻi mutaṉilaiyāka vārātapōtu, nalipu vaṇṇam eṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭa kuṟṟāytam ippōtu pala ayaṟcoṟkaḷil mutalāytamāka vantukoṇṭirukkiṟatu.
ḵpuḷu	ḵpaiṉāṉciyar
ḵpail	ḵpiṭṭar
ḵpērm	ḵpelōcip
ḵpipilim	ḵpicceṭ
ḵpirī	ḵparṉiccar
ḵpērlēṭi	ḵpēṉkaḷ
ḵpōrṭu	ḵpaiyarmeṉ
ḵpipcu	ḵpuṭvēr
ḵpēṉci	ḵpiṉleṇṭ
ḵpārmaci	ḵpirāṉcu
āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu eḻuttukku nikarāka iv vāytattaik koṇṭuvarum pōkkiṉai viḷamparaṅ kaḷilum mikutiyākak kāṇkiṉṟōm. itaṉālvarum. varappōkum keṭutalkaḷai uṇarvār cilariṉum cilarē. tamiḻ tērntu teḷinta cemmoḻi eṉṟu aṟiyātārum, pukuntaṉa vantaṉa vellām vaḷarcci eṉṟu karutuvārum ivvāykkēṭṭiṉai uṇaramāṭṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl putiya coṟkaḷil ākkam taṭaippaṭuvatōṭu, vēṟṟolikaḷ ellām tamiḻukku mutaleḻuttukkaḷāki viṭum. ātikkattaiyum uṇara vēṇṭum. āytamum āṅkilattiṉ āṟāvatu eḻuttum oṉṟu eṉṟa mayakkam ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭāl, aḵṟiṇai, eḵku, aḵkutal, veḵkāmai, paḵṟoṭai, aḵtu, iḵtu, paḵṟuḻi, kaḵcu mutalāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ varuṅkālattil eṉṉa olippāṭu paṭumō?
ilakkiyam kaṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam iyampal eṉpatu naṉmarapāyiṉum, aṅṅaṉam ilakkaṇam iyampuvataṟkumuṉ etu  ilakkiyam eṉṟu pakuttaṟivup pārvai vēṇṭum. ippakuttaṟivaip peṟṟiruntavarkaḷ nam ilakkaṇac cāṉṟōrkaḷ. ataṉālaṉṟō nam toṉmoḻi iṉṟum ilaṅkumoḻiyāka niṟkiṉṟatu purikiṉṟatu.
kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅkaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇuṟat terintāynta ippacuntamiḻ ēṉai
maṇṇiṭaic cilavilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇiṭaip paṭakkiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō.

tolkāppiyam moḻimarapum
māṇikkavuraiyum muṟṟum.
